type ObjectMeta
    string logicalId
    string symbol
    string originTf

    int originTime
    int confirmationTime
    int lastUpdateTime
    int createdBar

    float authority
    float quality
    float confluence

    bool confirmed
    bool archived

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

    ObjectMeta meta

    SwingSide side
    SwingBasis basis
    SwingDegree degree
    SwingRelation relation

    float wickPrice
    float closePrice
    float price

    int pivotBar

    int strength

    bool liquidityEligible

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

f_normalizeTf(
     string tf
     ) =>

    string result =
         tf == "" ?
         timeframe.period :
         tf

    result

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

f_priceToTicks(
     float price
     ) =>

    int result = na

    if not na(price)

        result :=
             int(
                  math.round(
                       price / syminfo.mintick
                  )
             )

    result

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
f_swingSideToken(
     SwingSide side
     ) =>

    string result = "U"

    if side == SwingSide.High
        result := "H"

    else if side == SwingSide.Low
        result := "L"

    result

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

f_swingDegreeToken(
     SwingDegree degree
     ) =>

    string result = "UNK"

    if degree == SwingDegree.External
        result := "EXT"

    else if degree == SwingDegree.Internal
        result := "INT"

    else if degree == SwingDegree.Micro
        result := "MICRO"

    result

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

f_swingBasisToken(
     SwingBasis basis
     ) =>

    string result = "UNK"

    if basis == SwingBasis.Wick
        result := "WICK"

    else if basis == SwingBasis.Close
        result := "CLOSE"

    else if basis == SwingBasis.Combined
        result := "COMB"

    result

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

f_makeLogicalId(
     string objectType,
     string subtype,
     string originTf,
     int originTime,
     float price
     ) =>

    string normalizedTf =
         f_normalizeTf(originTf)

    int priceTicks =
         f_priceToTicks(price)

    string priceToken =
         na(priceTicks) ?
         "NA" :
         str.tostring(priceTicks)

    string timeToken =
         na(originTime) ?
         "NA" :
         str.tostring(originTime)

    string result =
         syminfo.tickerid +
         "|" +
         normalizedTf +
         "|" +
         objectType +
         "|" +
         subtype +
         "|" +
         timeToken +
         "|" +
         priceToken

    result

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
f_newMeta(
     string objectType,
     string subtype,
     string originTf,
     int originTime,
     float price
     ) =>

    string logicalId =
         f_makeLogicalId(
              objectType,
              subtype,
              originTf,
              originTime,
              price
         )

    ObjectMeta meta =
         ObjectMeta.new(
              logicalId,
              syminfo.tickerid,
              f_normalizeTf(originTf),
              originTime,
              na,
              originTime,
              bar_index,
              0.0,
              0.0,
              0.0,
              false,
              false
         )

    meta

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_newSwing(
     string originTf,
     SwingSide side,
     SwingBasis basis,
     SwingDegree degree,
     SwingRelation relation,
     float wickPrice,
     float closePrice,
     float primaryPrice,
     int pivotTime,
     int pivotBar,
     int strength,
     string parentSwingId,
     string previousPeerSwingId,
     bool liquidityEligible
     ) =>

    string subtype = f_swingSideToken(side) + "_" + f_swingDegreeToken(degree) + "_" + f_swingBasisToken(basis)

    ObjectMeta meta = f_newMeta(
         "SWING",
         subtype,
         originTf,
         pivotTime,
         primaryPrice
    )

    int confirmedAt = na(time_close) ? time : time_close

    meta.confirmationTime := confirmedAt
    meta.lastUpdateTime := confirmedAt
    meta.confirmed := true
    meta.archived := false

    SwingObject result = SwingObject.new(
         meta,
         side,
         basis,
         degree,
         relation,
         wickPrice,
         closePrice,
         primaryPrice,
         pivotTime,
         pivotBar,
         strength,
         parentSwingId,
         previousPeerSwingId,
         liquidityEligible
    )

    result
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

f_findSwingIndexById(
     array<SwingObject> storage,
     string logicalId
     ) =>

    int result = -1

    int storageSize =
         array.size(storage)

    if storageSize > 0

        for i = 0 to storageSize - 1

            SwingObject current =
                 array.get(
                      storage,
                      i
                 )

            if current.meta.logicalId == logicalId

                result := i

                break

    result

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
f_getLineTransparency(bool isMitigated) =>
    int base = transparencyLines
    int extra = isMitigated ? transparencyMitigatedExtra : 0
    int resolved = base + extra
    math.min(resolved, 100)

f_getBoxTransparency(bool isMitigated) =>
    int base = transparencyBoxes
    int extra = isMitigated ? transparencyMitigatedExtra : 0
    int resolved = base + extra
    math.min(resolved, 100)

f_getLabelTransparency(bool isMitigated) =>
    int base = transparencyLabels
    int extra = isMitigated ? transparencyMitigatedExtra : 0
    int resolved = base + extra
    math.min(resolved, 100)

         swingObjects,
         testSwing
    )

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Fast Swing logical-ID index
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    map.put(
         swingObjectById,
         testSwing.meta.logicalId,
         testSwing
    )

f_swingDegreeVisible(
     SwingDegree degree
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    if degree == SwingDegree.External

        result := showExternalSwing

    else if degree == SwingDegree.Internal

        result := showInternalSwing

    else if degree == SwingDegree.Micro

        result := showMicroSwing

    result

     group = grpLiquidityVisibility

f_liquidityDegreeVisible(
     SwingDegree degree
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    if not showLiquidityMarkers
        result := false

    else if degree == SwingDegree.External

        result := showExternalLiquidity

    else if degree == SwingDegree.Internal

        result := showInternalLiquidity

    else if degree == SwingDegree.Micro

        result := showMicroLiquidity

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_detectRawSwingHigh(
     int strength,
     string detectionMode
     ) =>

    float wickPivot = ta.pivothigh(high, strength, strength)
    float closePivot = ta.pivothigh(close, strength, strength)

    bool wickFound = not na(wickPivot)
    bool closeFound = not na(closePivot)

    bool found = false
    SwingBasis basis = SwingBasis.Wick

    float wickPrice = na
    float closePrice = na
    float primaryPrice = na

    int pivotTime = na
    int pivotBar = na

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // WICK MODE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    if detectionMode == "Wick"

        if wickFound
            found := true
            basis := SwingBasis.Wick
            wickPrice := high[strength]
            closePrice := close[strength]
            primaryPrice := wickPivot

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // CLOSE MODE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    else if detectionMode == "Close"

        if closeFound
            found := true
            basis := SwingBasis.Close
            wickPrice := high[strength]
            closePrice := close[strength]
            primaryPrice := closePivot

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // COMBINED MODE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    else

        // Both Wick and Close confirm a pivot on the same source bar.
        if wickFound and closeFound
            found := true
            basis := SwingBasis.Combined
            wickPrice := high[strength]
            closePrice := close[strength]
            primaryPrice := wickPivot

        // Wick-only pivot.
        else if wickFound
            found := true
            basis := SwingBasis.Wick
            wickPrice := high[strength]
            closePrice := close[strength]
            primaryPrice := wickPivot

        // Close-only pivot.
        else if closeFound
            found := true
            basis := SwingBasis.Close
            wickPrice := high[strength]
            closePrice := close[strength]
            primaryPrice := closePivot

    if found
        pivotTime := time[strength]
        pivotBar := bar_index - strength

    [found, basis, wickPrice, closePrice, primaryPrice, pivotTime, pivotBar]

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_detectRawSwingLow(
     int strength,
     string detectionMode
     ) =>

    float wickPivot = ta.pivotlow(low, strength, strength)
    float closePivot = ta.pivotlow(close, strength, strength)

    bool wickFound = not na(wickPivot)
    bool closeFound = not na(closePivot)

    bool found = false
    SwingBasis basis = SwingBasis.Wick

    float wickPrice = na
    float closePrice = na
    float primaryPrice = na

    int pivotTime = na
    int pivotBar = na

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // WICK MODE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    if detectionMode == "Wick"

        if wickFound
            found := true
            basis := SwingBasis.Wick
            wickPrice := low[strength]
            closePrice := close[strength]
            primaryPrice := wickPivot

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // CLOSE MODE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    else if detectionMode == "Close"

        if closeFound
            found := true
            basis := SwingBasis.Close
            wickPrice := low[strength]
            closePrice := close[strength]
            primaryPrice := closePivot

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // COMBINED MODE
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    else

        if wickFound and closeFound
            found := true
            basis := SwingBasis.Combined
            wickPrice := low[strength]
            closePrice := close[strength]
            primaryPrice := wickPivot

        else if wickFound
            found := true
            basis := SwingBasis.Wick
            wickPrice := low[strength]
            closePrice := close[strength]
            primaryPrice := wickPivot

        else if closeFound
            found := true
            basis := SwingBasis.Close
            wickPrice := low[strength]
            closePrice := close[strength]
            primaryPrice := closePivot

    if found
        pivotTime := time[strength]
        pivotBar := bar_index - strength

    [found, basis, wickPrice, closePrice, primaryPrice, pivotTime, pivotBar]

     array.new<label>()

f_renderDevSwing(
     SwingObject swingObject
     ) =>

    label result = na

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // VISIBILITY ONLY
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    bool degreeVisible = f_swingDegreeVisible(
         swingObject.degree
    )

    if swingDevShowRawMarkers and degreeVisible

        string markerText =
             f_swingDegreeToken(
                  swingObject.degree
             ) +
             "\n" +
             f_swingBasisToken(
                  swingObject.basis
             )

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // HIGH SWING
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        if swingObject.side == SwingSide.High

            result :=
                 label.new(
                      x = swingObject.pivotTime,
                      y = swingObject.price,
                      text = markerText,
                      xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                      style = label.style_label_down,
                      color = color.new(
                           color.gray,
                           80
                      ),
                      textcolor = chart.fg_color,
                      size = size.tiny
                 )

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // LOW SWING
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        else

            result :=
                 label.new(
                      x = swingObject.pivotTime,
                      y = swingObject.price,
                      text = markerText,
                      xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                      style = label.style_label_up,
                      color = color.new(
                           color.gray,
                           80
                      ),
                      textcolor = chart.fg_color,
                      size = size.tiny
                 )

    result

while array.size(swingObjects) > CORE_MAX_SWINGS

    SwingObject oldestSwing = array.get(
         swingObjects,
         0
    )

    string oldestSwingId = oldestSwing.meta.logicalId

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Remove oldest Swing from ordered analytical history
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    array.shift(
         swingObjects
    )

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Remove same Swing from fast logical-ID index
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    if oldestSwingId != "" and map.contains(
         swingObjectById,
         oldestSwingId
    )

        map.remove(
             swingObjectById,
             oldestSwingId
        )

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Keep incremental processing cursors synchronized with shifted history
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    swingHierarchyCursor := math.max(
         swingHierarchyCursor - 1,
         0
    )

    swingSequenceCursor := math.max(
         swingSequenceCursor - 1,
         0
    )

    swingFinalizationCursor := math.max(
         swingFinalizationCursor - 1,
         0
    )

    liquidityCreationCursor := math.max(
         liquidityCreationCursor - 1,
         0
    )

    equalLiquidityCursor := math.max(
         equalLiquidityCursor - 1,
         0
    )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(swingDevLabels) > swingDevMarkerLimit

    label oldLabel = array.shift(
         swingDevLabels
    )

    if not na(oldLabel)

        label.delete(
             oldLabel
        )

     display = display.none

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_swingRelationToken(SwingRelation relation) =>

    string result = "-"

    if relation == SwingRelation.HH
        result := "HH"

    else if relation == SwingRelation.HL
        result := "HL"

    else if relation == SwingRelation.LH
        result := "LH"

    else if relation == SwingRelation.LL
        result := "LL"

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_findPreviousPeerSwingIndex(
     array<SwingObject> storage,
     SwingObject currentSwing
     ) =>

    int result = -1
    int storageSize = array.size(storage)

    if storageSize > 0

        for offset = 0 to storageSize - 1

            int i = storageSize - 1 - offset

            SwingObject candidate = array.get(
                 storage,
                 i
            )

            bool differentObject = candidate.meta.logicalId != currentSwing.meta.logicalId
            bool alreadyConfirmed = candidate.meta.confirmed
            bool sameTf = candidate.meta.originTf == currentSwing.meta.originTf
            bool sameDegree = candidate.degree == currentSwing.degree
            bool sameSide = candidate.side == currentSwing.side
            bool olderPivot = candidate.pivotTime < currentSwing.pivotTime

            bool validPeer = differentObject and alreadyConfirmed and sameTf and sameDegree and sameSide and olderPivot

            if validPeer

                result := i
                break

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_classifySwingRelation(SwingSide side, float currentPrice, float previousPrice) =>

    SwingRelation result = SwingRelation.Unknown
    float comparisonTolerance = syminfo.mintick * 0.5

    if not na(currentPrice) and not na(previousPrice)

        float difference = currentPrice - previousPrice
        bool effectivelyEqual = math.abs(difference) <= comparisonTolerance

        if not effectivelyEqual

            if side == SwingSide.High

                if currentPrice > previousPrice
                    result := SwingRelation.HH

                else
                    result := SwingRelation.LH

            else

                if currentPrice > previousPrice
                    result := SwingRelation.HL

                else
                    result := SwingRelation.LL

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_findLatestHigherDegreeSwingIndex(
     array<SwingObject> storage,
     SwingObject currentSwing,
     SwingDegree targetDegree
     ) =>

    int result = -1
    int storageSize = array.size(storage)

    if storageSize > 0

        for offset = 0 to storageSize - 1

            int i = storageSize - 1 - offset

            SwingObject candidate = array.get(
                 storage,
                 i
            )

            bool differentObject = candidate.meta.logicalId != currentSwing.meta.logicalId
            bool alreadyConfirmed = candidate.meta.confirmed
            bool sameTf = candidate.meta.originTf == currentSwing.meta.originTf
            bool sameSide = candidate.side == currentSwing.side
            bool correctDegree = candidate.degree == targetDegree
            bool validTime = candidate.pivotTime <= currentSwing.pivotTime

            bool validParent = differentObject and alreadyConfirmed and sameTf and sameSide and correctDegree and validTime

            if validParent

                result := i
                break

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_findParentSwingIndex(
     array<SwingObject> storage,
     SwingObject currentSwing
     ) =>

    int result = -1

    if currentSwing.degree == SwingDegree.Internal

        result := f_findLatestHigherDegreeSwingIndex(
             storage,
             currentSwing,
             SwingDegree.External
        )

    else if currentSwing.degree == SwingDegree.Micro

        result := f_findLatestHigherDegreeSwingIndex(
             storage,
             currentSwing,
             SwingDegree.Internal
        )

        if result == -1

            result := f_findLatestHigherDegreeSwingIndex(
                 storage,
                 currentSwing,
                 SwingDegree.External
            )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getBaseSwingAuthority(SwingDegree degree, int strength) =>

    float baseAuthority = 0.0

    if degree == SwingDegree.External
        baseAuthority := 70.0

    else if degree == SwingDegree.Internal
        baseAuthority := 45.0

    else if degree == SwingDegree.Micro
        baseAuthority := 25.0

    float strengthBonus = math.min(float(strength) * 1.5, 20.0)
    float result = math.min(baseAuthority + strengthBonus, 100.0)

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getBaseSwingQuality(SwingBasis basis) =>

    float result = 60.0

    if basis == SwingBasis.Combined
        result := 85.0

    else if basis == SwingBasis.Wick
        result := 72.0

    else if basis == SwingBasis.Close
        result := 68.0

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

var array<label> swingHierarchyDevLabels = array.new<label>()

f_renderSwingHierarchyDev(SwingObject swingObject) =>

    label result = na

    if swingDevShowHierarchyLabels

        string relationText = f_swingRelationToken(swingObject.relation)
        string degreeText = f_swingDegreeToken(swingObject.degree)

        string markerText = degreeText + " | " + relationText

        if swingObject.side == SwingSide.High

            result := label.new(
                 x = swingObject.pivotTime,
                 y = swingObject.price,
                 text = markerText,
                 xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                 style = label.style_label_down,
                 color = color.new(color.gray, 78),
                 textcolor = chart.fg_color,
                 size = size.tiny
            )

        else

            result := label.new(
                 x = swingObject.pivotTime,
                 y = swingObject.price,
                 text = markerText,
                 xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                 style = label.style_label_up,
                 color = color.new(color.gray, 78),
                 textcolor = chart.fg_color,
                 size = size.tiny
            )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int swingHierarchyStorageSize = array.size(
     swingObjects
)

while swingHierarchyCursor < swingHierarchyStorageSize

    int i = swingHierarchyCursor

    SwingObject currentSwing = array.get(
         swingObjects,
         i
    )

    // ========================================================================
    // PREVIOUS PEER
    // ========================================================================

    int previousPeerIndex = f_findPreviousPeerSwingIndex(
         swingObjects,
         currentSwing
    )

    if previousPeerIndex >= 0

        SwingObject previousPeer = array.get(
             swingObjects,
             previousPeerIndex
        )

        currentSwing.previousPeerSwingId := previousPeer.meta.logicalId

        currentSwing.relation := f_classifySwingRelation(
             currentSwing.side,
             currentSwing.price,
             previousPeer.price
        )

    else

        currentSwing.previousPeerSwingId := ""
        currentSwing.relation := SwingRelation.Unknown

    // ========================================================================
    // INITIAL PARENT HIERARCHY
    // ========================================================================

    int parentIndex = f_findParentSwingIndex(
         swingObjects,
         currentSwing
    )

    if parentIndex >= 0

        SwingObject parentSwing = array.get(
             swingObjects,
             parentIndex
        )

        currentSwing.parentSwingId := parentSwing.meta.logicalId

    else

        currentSwing.parentSwingId := ""

    // ========================================================================
    // BASE AUTHORITY / QUALITY
    // ========================================================================

    ObjectMeta currentMeta = currentSwing.meta

    currentMeta.authority := f_getBaseSwingAuthority(
         currentSwing.degree,
         currentSwing.strength
    )

    currentMeta.quality := f_getBaseSwingQuality(
         currentSwing.basis
    )

    currentMeta.confluence := 0.0
    currentMeta.lastUpdateTime := time_close

    currentSwing.meta := currentMeta

    // ========================================================================
    // DATABASE WRITE
    // ========================================================================

    array.set(
         swingObjects,
         i,
         currentSwing
    )

    // ========================================================================
    // ========================================================================

    label hierarchyLabel = na

    if f_swingDegreeVisible(
        currentSwing.degree
    )

        hierarchyLabel := f_renderSwingHierarchyDev(
            currentSwing
        )

    if not na(hierarchyLabel)

        array.push(
            swingHierarchyDevLabels,
            hierarchyLabel
        )

    // ========================================================================
    // ADVANCE PROCESSING CURSOR
    // ========================================================================

    swingHierarchyCursor += 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(swingHierarchyDevLabels) > swingDevHierarchyLabelLimit

    label oldHierarchyLabel = array.shift(swingHierarchyDevLabels)

    if not na(oldHierarchyLabel)
        label.delete(oldHierarchyLabel)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int confirmedSwingCount = 0
int externalSwingCount = 0
int internalSwingCount = 0
int microSwingCount = 0

int hhCount = 0
int hlCount = 0
int lhCount = 0
int llCount = 0

if barstate.islast and coreDebug

    int hierarchyCountSize = array.size(swingObjects)

    if hierarchyCountSize > 0

        for i = 0 to hierarchyCountSize - 1

            SwingObject debugSwing = array.get(
                 swingObjects,
                 i
            )

            if debugSwing.meta.confirmed

                confirmedSwingCount += 1

                if debugSwing.degree == SwingDegree.External
                    externalSwingCount += 1

                else if debugSwing.degree == SwingDegree.Internal
                    internalSwingCount += 1

                else if debugSwing.degree == SwingDegree.Micro
                    microSwingCount += 1

                if debugSwing.relation == SwingRelation.HH
                    hhCount += 1

                else if debugSwing.relation == SwingRelation.HL
                    hlCount += 1

                else if debugSwing.relation == SwingRelation.LH
                    lhCount += 1

                else if debugSwing.relation == SwingRelation.LL
                    llCount += 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(confirmedSwingCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG CONFIRMED SWINGS",
     display = display.none
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(externalSwingCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG EXTERNAL SWINGS",
     display = display.none
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(internalSwingCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG INTERNAL SWINGS",
     display = display.none
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(microSwingCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG MICRO SWINGS",
     display = display.none
)

swingDevShowStructuralSequence = input.bool(
     false,
     "Show Structural Sequence Labels",
     group = grpSwingHierarchyDev
)

     80,
     "Maximum Structural Sequence Labels",
     minval = 10,
     maxval = 250,
     group = grpSwingHierarchyDev
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_findLatestSequenceNodeIndex(
     array<SwingSequenceNode> storage,
     string originTf,
     SwingDegree degree
     ) =>

    int result = -1
    int storageSize = array.size(storage)

    if storageSize > 0

        for offset = 0 to storageSize - 1

            int i = storageSize - 1 - offset

            SwingSequenceNode node = array.get(
                 storage,
                 i
            )

            bool sameTf = node.originTf == originTf
            bool sameDegree = node.degree == degree

            if sameTf and sameDegree

                result := i
                break

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_findPreviousSameSideSequenceIndex(
     array<SwingSequenceNode> storage,
     SwingObject currentSwing,
     int excludeIndex
     ) =>

    int result = -1
    int storageSize = array.size(storage)

    if storageSize > 0

        for offset = 0 to storageSize - 1

            int i = storageSize - 1 - offset

            if i != excludeIndex

                SwingSequenceNode node = array.get(
                     storage,
                     i
                )

                bool sameTf = node.originTf == currentSwing.meta.originTf
                bool sameDegree = node.degree == currentSwing.degree
                bool sameSide = node.side == currentSwing.side
                bool older = node.pivotTime < currentSwing.pivotTime

                bool validNode = sameTf and sameDegree and sameSide and older

                if validNode

                    result := i
                    break

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_classifyStructuralRelation(
     SwingSide side,
     float currentPrice,
     float previousPrice
     ) =>

    SwingRelation result = SwingRelation.Unknown

    float tolerance = syminfo.mintick * 0.5

    if not na(currentPrice) and not na(previousPrice)

        float difference = currentPrice - previousPrice
        bool equalEnough = math.abs(difference) <= tolerance

        if not equalEnough

            if side == SwingSide.High

                if currentPrice > previousPrice
                    result := SwingRelation.HH

                else
                    result := SwingRelation.LH

            else

                if currentPrice > previousPrice
                    result := SwingRelation.HL

                else
                    result := SwingRelation.LL

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_isMoreExtremeSwing(
     SwingSide side,
     float currentPrice,
     float referencePrice
     ) =>

    bool result = false
    float tolerance = syminfo.mintick * 0.5

    if not na(currentPrice) and not na(referencePrice)

        if side == SwingSide.High
            result := currentPrice > referencePrice + tolerance

        else
            result := currentPrice < referencePrice - tolerance

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_newSwingSequenceNode(
     SwingObject swingObject,
     SwingRelation relation,
     string previousSequenceSwingId,
     string previousSameSideSwingId
     ) =>

    SwingSequenceNode result = SwingSequenceNode.new(
         swingObject.meta.logicalId,
         swingObject.meta.originTf,
         swingObject.degree,
         swingObject.side,
         relation,
         swingObject.price,
         swingObject.pivotTime,
         previousSequenceSwingId,
         previousSameSideSwingId
    )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_reconcileSwingChildren(
     array<SwingObject> storage,
     SwingObject newParent
     ) =>

    int storageSize = array.size(storage)

    if storageSize > 0 and newParent.meta.confirmed

        for i = 0 to storageSize - 1

            SwingObject child = array.get(
                 storage,
                 i
            )

            bool differentObject = child.meta.logicalId != newParent.meta.logicalId
            bool confirmedChild = child.meta.confirmed
            bool sameTf = child.meta.originTf == newParent.meta.originTf
            bool sameSide = child.side == newParent.side

            // Parent pivot must be at or before the child's pivot.
            bool validPivotRelationship = child.pivotTime >= newParent.pivotTime

            bool commonValid = differentObject and confirmedChild and sameTf and sameSide and validPivotRelationship

            // =================================================================
            // =================================================================

            if commonValid and newParent.degree == SwingDegree.External and child.degree == SwingDegree.Internal

                child.parentSwingId := newParent.meta.logicalId
                child.meta.lastUpdateTime := time_close

                array.set(
                     storage,
                     i,
                     child
                )

            // =================================================================
            // =================================================================

            if commonValid and newParent.degree == SwingDegree.External and child.degree == SwingDegree.Micro

                if child.parentSwingId == ""

                    child.parentSwingId := newParent.meta.logicalId
                    child.meta.lastUpdateTime := time_close

                    array.set(
                         storage,
                         i,
                         child
                    )

            // =================================================================
            // =================================================================

            if commonValid and newParent.degree == SwingDegree.Internal and child.degree == SwingDegree.Micro

                child.parentSwingId := newParent.meta.logicalId
                child.meta.lastUpdateTime := time_close

                array.set(
                     storage,
                     i,
                     child
                )

    true

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

var array<label> swingSequenceDevLabels = array.new<label>()

var array<string> swingSequenceDevLabelIds = array.new<string>()

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_findStringIndex(
     array<string> storage,
     string target
     ) =>

    int result = -1
    int storageSize = array.size(storage)

    if storageSize > 0

        for i = 0 to storageSize - 1

            if array.get(storage, i) == target
                result := i
                break

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_removeStructuralDevLabel(
     array<label> labelStorage,
     array<string> idStorage,
     string swingId
     ) =>

    bool removed = false

    int targetIndex = f_findStringIndex(
         idStorage,
         swingId
    )

    if targetIndex >= 0

        label oldLabel = array.get(
             labelStorage,
             targetIndex
        )

        if not na(oldLabel)
            label.delete(oldLabel)

        array.remove(
             labelStorage,
             targetIndex
        )

        array.remove(
             idStorage,
             targetIndex
        )

        removed := true

    removed

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_renderStructuralSequenceDev(
     SwingObject swingObject,
     SwingRelation structuralRelation
     ) =>

    label result = na

    if swingDevShowStructuralSequence

        string markerText = f_swingDegreeToken(swingObject.degree) + " | " + f_swingRelationToken(structuralRelation) + " | SEQ"

        if swingObject.side == SwingSide.High

            result := label.new(
                 x = swingObject.pivotTime,
                 y = swingObject.price * 1.004,
                 text = markerText,
                 xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                 style = label.style_label_down,
                 color = color.new(color.gray, 78),
                 textcolor = chart.fg_color,
                 size = size.tiny
            )

        else

            result := label.new(
                 x = swingObject.pivotTime,
                 y = swingObject.price * 0.996,
                 text = markerText,
                 xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                 style = label.style_label_up,
                 color = color.new(color.gray, 78),
                 textcolor = chart.fg_color,
                 size = size.tiny
            )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int sequenceInputSize = array.size(swingObjects)

while swingSequenceCursor < sequenceInputSize

    int i = swingSequenceCursor

    SwingObject currentSwing = array.get(
         swingObjects,
         i
    )

    if currentSwing.meta.confirmed

        bool acceptedIntoSequence = false
        bool replacedExistingNode = false

        string replacedSwingId = ""

        SwingRelation structuralRelation = SwingRelation.Unknown

        string previousSequenceSwingId = ""
        string previousSameSideSwingId = ""

        // ====================================================================
        // DELAYED PARENT RECONCILIATION
        // ====================================================================

        if currentSwing.degree == SwingDegree.External or currentSwing.degree == SwingDegree.Internal

            f_reconcileSwingChildren(
                 swingObjects,
                 currentSwing
            )

        // ====================================================================
        // FIND CURRENT STRUCTURAL SEQUENCE
        // ====================================================================

        int latestNodeIndex = f_findLatestSequenceNodeIndex(
             swingSequenceNodes,
             currentSwing.meta.originTf,
             currentSwing.degree
        )

        // ====================================================================
        // FIRST STRUCTURAL NODE
        // ====================================================================

        if latestNodeIndex < 0

            SwingSequenceNode firstNode = f_newSwingSequenceNode(
                 currentSwing,
                 SwingRelation.Unknown,
                 "",
                 ""
            )

            array.push(
                 swingSequenceNodes,
                 firstNode
            )

            acceptedIntoSequence := true

        // ====================================================================
        // EXISTING STRUCTURAL SEQUENCE
        // ====================================================================

        else

            SwingSequenceNode latestNode = array.get(
                 swingSequenceNodes,
                 latestNodeIndex
            )

            // ================================================================
            // OPPOSITE SIDE
            // ================================================================

            if latestNode.side != currentSwing.side

                int previousSameSideIndex = f_findPreviousSameSideSequenceIndex(
                     swingSequenceNodes,
                     currentSwing,
                     -1
                )

                if previousSameSideIndex >= 0

                    SwingSequenceNode previousSameSideNode = array.get(
                         swingSequenceNodes,
                         previousSameSideIndex
                    )

                    previousSameSideSwingId := previousSameSideNode.swingId

                    structuralRelation := f_classifyStructuralRelation(
                         currentSwing.side,
                         currentSwing.price,
                         previousSameSideNode.price
                    )

                previousSequenceSwingId := latestNode.swingId

                SwingSequenceNode newNode = f_newSwingSequenceNode(
                     currentSwing,
                     structuralRelation,
                     previousSequenceSwingId,
                     previousSameSideSwingId
                )

                array.push(
                     swingSequenceNodes,
                     newNode
                )

                acceptedIntoSequence := true

            // ================================================================
            // SAME SIDE
            // ================================================================

            else

                bool moreExtreme = f_isMoreExtremeSwing(
                     currentSwing.side,
                     currentSwing.price,
                     latestNode.price
                )

                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // ------------------------------------------------------------

                if moreExtreme

                    int previousSameSideIndex = f_findPreviousSameSideSequenceIndex(
                         swingSequenceNodes,
                         currentSwing,
                         latestNodeIndex
                    )

                    if previousSameSideIndex >= 0

                        SwingSequenceNode previousSameSideNode = array.get(
                             swingSequenceNodes,
                             previousSameSideIndex
                        )

                        previousSameSideSwingId := previousSameSideNode.swingId

                        structuralRelation := f_classifyStructuralRelation(
                             currentSwing.side,
                             currentSwing.price,
                             previousSameSideNode.price
                        )

                    previousSequenceSwingId := latestNode.previousSequenceSwingId

                    replacedSwingId := latestNode.swingId

                    SwingSequenceNode replacementNode = f_newSwingSequenceNode(
                         currentSwing,
                         structuralRelation,
                         previousSequenceSwingId,
                         previousSameSideSwingId
                    )

                    array.set(
                         swingSequenceNodes,
                         latestNodeIndex,
                         replacementNode
                    )

                    acceptedIntoSequence := true
                    replacedExistingNode := true

        // ====================================================================
        // WRITE STRUCTURAL CLASSIFICATION BACK TO SWING
        // ====================================================================

        if acceptedIntoSequence

            currentSwing.relation := structuralRelation
            currentSwing.meta.lastUpdateTime := time_close

            array.set(
                 swingObjects,
                 i,
                 currentSwing
            )

        // ========================================================================
        // ========================================================================

        int publishedNodeIndex = f_findLatestSequenceNodeIndex(
            swingSequenceNodes,
            currentSwing.meta.originTf,
            currentSwing.degree
        )

        if publishedNodeIndex >= 0

            SwingSequenceNode publishedNode = array.get(
                swingSequenceNodes,
                publishedNodeIndex
            )

            array.push(
                structureSwingQueue,
                publishedNode
            )
            // ================================================================
            // DEV DRAWING REPLACEMENT
            // ================================================================

            if replacedExistingNode and replacedSwingId != ""

                f_removeStructuralDevLabel(
                     swingSequenceDevLabels,
                     swingSequenceDevLabelIds,
                     replacedSwingId
                )

            // ================================================================
            // DEV RENDERER
            // ================================================================

            label structuralLabel = f_renderStructuralSequenceDev(
                 currentSwing,
                 structuralRelation
            )

            if not na(structuralLabel)

                array.push(
                     swingSequenceDevLabels,
                     structuralLabel
                )

                array.push(
                     swingSequenceDevLabelIds,
                     currentSwing.meta.logicalId
                )

    // ========================================================================
    // MOVE TO NEXT RAW SWING
    // ========================================================================

    swingSequenceCursor += 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(swingSequenceNodes) > CORE_MAX_SWINGS
    array.shift(swingSequenceNodes)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(swingSequenceDevLabels) > swingDevStructuralLabelLimit

    label oldestStructuralLabel = array.shift(
         swingSequenceDevLabels
    )

    array.shift(
         swingSequenceDevLabelIds
    )

    if not na(oldestStructuralLabel)
        label.delete(oldestStructuralLabel)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int totalStructuralNodes = array.size(
     swingSequenceNodes
)

int externalStructuralNodes = 0
int internalStructuralNodes = 0
int microStructuralNodes = 0

if barstate.islast and coreDebug and totalStructuralNodes > 0

    for i = 0 to totalStructuralNodes - 1

        SwingSequenceNode node = array.get(
             swingSequenceNodes,
             i
        )

        if node.degree == SwingDegree.External
            externalStructuralNodes += 1

        else if node.degree == SwingDegree.Internal
            internalStructuralNodes += 1

        else if node.degree == SwingDegree.Micro
            microStructuralNodes += 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getFinalSwingQuality(
     SwingBasis basis,
     int strength,
     SwingRelation relation
     ) =>

    float basisScore = 0.0

    if basis == SwingBasis.Combined
        basisScore := 70.0

    else if basis == SwingBasis.Wick
        basisScore := 64.0

    else if basis == SwingBasis.Close
        basisScore := 60.0

    float strengthBonus = math.min(
         float(strength) * 1.5,
         20.0
    )

    float relationBonus = 0.0

    if relation != SwingRelation.Unknown
        relationBonus := 5.0

    float result = math.min(
         basisScore + strengthBonus + relationBonus,
         100.0
    )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getFinalLocalSwingAuthority(
     SwingDegree degree,
     int strength
     ) =>

    float degreeScore = 0.0

    if degree == SwingDegree.External
        degreeScore := 70.0

    else if degree == SwingDegree.Internal
        degreeScore := 45.0

    else if degree == SwingDegree.Micro
        degreeScore := 25.0

    float strengthBonus = math.min(
         float(strength) * 1.5,
         20.0
    )

    float result = math.min(
         degreeScore + strengthBonus,
         100.0
    )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getBaseLiquidityEligibility(
     SwingObject swingObject
     ) =>

    bool validPrice = not na(swingObject.price)
    bool validTime = not na(swingObject.pivotTime)
    bool validStrength = swingObject.strength > 0

    bool result = swingObject.meta.confirmed and validPrice and validTime and validStrength

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_finalizeSwingObject(
     SwingObject swingObject
     ) =>

    swingObject.meta.authority := f_getFinalLocalSwingAuthority(
         swingObject.degree,
         swingObject.strength
    )

    swingObject.meta.quality := f_getFinalSwingQuality(
         swingObject.basis,
         swingObject.strength,
         swingObject.relation
    )

    // Confluence intentionally starts at zero.
    //      DOL

    swingObject.meta.confluence := 0.0

    swingObject.liquidityEligible := f_getBaseLiquidityEligibility(
         swingObject
    )

    swingObject.meta.lastUpdateTime := time_close

    swingObject

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int swingFinalizationStorageSize = array.size(
     swingObjects
)

while swingFinalizationCursor < swingFinalizationStorageSize

    int i = swingFinalizationCursor

    SwingObject currentSwing = array.get(
         swingObjects,
         i
    )

    if currentSwing.meta.confirmed

        currentSwing := f_finalizeSwingObject(
             currentSwing
        )

        array.set(
             swingObjects,
             i,
             currentSwing
        )

    swingFinalizationCursor += 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int structuralSequenceViolations = 0

if barstate.islast and coreDebug

    int externalLastSide = 0
    int internalLastSide = 0
    int microLastSide = 0

    int sequenceValidationSize = array.size(
         swingSequenceNodes
    )

    if sequenceValidationSize > 0

        for i = 0 to sequenceValidationSize - 1

            SwingSequenceNode node = array.get(
                 swingSequenceNodes,
                 i
            )

            int currentSide = 0

            if node.side == SwingSide.High
                currentSide := 1

            else
                currentSide := -1

            // ================================================================
            // EXTERNAL
            // ================================================================

            if node.degree == SwingDegree.External

                if externalLastSide != 0 and externalLastSide == currentSide
                    structuralSequenceViolations += 1

                externalLastSide := currentSide

            // ================================================================
            // INTERNAL
            // ================================================================

            else if node.degree == SwingDegree.Internal

                if internalLastSide != 0 and internalLastSide == currentSide
                    structuralSequenceViolations += 1

                internalLastSide := currentSide

            // ================================================================
            // MICRO
            // ================================================================

            else if node.degree == SwingDegree.Micro

                if microLastSide != 0 and microLastSide == currentSide
                    structuralSequenceViolations += 1

                microLastSide := currentSide

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int finalSwingCount = array.size(
     swingObjects
)

int finalStructuralSwingCount = array.size(
     swingSequenceNodes
)

int liquidityEligibleSwingCount = 0

if barstate.islast and coreDebug

    if finalSwingCount > 0

        for i = 0 to finalSwingCount - 1

            SwingObject debugSwing = array.get(
                 swingObjects,
                 i
            )

            if debugSwing.liquidityEligible
                liquidityEligibleSwingCount += 1

     display = display.none

     display = display.none

f_liquidityKindToken(
     LiquidityKind kind
     ) =>

    string result = "LIQ"

    if kind == LiquidityKind.BSL
        result := "BSL"

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.SSL
        result := "SSL"

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.EQH
        result := "EQH"

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.EQL
        result := "EQL"

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.PDH
        result := "PDH"

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.PDL
        result := "PDL"

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.PWH
        result := "PWH"

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.PWL
        result := "PWL"

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.SessionHigh
        result := "SESSION_H"

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.SessionLow
        result := "SESSION_L"

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.CustomHigh
        result := "CUSTOM_H"

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.CustomLow
        result := "CUSTOM_L"

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getBaseLiquidityKind(
     SwingObject swingObject
     ) =>

    LiquidityKind result = LiquidityKind.BSL

    if swingObject.side == SwingSide.High
        result := LiquidityKind.BSL

    else
        result := LiquidityKind.SSL

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getBaseLiquiditySubtype(
     SwingObject swingObject,
     LiquidityKind kind
     ) =>

    string result = f_liquidityKindToken(kind) + "_" + f_swingDegreeToken(swingObject.degree) + "_" + f_swingBasisToken(swingObject.basis)

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_newBaseLiquidityObject(
     SwingObject swingObject
     ) =>

    LiquidityKind kind = f_getBaseLiquidityKind(
         swingObject
    )

    string subtype = f_getBaseLiquiditySubtype(
         swingObject,
         kind
    )

    ObjectMeta meta = f_newMeta(
         "LIQ",
         subtype,
         swingObject.meta.originTf,
         swingObject.pivotTime,
         swingObject.price
    )

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Liquidity exists because the source Swing is confirmed.
    // Keep confirmation metadata aligned with that Swing.
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    meta.confirmationTime := swingObject.meta.confirmationTime
    meta.lastUpdateTime := swingObject.meta.lastUpdateTime
    meta.authority := swingObject.meta.authority
    meta.quality := swingObject.meta.quality
    meta.confluence := 0.0
    meta.confirmed := true
    meta.archived := false

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    array<string> memberIds = array.new<string>()

    array.push(
         memberIds,
         swingObject.meta.logicalId
    )

    int initialStateTime = swingObject.meta.confirmationTime

    if na(initialStateTime)
        initialStateTime := time_close

    LiquidityObject result = LiquidityObject.new(
         meta,
         kind,
         LiquidityOrigin.Swing,
         swingObject.basis,
         swingObject.degree,
         swingObject.price,
         swingObject.price,
         swingObject.price,
         swingObject.meta.logicalId,
         memberIds,
         1,
         LiquidityLifecycle.Fresh,
         0,
         na,
         initialStateTime,
         "",
         0.0,
         false
    )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================
f_findLiquidityIndexById(
     array<LiquidityObject> storage,
     string logicalId
     ) =>

    int result = -1
    int storageSize = array.size(storage)

    if storageSize > 0

        for offset = 0 to storageSize - 1

            int i = storageSize - 1 - offset

            LiquidityObject candidate = array.get(
                 storage,
                 i
            )

            if candidate.meta.logicalId == logicalId
                result := i
                break

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_unregisterActiveLiquidityById(
     string logicalId
     ) =>

    bool removed = false

    if logicalId != "" and map.contains(
         activeLiquidityPositionById,
         logicalId
    )

        int activePosition = map.get(
             activeLiquidityPositionById,
             logicalId
        )

        int activeSize = array.size(
             activeLiquidityObjects
        )

        int lastPosition = activeSize - 1

        if activePosition >= 0 and activePosition <= lastPosition

            // ----------------------------------------------------------------
            // Swap last active Object into removed position.
            // ----------------------------------------------------------------

            if activePosition != lastPosition

                LiquidityObject movedLiquidity = array.get(
                     activeLiquidityObjects,
                     lastPosition
                )

                array.set(
                     activeLiquidityObjects,
                     activePosition,
                     movedLiquidity
                )

                map.put(
                     activeLiquidityPositionById,
                     movedLiquidity.meta.logicalId,
                     activePosition
                )

            // ----------------------------------------------------------------
            // Remove last element.
            // ----------------------------------------------------------------

            array.pop(
                 activeLiquidityObjects
            )

        map.remove(
             activeLiquidityPositionById,
             logicalId
        )

        removed := true

    removed

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_registerOrRefreshActiveLiquidity(
     LiquidityObject liquidityObject
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    string logicalId = liquidityObject.meta.logicalId

    bool terminalState = false

    if liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.ValidatedSweep
        terminalState := true

    else if liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond
        terminalState := true

    else if liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.Consumed
        terminalState := true

    else if liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.Invalid
        terminalState := true

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Terminal Object must not remain in active registry.
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    if terminalState

        f_unregisterActiveLiquidityById(
             logicalId
        )

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Active Object.
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    else if logicalId != ""

        if map.contains(
             activeLiquidityPositionById,
             logicalId
        )

            int existingPosition = map.get(
                 activeLiquidityPositionById,
                 logicalId
            )

            if existingPosition >= 0 and existingPosition < array.size(activeLiquidityObjects)

                // EQH/EQL may have been rebuilt as a new UDT reference.
                // Refresh the active reference.

                array.set(
                     activeLiquidityObjects,
                     existingPosition,
                     liquidityObject
                )

            else

                // Defensive repair of an invalid map position.

                map.remove(
                     activeLiquidityPositionById,
                     logicalId
                )

                array.push(
                     activeLiquidityObjects,
                     liquidityObject
                )

                int newPosition = array.size(activeLiquidityObjects) - 1

                map.put(
                     activeLiquidityPositionById,
                     logicalId,
                     newPosition
                )

        else

            array.push(
                 activeLiquidityObjects,
                 liquidityObject
            )

            int newPosition = array.size(activeLiquidityObjects) - 1

            map.put(
                 activeLiquidityPositionById,
                 logicalId,
                 newPosition
            )

        result := true

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_shiftOldestLiquidityObject() =>

    bool shifted = false

    if array.size(liquidityObjects) > 0

        LiquidityObject removedLiquidity = array.shift(
             liquidityObjects
        )

        f_unregisterActiveLiquidityById(
             removedLiquidity.meta.logicalId
        )

        shifted := true

    shifted

var array<label> liquidityDevLabels = array.new<label>()
var array<line> liquidityDevLines = array.new<line>()

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_clearLiquidityDevDrawings() =>

    while array.size(liquidityDevLabels) > 0

        label oldLabel = array.pop(
             liquidityDevLabels
        )

        if not na(oldLabel)
            label.delete(oldLabel)

    while array.size(liquidityDevLines) > 0

        line oldLine = array.pop(
             liquidityDevLines
        )

        if not na(oldLine)
            line.delete(oldLine)

    true

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int liquidityInputSize = array.size(
     swingObjects
)

while liquidityCreationCursor < liquidityInputSize

    int i = liquidityCreationCursor

    SwingObject sourceSwing = array.get(
         swingObjects,
         i
    )

    bool createLiquidity = sourceSwing.meta.confirmed and sourceSwing.liquidityEligible

    if createLiquidity

        LiquidityObject newLiquidity = f_newBaseLiquidityObject(
             sourceSwing
        )

        array.push(
             liquidityObjects,
             newLiquidity
        )

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Fresh Liquidity requires Lifecycle monitoring.
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        f_registerOrRefreshActiveLiquidity(
             newLiquidity
        )

    liquidityCreationCursor += 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(liquidityObjects) > CORE_MAX_LIQUIDITY

    f_shiftOldestLiquidityObject()

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if barstate.islast

    f_clearLiquidityDevDrawings()

    if liquidityDevShowBaseLevels

        int totalLiquidityObjects = array.size(
             liquidityObjects
        )

        int liquidityIndex = totalLiquidityObjects - 1
        int baseLevelsDrawn = 0

        while liquidityIndex >= 0 and baseLevelsDrawn < liquidityDevMarkerLimit

            LiquidityObject liquidityObject = array.get(
                 liquidityObjects,
                 liquidityIndex
            )

            bool isBaseLiquidity = liquidityObject.kind == LiquidityKind.BSL or liquidityObject.kind == LiquidityKind.SSL

            bool isConsumed = liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.Consumed or liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.Invalid

            if isBaseLiquidity and not isConsumed and f_liquidityDegreeVisible(liquidityObject.degree)

                float levelPrice = liquidityObject.price

                int levelStartTime = liquidityObject.meta.originTime

                if na(levelStartTime)
                    levelStartTime := time

                string kindText = f_liquidityKindToken(
                     liquidityObject.kind
                )

                string degreeText = f_swingDegreeToken(
                     liquidityObject.degree
                )

                string basisText = f_swingBasisToken(
                     liquidityObject.basis
                )

                string markerText = kindText + " | " + degreeText + " | " + basisText

                color levelColor = liquidityObject.kind == LiquidityKind.BSL
                     ? color.red
                     : color.lime

                bool showLine  = liquidityDevDisplayMode != "Label Only"
                bool showLabel = liquidityDevDisplayMode != "Line Only"

                if not na(levelPrice)

                    if showLine
                        int x2Val = time

                        if liquidityExtendMode == "Fixed Bars After Current"
                            x2Val := levelStartTime + liquidityFixedExtendBars * timeframe.multiplier * 1000
                        else if liquidityExtendMode == "To Mitigation Point" and isConsumed
                            x2Val := liquidityObject.lastStateChangeTime
                        else if liquidityExtendMode == "Fixed Bars After Mitigation" and isConsumed
                            x2Val := liquidityObject.lastStateChangeTime + liquidityFixedExtendBars * timeframe.multiplier * 1000

                        line liquidityLine = line.new(
                             x1 = levelStartTime,
                             y1 = levelPrice,
                             x2 = x2Val,
                             y2 = levelPrice,
                             xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                             extend = extend.none,
                             color = color.new(levelColor, f_getLineTransparency(false)),
                             width = 2,
                             style = line.style_dotted
                        )

                        array.push(liquidityDevLines, liquidityLine)

                    if showLabel
                        label liquidityLabel = label.new(
                             x = time,
                             y = levelPrice,
                             text = markerText,
                             xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                             yloc = yloc.price,
                             style = label.style_label_left,
                             color = color.new(color.black, 100),
                             textcolor = color.new(levelColor, f_getLabelTransparency(false)),
                             size = size.tiny
                        )

                        array.push(liquidityDevLabels, liquidityLabel)

                    baseLevelsDrawn += 1

            liquidityIndex -= 1

// ============================================================================

     "ATR",
     "Tolerance Mode",
     options = ["Ticks", "ATR", "Percentage", "Hybrid"],

     2,
     "Tolerance Ticks",
     minval = 0,
     group = grpEqualLiquidity

     14,
     "ATR Length",
     minval = 1,
     group = grpEqualLiquidity
)

     0.05,
     "ATR Multiplier",
     minval = 0.0,
     step = 0.01,
     group = grpEqualLiquidity
)

     0.05,
     "Tolerance Percentage",
     minval = 0.0,
     step = 0.01,
     group = grpEqualLiquidity
)

     eqToleranceATRLength
