// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getEqualLiquidityTolerance(
     float referencePrice,
     float atrValue
     ) =>

    float tickTolerance = syminfo.mintick * float(eqToleranceTicks)

    float atrTolerance = 0.0

    if not na(atrValue)
        atrTolerance := atrValue * eqToleranceATRMultiplier

    float percentageTolerance = math.abs(referencePrice) * eqTolerancePercent / 100.0

    float result = tickTolerance

    if eqToleranceMode == "Ticks"

        result := tickTolerance

    else if eqToleranceMode == "ATR"

        if atrTolerance > 0.0
            result := atrTolerance
        else
            result := tickTolerance

    else if eqToleranceMode == "Percentage"

        if percentageTolerance > 0.0
            result := percentageTolerance
        else
            result := tickTolerance

    else

        float adaptiveTolerance = 0.0

        if atrTolerance > 0.0 and percentageTolerance > 0.0
            adaptiveTolerance := math.min(
                 atrTolerance,
                 percentageTolerance
            )

        else if atrTolerance > 0.0
            adaptiveTolerance := atrTolerance

        else
            adaptiveTolerance := percentageTolerance

        result := math.max(
             tickTolerance,
             adaptiveTolerance
        )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getEqualLiquidityKind(
     SwingSide side
     ) =>

    LiquidityKind result = LiquidityKind.EQH

    if side == SwingSide.High
        result := LiquidityKind.EQH

    else
        result := LiquidityKind.EQL

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getEqualLiquiditySubtype(
     LiquidityKind kind,
     SwingDegree degree,
     SwingBasis basis
     ) =>

    string result = f_liquidityKindToken(kind) + "_" + f_swingDegreeToken(degree) + "_" + f_swingBasisToken(basis) + "_CLUSTER"

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_stringArrayContains(
     array<string> storage,
     string target
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    int storageSize = array.size(
         storage
    )

    if storageSize > 0

        for i = 0 to storageSize - 1

            if array.get(storage, i) == target

                result := true

                break

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getEqualClusterAuthority(
     float maxMemberAuthority,
     int memberCount
     ) =>

    float memberBonus = math.min(
         float(math.max(memberCount - 1, 0)) * 3.0,
         12.0
    )

    float result = math.min(
         maxMemberAuthority + memberBonus,
         100.0
    )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getEqualClusterQuality(
     int memberCount,
     int firstPivotBar,
     int lastPivotBar,
     float maxSpread,
     float tolerance,
     int combinedSourceCount
     ) =>

    float baseScore = 45.0

    float memberBonus = math.min(
         float(math.max(memberCount - 1, 0)) * 7.0,
         21.0
    )

    int separationBars = math.max(
         lastPivotBar - firstPivotBar,
         0
    )

    float separationBonus = math.min(
         float(separationBars) * 0.5,
         14.0
    )

    float compactnessBonus = 0.0

    if tolerance > 0.0

        float normalizedSpread = math.min(
             maxSpread / (tolerance * 2.0),
             1.0
        )

        compactnessBonus := (
             1.0 - normalizedSpread
        ) * 15.0

    else if maxSpread == 0.0

        compactnessBonus := 15.0

    float combinedBonus = math.min(
         float(combinedSourceCount) * 2.5,
         5.0
    )

    float result = math.min(
         baseScore + memberBonus + separationBonus + compactnessBonus + combinedBonus,
         100.0
    )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_newEqualLiquidityCluster(
     SwingObject sourceSwing,
     SwingBasis candidateBasis,
     float candidatePrice,
     float tolerance
     ) =>

    LiquidityKind kind = f_getEqualLiquidityKind(
         sourceSwing.side
    )

    string subtype = f_getEqualLiquiditySubtype(
         kind,
         sourceSwing.degree,
         candidateBasis
    )

    ObjectMeta tempMeta = f_newMeta(
         "LIQ",
         subtype,
         sourceSwing.meta.originTf,
         sourceSwing.pivotTime,
         candidatePrice
    )

    array<string> memberIds = array.new<string>()

    array.push(
         memberIds,
         sourceSwing.meta.logicalId
    )

    int combinedCount = 0

    if sourceSwing.basis == SwingBasis.Combined
        combinedCount := 1

    EqualLiquidityClusterObject result = EqualLiquidityClusterObject.new(
         tempMeta.logicalId,
         sourceSwing.meta.originTf,
         kind,
         candidateBasis,
         sourceSwing.degree,
         candidatePrice,
         candidatePrice,
         candidatePrice,
         candidatePrice,
         0.0,
         tolerance,
         sourceSwing.meta.authority,
         0.0,
         sourceSwing.meta.authority,
         sourceSwing.meta.authority,
         sourceSwing.pivotTime,
         sourceSwing.pivotTime,
         sourceSwing.pivotBar,
         sourceSwing.pivotBar,
         na,
         sourceSwing.meta.confirmationTime,
         sourceSwing.meta.logicalId,
         memberIds,
         1,
         combinedCount,
         false,
         true
    )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_findBestEqualClusterIndex(
     array<EqualLiquidityClusterObject> storage,
     SwingObject sourceSwing,
     LiquidityKind kind,
     SwingBasis candidateBasis,
     float candidatePrice,
     float candidateTolerance
     ) =>

    int result = -1

    float bestDistance = na

    int storageSize = array.size(
         storage
    )

    if storageSize > 0

        for offset = 0 to storageSize - 1

            int i = storageSize - 1 - offset

            EqualLiquidityClusterObject cluster = array.get(
                 storage,
                 i
            )

            bool sameTf = cluster.originTf == sourceSwing.meta.originTf
            bool sameKind = cluster.kind == kind
            bool sameDegree = cluster.degree == sourceSwing.degree
            bool sameBasis = cluster.basis == candidateBasis

            bool newSource = not f_stringArrayContains(
                 cluster.memberSwingIds,
                 sourceSwing.meta.logicalId
            )

            bool newerPivot = sourceSwing.pivotTime > cluster.lastPivotTime

            bool validIdentity = cluster.acceptingMembers and sameTf and sameKind and sameDegree and sameBasis and newSource and newerPivot

            if validIdentity

                float comparisonTolerance = math.min(
                     cluster.anchorTolerance,
                     candidateTolerance
                )

                float anchorDistance = math.abs(
                     candidatePrice - cluster.anchorPrice
                )

                float newUpper = math.max(
                     cluster.upperBound,
                     candidatePrice
                )

                float newLower = math.min(
                     cluster.lowerBound,
                     candidatePrice
                )

                float newSpread = newUpper - newLower

                bool anchorValid = anchorDistance <= comparisonTolerance

                bool spreadValid = newSpread <= comparisonTolerance * 2.0

                if anchorValid and spreadValid

                    if na(bestDistance) or anchorDistance < bestDistance

                        bestDistance := anchorDistance

                        result := i

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_buildEqualLiquidityObject(
     EqualLiquidityClusterObject cluster
     ) =>

    string subtype = f_getEqualLiquiditySubtype(
         cluster.kind,
         cluster.degree,
         cluster.basis
    )

    ObjectMeta meta = f_newMeta(
         "LIQ",
         subtype,
         cluster.originTf,
         cluster.firstPivotTime,
         cluster.anchorPrice
    )

    meta.confirmationTime := cluster.confirmationTime
    meta.lastUpdateTime := cluster.lastUpdateTime

    meta.authority := cluster.authority
    meta.quality := cluster.quality
    meta.confluence := 0.0

    meta.confirmed := cluster.memberCount >= 2
    meta.archived := false

    array<string> memberIds = array.copy(
         cluster.memberSwingIds
    )

    LiquidityObject result = LiquidityObject.new(
         meta,
         cluster.kind,
         LiquidityOrigin.Cluster,
         cluster.basis,
         cluster.degree,
         cluster.meanPrice,
         cluster.upperBound,
         cluster.lowerBound,
         cluster.firstSourceSwingId,
         memberIds,
         cluster.memberCount,
         LiquidityLifecycle.Fresh,
         0,
         na,
         cluster.confirmationTime,
         "",
         cluster.maxSpread,
         true
    )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_syncEqualLiquidityObject(
     EqualLiquidityClusterObject cluster
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    if cluster.memberCount >= 2

        LiquidityObject equalLiquidityObject = f_buildEqualLiquidityObject(
             cluster
        )

        int existingIndex = f_findLiquidityIndexById(
             liquidityObjects,
             equalLiquidityObject.meta.logicalId
        )

        // ====================================================================
        // UPDATE EXISTING EQH / EQL
        // ====================================================================

        if existingIndex >= 0

            LiquidityObject existingLiquidity = array.get(
                 liquidityObjects,
                 existingIndex
            )

            // ----------------------------------------------------------------
            // Preserve Lifecycle.
            // ----------------------------------------------------------------

            equalLiquidityObject.state := existingLiquidity.state

            equalLiquidityObject.touchCount := existingLiquidity.touchCount

            equalLiquidityObject.meta.archived := existingLiquidity.meta.archived

            if existingLiquidity.state != LiquidityLifecycle.Fresh

                equalLiquidityObject.meta.lastUpdateTime := existingLiquidity.meta.lastUpdateTime

            // ----------------------------------------------------------------
            // Analytical database update.
            // ----------------------------------------------------------------

            array.set(
                 liquidityObjects,
                 existingIndex,
                 equalLiquidityObject
            )

            // ----------------------------------------------------------------
            // Active Registry reference must also be refreshed.
            // Terminal states will automatically be removed.
            // ----------------------------------------------------------------

            f_registerOrRefreshActiveLiquidity(
                 equalLiquidityObject
            )

        // ====================================================================
        // CREATE NEW CONFIRMED EQH / EQL
        // ====================================================================

        else

            array.push(
                 liquidityObjects,
                 equalLiquidityObject
            )

            f_registerOrRefreshActiveLiquidity(
                 equalLiquidityObject
            )

        result := true

    result    

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_addMemberToEqualCluster(
     EqualLiquidityClusterObject cluster,
     SwingObject sourceSwing,
     float candidatePrice
     ) =>

    int oldCount = cluster.memberCount

    int newCount = oldCount + 1

    cluster.meanPrice := (
         cluster.meanPrice * float(oldCount) + candidatePrice
    ) / float(newCount)

    cluster.upperBound := math.max(
         cluster.upperBound,
         candidatePrice
    )

    cluster.lowerBound := math.min(
         cluster.lowerBound,
         candidatePrice
    )

    cluster.maxSpread := cluster.upperBound - cluster.lowerBound

    cluster.lastPivotTime := sourceSwing.pivotTime
    cluster.lastPivotBar := sourceSwing.pivotBar

    cluster.lastUpdateTime := sourceSwing.meta.confirmationTime

    cluster.maxMemberAuthority := math.max(
         cluster.maxMemberAuthority,
         sourceSwing.meta.authority
    )

    cluster.totalMemberAuthority += sourceSwing.meta.authority

    if sourceSwing.basis == SwingBasis.Combined
        cluster.combinedSourceCount += 1

    array.push(
         cluster.memberSwingIds,
         sourceSwing.meta.logicalId
    )

    cluster.memberCount := newCount

    if newCount == 2

        cluster.confirmationTime := sourceSwing.meta.confirmationTime

        if na(cluster.confirmationTime)
            cluster.confirmationTime := time_close

    cluster.authority := f_getEqualClusterAuthority(
         cluster.maxMemberAuthority,
         cluster.memberCount
    )

    cluster.quality := f_getEqualClusterQuality(
         cluster.memberCount,
         cluster.firstPivotBar,
         cluster.lastPivotBar,
         cluster.maxSpread,
         cluster.anchorTolerance,
         cluster.combinedSourceCount
    )

    cluster

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_processEqualLiquidityCandidate(
     SwingObject sourceSwing,
     SwingBasis candidateBasis,
     float candidatePrice,
     float candidateTolerance
     ) =>

    bool processed = false

    if not na(candidatePrice)

        LiquidityKind kind = f_getEqualLiquidityKind(
             sourceSwing.side
        )

        int clusterIndex = f_findBestEqualClusterIndex(
             equalLiquidityClusters,
             sourceSwing,
             kind,
             candidateBasis,
             candidatePrice,
             candidateTolerance
        )

        // ====================================================================
        // CREATE NEW PROVISIONAL CLUSTER
        // ====================================================================

        if clusterIndex < 0

            EqualLiquidityClusterObject newCluster = f_newEqualLiquidityCluster(
                 sourceSwing,
                 candidateBasis,
                 candidatePrice,
                 candidateTolerance
            )

            array.push(
                 equalLiquidityClusters,
                 newCluster
            )

            processed := true

        // ====================================================================
        // ADD TO EXISTING CLUSTER
        // ====================================================================

        else

            EqualLiquidityClusterObject cluster = array.get(
                 equalLiquidityClusters,
                 clusterIndex
            )

            cluster := f_addMemberToEqualCluster(
                 cluster,
                 sourceSwing,
                 candidatePrice
            )

            array.set(
                 equalLiquidityClusters,
                 clusterIndex,
                 cluster
            )

            bool synced = f_syncEqualLiquidityObject(
                 cluster
            )

            if synced

                cluster.liquidityObjectCreated := true

                array.set(
                     equalLiquidityClusters,
                     clusterIndex,
                     cluster
                )

            processed := true

    processed

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int equalLiquidityInputSize = array.size(
     swingObjects
)

while equalLiquidityCursor < equalLiquidityInputSize

    int i = equalLiquidityCursor

    SwingObject sourceSwing = array.get(
         swingObjects,
         i
    )

    bool validSource = enableEqualLiquidity and sourceSwing.meta.confirmed and sourceSwing.liquidityEligible

    if validSource

        // ====================================================================
        // WICK STREAM
        // ====================================================================

        if sourceSwing.basis == SwingBasis.Wick or sourceSwing.basis == SwingBasis.Combined

            float wickTolerance = f_getEqualLiquidityTolerance(
                 sourceSwing.wickPrice,
                 equalLiquidityATR
            )

            f_processEqualLiquidityCandidate(
                 sourceSwing,
                 SwingBasis.Wick,
                 sourceSwing.wickPrice,
                 wickTolerance
            )

        // ====================================================================
        // CLOSE STREAM
        // ====================================================================

        if sourceSwing.basis == SwingBasis.Close or sourceSwing.basis == SwingBasis.Combined

            float closeTolerance = f_getEqualLiquidityTolerance(
                 sourceSwing.closePrice,
                 equalLiquidityATR
            )

            f_processEqualLiquidityCandidate(
                 sourceSwing,
                 SwingBasis.Close,
                 sourceSwing.closePrice,
                 closeTolerance
            )

    equalLiquidityCursor += 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(equalLiquidityClusters) > CORE_MAX_LIQUIDITY

    array.shift(
         equalLiquidityClusters
    )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(liquidityObjects) > CORE_MAX_LIQUIDITY

    f_shiftOldestLiquidityObject()

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

var array<line> equalLiquidityDevLines = array.new<line>()

var array<label> equalLiquidityDevLabels = array.new<label>()

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_clearEqualLiquidityDevDrawings() =>

    while array.size(equalLiquidityDevLines) > 0

        line oldLine = array.pop(
             equalLiquidityDevLines
        )

        if not na(oldLine)
            line.delete(oldLine)

    while array.size(equalLiquidityDevLabels) > 0

        label oldLabel = array.pop(
             equalLiquidityDevLabels
        )

        if not na(oldLabel)
            label.delete(oldLabel)

    true

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if barstate.islast

    f_clearEqualLiquidityDevDrawings()

    if equalLiquidityDevShowClusters

        int liquidityCount = array.size(
             liquidityObjects
        )

        int liquidityIndex = liquidityCount - 1

        int clusterDrawCount = 0

        while liquidityIndex >= 0 and clusterDrawCount < equalLiquidityDevMarkerLimit

            LiquidityObject liquidityObject = array.get(
                 liquidityObjects,
                 liquidityIndex
            )

            bool isEqualLiquidity = liquidityObject.kind == LiquidityKind.EQH or liquidityObject.kind == LiquidityKind.EQL

            bool isConsumed = liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.Consumed or liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.Invalid

            if isEqualLiquidity and not isConsumed and f_liquidityDegreeVisible(liquidityObject.degree)

                float levelPrice = liquidityObject.price

                int startTime = liquidityObject.meta.originTime

                if na(startTime)
                    startTime := time

                string kindText = f_liquidityKindToken(
                     liquidityObject.kind
                )

                string degreeText = f_swingDegreeToken(
                     liquidityObject.degree
                )

                string basisText = f_swingBasisToken(
                     liquidityObject.basis
                )

                string markerText = kindText + " | " + degreeText + " | " + basisText

                color levelColor = liquidityObject.kind == LiquidityKind.EQL
                     ? color.aqua
                     : color.orange

                bool clShowLine  = eqClusterDisplayMode == "Line + Label" or eqClusterDisplayMode == "Line Only"
                bool clShowLabel = eqClusterDisplayMode == "Line + Label" or eqClusterDisplayMode == "Label Only"
                bool clShowDot   = eqClusterDisplayMode == "Dot Markers"

                if not na(levelPrice)

                    if clShowLine
                        int x2Val = time

                        if eqExtendMode == "Fixed Bars After Current"
                            x2Val := startTime + eqFixedExtendBars * timeframe.multiplier * 1000
                        else if eqExtendMode == "To Mitigation Point" and isConsumed
                            x2Val := liquidityObject.lastStateChangeTime
                        else if eqExtendMode == "Fixed Bars After Mitigation" and isConsumed
                            x2Val := liquidityObject.lastStateChangeTime + eqFixedExtendBars * timeframe.multiplier * 1000

                        line clusterLine = line.new(
                             x1 = startTime,
                             y1 = levelPrice,
                             x2 = x2Val,
                             y2 = levelPrice,
                             xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                             extend = extend.none,
                             color = color.new(levelColor, f_getLineTransparency(false)),
                             width = 2,
                             style = line.style_solid
                        )

                        array.push(equalLiquidityDevLines, clusterLine)

                    if clShowLabel
                        label clusterLabel = label.new(
                             x = time,
                             y = levelPrice,
                             text = markerText,
                             xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                             yloc = yloc.price,
                             style = label.style_label_left,
                             color = color.new(color.black, 100),
                             textcolor = color.new(levelColor, f_getLabelTransparency(false)),
                             size = size.tiny
                        )

                        array.push(equalLiquidityDevLabels, clusterLabel)

                    if clShowDot
                        for di = 0 to clusterMarkerCount - 1
                            label dotLabel = label.new(
                                 x = time - (di * timeframe.multiplier * 1000 * 2),
                                 y = levelPrice,
                                 text = "●",
                                 xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                                 yloc = yloc.price,
                                 style = label.style_label_center,
                                 color = color.new(color.black, 100),
                                 textcolor = color.new(levelColor, f_getLabelTransparency(false)),
                                 size = size.tiny
                            )

                            array.push(equalLiquidityDevLabels, dotLabel)

                    clusterDrawCount += 1

            liquidityIndex -= 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int equalClusterCandidateCount = array.size(
     equalLiquidityClusters
)

int eqhCount = 0
int eqlCount = 0

int wickEqualLiquidityCount = 0
int closeEqualLiquidityCount = 0

if barstate.islast and coreDebug

    int liquidityCount = array.size(
         liquidityObjects
    )

    if liquidityCount > 0

        for i = 0 to liquidityCount - 1

            LiquidityObject debugLiquidity = array.get(
                 liquidityObjects,
                 i
            )

            if debugLiquidity.kind == LiquidityKind.EQH

                eqhCount += 1

                if debugLiquidity.basis == SwingBasis.Wick
                    wickEqualLiquidityCount += 1

                else if debugLiquidity.basis == SwingBasis.Close
                    closeEqualLiquidityCount += 1

            else if debugLiquidity.kind == LiquidityKind.EQL

                eqlCount += 1

                if debugLiquidity.basis == SwingBasis.Wick
                    wickEqualLiquidityCount += 1

                else if debugLiquidity.basis == SwingBasis.Close
                    closeEqualLiquidityCount += 1

// ============================================================================

plot(

     title = "DEBUG EQ CLUSTER CANDIDATES",

plot(

     title = "DEBUG EQH COUNT",
     display = display.none
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(eqlCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG EQL COUNT",
     display = display.none
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(wickEqualLiquidityCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG WICK EQ LIQUIDITY",
     display = display.none
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(closeEqualLiquidityCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG CLOSE EQ LIQUIDITY",
     display = display.none
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

string grpLiquidityLifecycle = "02 LIQUIDITY ENGINE / LIFECYCLE"

bool enableLiquidityLifecycle = input.bool(
     true,
     "Enable Liquidity Lifecycle",
     group = grpLiquidityLifecycle
)

int sweepMinimumPenetrationTicks = input.int(
     1,
     "Minimum Sweep Penetration Ticks",
     minval = 0,
     maxval = 100,
     group = grpLiquidityLifecycle
)

int sweepReclaimBufferTicks = input.int(
     0,
     "Reclaim Buffer Ticks",
     minval = 0,
     maxval = 100,
     group = grpLiquidityLifecycle
)

int liquidityAcceptanceBufferTicks = input.int(
     1,
     "Acceptance Beyond Buffer Ticks",
     minval = 0,
     maxval = 100,
     group = grpLiquidityLifecycle
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_isBuySideLiquidity(
     LiquidityKind kind
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    if kind == LiquidityKind.BSL
        result := true

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.EQH
        result := true

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.PDH
        result := true

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.PWH
        result := true

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.SessionHigh
        result := true

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.CustomHigh
        result := true

    result

f_isSellSideLiquidity(
     LiquidityKind kind
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    if kind == LiquidityKind.SSL
        result := true

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.EQL
        result := true

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.PDL
        result := true

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.PWL
        result := true

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.SessionLow
        result := true

    else if kind == LiquidityKind.CustomLow
        result := true

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_isTerminalLiquidityState(
     LiquidityLifecycle state
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    if state == LiquidityLifecycle.ValidatedSweep
        result := true

    else if state == LiquidityLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond
        result := true

    else if state == LiquidityLifecycle.Consumed
        result := true

    else if state == LiquidityLifecycle.Invalid
        result := true

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================
f_findEqualClusterIndexByLogicalId(
     array<EqualLiquidityClusterObject> storage,
     string logicalId
     ) =>

    int result = -1
    int storageSize = array.size(storage)

    if storageSize > 0

        for offset = 0 to storageSize - 1

            int i = storageSize - 1 - offset

            EqualLiquidityClusterObject cluster = array.get(
                 storage,
                 i
            )

            if cluster.logicalId == logicalId
                result := i
                break

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_closeEqualClusterByLogicalId(
     string logicalId
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    int clusterIndex = f_findEqualClusterIndexByLogicalId(
         equalLiquidityClusters,
         logicalId
    )

    if clusterIndex >= 0

        EqualLiquidityClusterObject cluster = array.get(
             equalLiquidityClusters,
             clusterIndex
        )

        cluster.acceptingMembers := false
        cluster.lastUpdateTime := time_close

        array.set(
             equalLiquidityClusters,
             clusterIndex,
             cluster
        )

        result := true

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_closeEqualClustersBySourceSwingId(
     string sourceSwingId
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    int clusterCount = array.size(
         equalLiquidityClusters
    )

    if clusterCount > 0 and sourceSwingId != ""

        for i = 0 to clusterCount - 1

            EqualLiquidityClusterObject cluster = array.get(
                 equalLiquidityClusters,
                 i
            )

            if cluster.acceptingMembers

                bool containsSource = f_stringArrayContains(
                     cluster.memberSwingIds,
                     sourceSwingId
                )

                if containsSource

                    cluster.acceptingMembers := false
                    cluster.lastUpdateTime := time_close

                    array.set(
                         equalLiquidityClusters,
                         i,
                         cluster
                    )

                    result := true

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

var int liquidityTouchTransitionCount = 0
var int liquiditySweepCandidateCount = 0
var int liquidityValidatedSweepCount = 0
var int liquidityAcceptedBeyondCount = 0

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if enableLiquidityLifecycle and barstate.isconfirmed

    int activeLiquidityCount = array.size(
         activeLiquidityObjects
    )

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------

    int activeLiquidityCap = math.min(activeLiquidityCount, ACTIVE_LIQUIDITY_SCAN)

    if activeLiquidityCap > 0

        for offset = 0 to activeLiquidityCap - 1

            int activePosition = activeLiquidityCap - 1 - offset

            if activePosition < array.size(activeLiquidityObjects)

                LiquidityObject liquidityObject = array.get(
                     activeLiquidityObjects,
                     activePosition
                )

                LiquidityLifecycle previousState = liquidityObject.state

                bool alreadyTerminal = f_isTerminalLiquidityState(
                     previousState
                )

                // =============================================================
                // ONLY ACTIVE LIQUIDITY IS PROCESSED
                // =============================================================

                if not alreadyTerminal

                    bool isBuySide = f_isBuySideLiquidity(
                         liquidityObject.kind
                    )

                    bool isSellSide = f_isSellSideLiquidity(
                         liquidityObject.kind
                    )

                    // =========================================================
                    // NORMALIZE ZONE
                    // =========================================================

                    float zoneTop = liquidityObject.upperBound

                    float zoneBottom = liquidityObject.lowerBound

                    if na(zoneTop)
                        zoneTop := liquidityObject.price

                    if na(zoneBottom)
                        zoneBottom := liquidityObject.price

                    if not na(zoneTop) and not na(zoneBottom)

                        float normalizedTop = math.max(
                             zoneTop,
                             zoneBottom
                        )

                        float normalizedBottom = math.min(
                             zoneTop,
                             zoneBottom
                        )

                        zoneTop := normalizedTop
                        zoneBottom := normalizedBottom

                    // =========================================================
                    // ACTIVATION TIME
                    // =========================================================

                    int activationTime = liquidityObject.meta.confirmationTime

                    if na(activationTime)
                        activationTime := liquidityObject.meta.originTime

                    bool canInteract = na(activationTime) or time_close > activationTime

                    // =========================================================
                    // PRICE BUFFERS
                    // =========================================================

                    float penetrationBuffer = syminfo.mintick * float(sweepMinimumPenetrationTicks)

                    float reclaimBuffer = syminfo.mintick * float(sweepReclaimBufferTicks)

                    float acceptanceBuffer = syminfo.mintick * float(liquidityAcceptanceBufferTicks)

                    // =========================================================
                    // VALIDITY
                    // =========================================================

                    bool validZone = not na(zoneTop) and not na(zoneBottom)

                    bool validKind = isBuySide or isSellSide

                    if not validZone or not validKind

                        liquidityObject.state := LiquidityLifecycle.Invalid

                    else if canInteract

                        // =====================================================
                        // BUY-SIDE LIQUIDITY
                        // =====================================================

                        if isBuySide

                            bool touchedNow = high >= zoneBottom

                            bool penetratedNow = high > zoneTop + penetrationBuffer

                            bool reclaimedNow = close <= zoneBottom - reclaimBuffer

                            bool acceptedBeyondNow = close > zoneTop + acceptanceBuffer

                            // -------------------------------------------------
                            // First Touch
                            // -------------------------------------------------

                            if previousState == LiquidityLifecycle.Fresh and touchedNow

                                liquidityObject.touchCount += 1

                                liquidityTouchTransitionCount += 1

                            // -------------------------------------------------
                            // Existing Sweep Candidate
                            // -------------------------------------------------

                            if previousState == LiquidityLifecycle.SweepCandidate

                                if acceptedBeyondNow

                                    liquidityObject.state := LiquidityLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond

                                else if reclaimedNow

                                    liquidityObject.state := LiquidityLifecycle.ValidatedSweep

                            // -------------------------------------------------
                            // Fresh / Touched
                            // -------------------------------------------------

                            else if previousState == LiquidityLifecycle.Fresh or previousState == LiquidityLifecycle.Touched

                                if acceptedBeyondNow

                                    liquidityObject.state := LiquidityLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond

                                else if penetratedNow

                                    if reclaimedNow

                                        liquidityObject.state := LiquidityLifecycle.ValidatedSweep

                                    else

                                        liquidityObject.state := LiquidityLifecycle.SweepCandidate

                                else if touchedNow

                                    liquidityObject.state := LiquidityLifecycle.Touched

                        // =====================================================
                        // SELL-SIDE LIQUIDITY
                        // =====================================================

                        else if isSellSide

                            bool touchedNow = low <= zoneTop

                            bool penetratedNow = low < zoneBottom - penetrationBuffer

                            bool reclaimedNow = close >= zoneTop + reclaimBuffer

                            bool acceptedBeyondNow = close < zoneBottom - acceptanceBuffer

                            // -------------------------------------------------
                            // First Touch
                            // -------------------------------------------------

                            if previousState == LiquidityLifecycle.Fresh and touchedNow

                                liquidityObject.touchCount += 1

                                liquidityTouchTransitionCount += 1

                            // -------------------------------------------------
                            // Existing Sweep Candidate
                            // -------------------------------------------------

                            if previousState == LiquidityLifecycle.SweepCandidate

                                if acceptedBeyondNow

                                    liquidityObject.state := LiquidityLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond

                                else if reclaimedNow

                                    liquidityObject.state := LiquidityLifecycle.ValidatedSweep

                            // -------------------------------------------------
                            // Fresh / Touched
                            // -------------------------------------------------

                            else if previousState == LiquidityLifecycle.Fresh or previousState == LiquidityLifecycle.Touched

                                if acceptedBeyondNow

                                    liquidityObject.state := LiquidityLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond

                                else if penetratedNow

                                    if reclaimedNow

                                        liquidityObject.state := LiquidityLifecycle.ValidatedSweep

                                    else

                                        liquidityObject.state := LiquidityLifecycle.SweepCandidate

                                else if touchedNow

                                    liquidityObject.state := LiquidityLifecycle.Touched

                // =================================================================
                // STATE TRANSITION MANAGEMENT
                // =================================================================

                bool stateChanged = liquidityObject.state != previousState

                if stateChanged

                    ObjectMeta lifecycleMeta = liquidityObject.meta

                    lifecycleMeta.lastUpdateTime := time_close

                    liquidityObject.meta := lifecycleMeta

                    // -------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Statistics
                    // -------------------------------------------------------------

                    if liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.SweepCandidate

                        liquiditySweepCandidateCount += 1

                    else if liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.ValidatedSweep

                        liquidityValidatedSweepCount += 1

                    else if liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond

                        liquidityAcceptedBeyondCount += 1

                    // -------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Explicit Active Registry update
                    // -------------------------------------------------------------

                    array.set(
                        activeLiquidityObjects,
                        activePosition,
                        liquidityObject
                    )

                    // -------------------------------------------------------------
                    // -------------------------------------------------------------

                    bool finalSweepEventTransition = liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.ValidatedSweep or liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond

                    if finalSweepEventTransition

                        array.push(
                            liquiditySweepTransitionQueue,
                            liquidityObject
                        )

                    // =============================================================
                    // TERMINAL TRANSITION
                    // =============================================================

                    bool becameTerminal = f_isTerminalLiquidityState(
                        liquidityObject.state
                    )

                    if becameTerminal

                        // ---------------------------------------------------------
                        // Confirmed EQH / EQL Cluster no longer accepts members.
                        // ---------------------------------------------------------

                        if liquidityObject.kind == LiquidityKind.EQH or liquidityObject.kind == LiquidityKind.EQL

                            f_closeEqualClusterByLogicalId(
                                liquidityObject.meta.logicalId
                            )

                        // ---------------------------------------------------------
                        // Terminal BSL / SSL cannot later seed a new provisional
                        // Equal-Liquidity Cluster.
                        // ---------------------------------------------------------

                        if liquidityObject.kind == LiquidityKind.BSL or liquidityObject.kind == LiquidityKind.SSL

                            f_closeEqualClustersBySourceSwingId(
                                liquidityObject.sourceSwingId
                            )

                        // ---------------------------------------------------------
                        // Remove ONLY from Active registry.
                        // Historical LiquidityObject remains preserved.
                        // ---------------------------------------------------------

                        f_unregisterActiveLiquidityById(
                            liquidityObject.meta.logicalId
                        )

                        // =================================================================
                        // =================================================================

                        if liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.SweepCandidate

                            array.push(
                                liquiditySweepTransitionQueue,
                                liquidityObject
                            )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(liquidityTouchTransitionCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG LIQ TOUCH TRANSITIONS",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(liquiditySweepCandidateCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG SWEEP CANDIDATES",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(liquidityValidatedSweepCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG VALIDATED SWEEPS",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(liquidityAcceptedBeyondCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG ACCEPTED BEYOND",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(array.size(activeLiquidityObjects)) : na,
     title = "DEBUG ACTIVE LIQUIDITY COUNT",
     display = display.data_window
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

string grpSweepEvent = "02 LIQUIDITY ENGINE / SWEEP EVENT"

int sweepQualityATRLength = input.int(
     14,
     "Sweep Quality ATR Length",
     minval = 1,
     group = grpSweepEvent
)

float sweepQualityFullPenetrationATR = input.float(
     0.20,
     "Full Penetration Score at ATR Fraction",
     minval = 0.01,
     maxval = 2.0,
     step = 0.01,
     group = grpSweepEvent
)

float sweepQualityFullReclaimATR = input.float(
     0.15,
     "Full Reclaim Score at ATR Fraction",
     minval = 0.01,
     maxval = 2.0,
     step = 0.01,
     group = grpSweepEvent
)

float sweepQualityFullAcceptanceATR = input.float(
     0.20,
     "Full Acceptance Score at ATR Fraction",
     minval = 0.01,
     maxval = 2.0,
     step = 0.01,
     group = grpSweepEvent
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

float sweepQualityATR = ta.atr(
     sweepQualityATRLength
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getSweepLifecycleFromLiquidity(
     LiquidityLifecycle liquidityState
     ) =>

    SweepLifecycle result = SweepLifecycle.Candidate

    if liquidityState == LiquidityLifecycle.SweepCandidate
        result := SweepLifecycle.Candidate

    else if liquidityState == LiquidityLifecycle.ValidatedSweep
        result := SweepLifecycle.Validated

    else if liquidityState == LiquidityLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond
        result := SweepLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond

    else if liquidityState == LiquidityLifecycle.Invalid
        result := SweepLifecycle.Invalid

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getSweepEventDirection(
     LiquidityObject liquidityObject,
     SweepLifecycle eventState
     ) =>

    Direction result = Direction.Neutral

    bool buySide = f_isBuySideLiquidity(
         liquidityObject.kind
    )

    bool sellSide = f_isSellSideLiquidity(
         liquidityObject.kind
    )

    if eventState == SweepLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond

        if buySide
            result := Direction.Bullish

        else if sellSide
            result := Direction.Bearish

    else

        if buySide
            result := Direction.Bearish

        else if sellSide
            result := Direction.Bullish

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getNormalizedDepthScore(
     float depth,
     float atrValue,
     float fullAtATR
     ) =>

    float result = 0.0

    if depth > 0.0

        if not na(atrValue) and atrValue > 0.0 and fullAtATR > 0.0

            float targetDepth = atrValue * fullAtATR

            result := math.min(
                 depth / targetDepth * 100.0,
                 100.0
            )

        else

            float fallbackDepth = syminfo.mintick * 10.0

            if fallbackDepth > 0.0

                result := math.min(
                     depth / fallbackDepth * 100.0,
                     100.0
                )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getSweepClusterStrengthScore(
     LiquidityObject liquidityObject
     ) =>

    float result = 0.0

    if liquidityObject.memberCount >= 2

        result := math.min(
             40.0 + float(liquidityObject.memberCount - 1) * 20.0,
             100.0
        )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getSweepFirstInteractionScore(
     int touchCount
     ) =>

    float result = 100.0

    if touchCount > 1

        result := math.max(
             100.0 - float(touchCount - 1) * 20.0,
             20.0
        )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getSweepResponseScore(
     LiquidityObject liquidityObject,
     SweepLifecycle eventState
     ) =>

    float result = 0.0

    float candleRange = high - low

    if candleRange > 0.0

        bool buySide = f_isBuySideLiquidity(
             liquidityObject.kind
        )

        if eventState == SweepLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond

            if buySide

                result := (
                     close - low
                ) / candleRange * 100.0

            else

                result := (
                     high - close
                ) / candleRange * 100.0

        else

            if buySide

                float upperWick = high - math.max(
                     open,
                     close
                )

                result := upperWick / candleRange * 100.0

            else

                float lowerWick = math.min(
                     open,
                     close
                ) - low

                result := lowerWick / candleRange * 100.0

    result := math.min(
         math.max(result, 0.0),
         100.0
    )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_recalculateSweepEventQuality(
     SweepEventObject event
     ) =>

    float result = 0.0

    // ========================================================================
    // CANDIDATE
    // ========================================================================

    if event.state == SweepLifecycle.Candidate

        result := event.sourceAuthorityScore * 0.25
        result += event.clusterStrengthScore * 0.10
        result += event.penetrationScore * 0.25
        result += event.rejectionScore * 0.15
        result += event.firstInteractionScore * 0.10
        result += event.postSweepDisplacementScore * 0.05
        result += event.structureResponseScore * 0.05
        result += event.sessionContextScore * 0.05

    // ========================================================================
    // VALIDATED SWEEP
    // ========================================================================

    else if event.state == SweepLifecycle.Validated

        result := event.sourceAuthorityScore * 0.20
        result += event.clusterStrengthScore * 0.10
        result += event.penetrationScore * 0.15
        result += event.reclaimScore * 0.20
        result += event.rejectionScore * 0.15
        result += event.firstInteractionScore * 0.10
        result += event.postSweepDisplacementScore * 0.05
        result += event.structureResponseScore * 0.03
        result += event.sessionContextScore * 0.02

    // ========================================================================
    // ACCEPTED BEYOND
    // ========================================================================

    else if event.state == SweepLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond

        result := event.sourceAuthorityScore * 0.20
        result += event.clusterStrengthScore * 0.10
        result += event.penetrationScore * 0.15
        result += event.acceptanceScore * 0.20
        result += event.rejectionScore * 0.15
        result += event.firstInteractionScore * 0.10
        result += event.postSweepDisplacementScore * 0.05
        result += event.structureResponseScore * 0.03
        result += event.sessionContextScore * 0.02

    event.quality := math.min(
         math.max(result, 0.0),
         100.0
    )

    event.meta.quality := event.quality

    event

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_syncSweepEventFromLiquidity(
     array<SweepEventObject> storage,
     map<string, SweepEventObject> eventIndex,
     LiquidityObject liquidityObject,
     float atrValue
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    string sourceLiquidityId = liquidityObject.meta.logicalId

    if sourceLiquidityId != ""

        SweepLifecycle eventState = f_getSweepLifecycleFromLiquidity(
             liquidityObject.state
        )

        bool relevantState = liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.SweepCandidate or liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.ValidatedSweep or liquidityObject.state == LiquidityLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond

        if relevantState

            bool buySide = f_isBuySideLiquidity(
                 liquidityObject.kind
            )

            // ----------------------------------------------------------------
            // Normalize liquidity zone.
            // ----------------------------------------------------------------

            float zoneTop = liquidityObject.upperBound
            float zoneBottom = liquidityObject.lowerBound

            if na(zoneTop)
                zoneTop := liquidityObject.price

            if na(zoneBottom)
                zoneBottom := liquidityObject.price

            float normalizedTop = math.max(
                 zoneTop,
                 zoneBottom
            )

            float normalizedBottom = math.min(
                 zoneTop,
                 zoneBottom
            )

            zoneTop := normalizedTop
            zoneBottom := normalizedBottom

            // ----------------------------------------------------------------
            // Current event metrics.
            // ----------------------------------------------------------------

            float currentExtreme = buySide ? high : low

            float currentPenetrationDepth = 0.0

            if buySide
                currentPenetrationDepth := math.max(
                     high - zoneTop,
                     0.0
                )

            else
                currentPenetrationDepth := math.max(
                     zoneBottom - low,
                     0.0
                )

            float currentReclaimDistance = 0.0

            if eventState == SweepLifecycle.Validated

                if buySide

                    currentReclaimDistance := math.max(
                         zoneBottom - close,
                         0.0
                    )

                else

                    currentReclaimDistance := math.max(
                         close - zoneTop,
                         0.0
                    )

            float currentAcceptanceDepth = 0.0

            if eventState == SweepLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond

                if buySide

                    currentAcceptanceDepth := math.max(
                         close - zoneTop,
                         0.0
                    )

                else

                    currentAcceptanceDepth := math.max(
                         zoneBottom - close,
                         0.0
                    )

            // ================================================================
            // EXISTING EVENT
            // ================================================================

            SweepEventObject event = na

            if map.contains(
                 eventIndex,
                 sourceLiquidityId
            )

                event := map.get(
                     eventIndex,
                     sourceLiquidityId
                )

            // ================================================================
            // NEW EVENT
            // ================================================================

            else

                event := SweepEventObject.new()

                string subtype = "SWEEP_" + f_liquidityKindToken(liquidityObject.kind) + "_" + f_swingDegreeToken(liquidityObject.degree) + "_" + f_swingBasisToken(liquidityObject.basis)

                ObjectMeta eventMeta = f_newMeta(
                     "SWEEP",
                     subtype,
                     liquidityObject.meta.originTf,
                     time,
                     liquidityObject.price
                )

                // Stable ID across Candidate -> Validated / AcceptedBeyond.
                eventMeta.logicalId := "SWEEP|" + sourceLiquidityId

                eventMeta.confirmationTime := na
                eventMeta.lastUpdateTime := time_close
                eventMeta.authority := liquidityObject.meta.authority
                eventMeta.quality := 0.0
                eventMeta.confluence := 0.0
                eventMeta.confirmed := false
                eventMeta.archived := false

                event.meta := eventMeta

                event.sourceLiquidityId := sourceLiquidityId
                event.sourceKind := liquidityObject.kind
                event.sourceDegree := liquidityObject.degree
                event.sourceBasis := liquidityObject.basis

                event.levelPrice := liquidityObject.price
                event.zoneTop := zoneTop
                event.zoneBottom := zoneBottom

                event.candidateTime := time_close
                event.validationTime := na
                event.acceptedTime := na
                event.lastEventTime := time_close
                event.eventBar := bar_index

                event.extremePrice := currentExtreme

                event.penetrationDepth := 0.0
                event.reclaimDistance := 0.0
                event.acceptanceDepth := 0.0

                event.sourceAuthorityScore := math.min(
                     math.max(liquidityObject.meta.authority, 0.0),
                     100.0
                )

                event.clusterStrengthScore := f_getSweepClusterStrengthScore(
                     liquidityObject
                )

                event.penetrationScore := 0.0
                event.reclaimScore := 0.0
                event.acceptanceScore := 0.0
                event.rejectionScore := 0.0

                event.firstInteractionScore := f_getSweepFirstInteractionScore(
                     liquidityObject.touchCount
                )

                event.postSweepDisplacementScore := 0.0
                event.structureResponseScore := 0.0
                event.sessionContextScore := 0.0

                event.quality := 0.0

                event.firstInteraction := liquidityObject.touchCount <= 1

                event.linkedDisplacementId := ""
                event.linkedStructureEventId := ""

                array.push(
                     storage,
                     event
                )

                map.put(
                     eventIndex,
                     sourceLiquidityId,
                     event
                )

            // =================================================================
            // STATE UPDATE
            // =================================================================

            event.state := eventState

            event.direction := f_getSweepEventDirection(
                 liquidityObject,
                 eventState
            )

            event.lastEventTime := time_close
            event.eventBar := bar_index

            event.zoneTop := zoneTop
            event.zoneBottom := zoneBottom

            event.sourceAuthorityScore := math.min(
                 math.max(liquidityObject.meta.authority, 0.0),
                 100.0
            )

            event.clusterStrengthScore := f_getSweepClusterStrengthScore(
                 liquidityObject
            )

            event.firstInteractionScore := f_getSweepFirstInteractionScore(
                 liquidityObject.touchCount
            )

            event.firstInteraction := liquidityObject.touchCount <= 1

            // =================================================================
            // MAX PENETRATION TRACKING
            // =================================================================

            event.penetrationDepth := math.max(
                 event.penetrationDepth,
                 currentPenetrationDepth
            )

            if buySide

                event.extremePrice := math.max(
                     event.extremePrice,
                     currentExtreme
                )

            else

                event.extremePrice := math.min(
                     event.extremePrice,
                     currentExtreme
                )

            event.penetrationScore := f_getNormalizedDepthScore(
                 event.penetrationDepth,
                 atrValue,
                 sweepQualityFullPenetrationATR
            )

            // =================================================================
            // RESPONSE SCORE
            // =================================================================

            event.rejectionScore := f_getSweepResponseScore(
                 liquidityObject,
                 eventState
            )

            // =================================================================
            // VALIDATED SWEEP
            // =================================================================

            if eventState == SweepLifecycle.Validated

                event.validationTime := time_close

                event.reclaimDistance := math.max(
                     event.reclaimDistance,
                     currentReclaimDistance
                )

                event.reclaimScore := f_getNormalizedDepthScore(
                     event.reclaimDistance,
                     atrValue,
                     sweepQualityFullReclaimATR
                )

                event.meta.confirmationTime := time_close
                event.meta.confirmed := true

            // =================================================================
            // ACCEPTED BEYOND
            // =================================================================

            else if eventState == SweepLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond

                event.acceptedTime := time_close

                event.acceptanceDepth := math.max(
                     event.acceptanceDepth,
                     currentAcceptanceDepth
                )

                event.acceptanceScore := f_getNormalizedDepthScore(
                     event.acceptanceDepth,
                     atrValue,
                     sweepQualityFullAcceptanceATR
                )

                event.meta.confirmationTime := time_close
                event.meta.confirmed := true

            // =================================================================
            // CANDIDATE
            // =================================================================

            else

                event.meta.confirmed := false

            event.meta.lastUpdateTime := time_close

            event.meta.authority := liquidityObject.meta.authority

            // =================================================================
            // QUALITY
            // =================================================================

            event := f_recalculateSweepEventQuality(
                 event
            )

            result := true

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_trimSweepEventHistory(
     array<SweepEventObject> storage,
     map<string, SweepEventObject> eventIndex,
     int maxCount
     ) =>

    bool trimmed = false

    while array.size(storage) > maxCount

        SweepEventObject removedEvent = array.shift(
             storage
        )

        string sourceLiquidityId = removedEvent.sourceLiquidityId

        if map.contains(
             eventIndex,
             sourceLiquidityId
        )

            SweepEventObject mappedEvent = map.get(
                 eventIndex,
                 sourceLiquidityId
            )

            if mappedEvent.meta.logicalId == removedEvent.meta.logicalId

                map.remove(
                     eventIndex,
                     sourceLiquidityId
                )

        trimmed := true

    trimmed

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(liquiditySweepTransitionQueue) > 0

    LiquidityObject eventLiquidity = array.shift(
         liquiditySweepTransitionQueue
    )

    f_syncSweepEventFromLiquidity(
         sweepEvents,
         sweepEventByLiquidityId,
         eventLiquidity,
         sweepQualityATR
    )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_trimSweepEventHistory(
     sweepEvents,
     sweepEventByLiquidityId,
     CORE_MAX_LIQUIDITY
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================
int sweepEventCount = array.size(
     sweepEvents
)

int sweepCandidateEventCount = 0
int validatedSweepEventCount = 0
int acceptedBeyondEventCount = 0

int bullishValidatedSweepCount = 0
int bearishValidatedSweepCount = 0

if barstate.islast and coreDebug and sweepEventCount > 0

    for i = 0 to sweepEventCount - 1

        SweepEventObject debugEvent = array.get(
             sweepEvents,
             i
        )

        if debugEvent.state == SweepLifecycle.Candidate

            sweepCandidateEventCount += 1

        else if debugEvent.state == SweepLifecycle.Validated

            validatedSweepEventCount += 1

            if debugEvent.direction == Direction.Bullish
                bullishValidatedSweepCount += 1

            else if debugEvent.direction == Direction.Bearish
                bearishValidatedSweepCount += 1

        else if debugEvent.state == SweepLifecycle.AcceptedBeyond

            acceptedBeyondEventCount += 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(sweepEventCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG SWEEP EVENT COUNT",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(sweepCandidateEventCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG ACTIVE SWEEP EVENT CANDIDATES",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(validatedSweepEventCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG VALIDATED SWEEP EVENTS",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(acceptedBeyondEventCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG ACCEPTED BEYOND EVENTS",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(bullishValidatedSweepCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG BULLISH VALIDATED SWEEPS",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(bearishValidatedSweepCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG BEARISH VALIDATED SWEEPS",
     display = display.data_window
)

     true,

     true,
     "Micro Structure",
     group = grpMarketStructure

     "Structure Break Method",
     options = ["Wick", "Close", "Combined"],

f_structureDegreeEnabled(
     SwingDegree degree
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    if degree == SwingDegree.External
        result := enableExternalStructure

    else if degree == SwingDegree.Internal
        result := enableInternalStructure

    else if degree == SwingDegree.Micro
        result := enableMicroStructure

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_makeStructureContextLogicalId(
     string originTf,
     SwingDegree degree
     ) =>

    string result = "STRUCT_CTX|" + syminfo.tickerid + "|" + originTf + "|" + f_swingDegreeToken(degree)

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_findStructureContextIndex(
     array<StructureContextObject> storage,
     string originTf,
     SwingDegree degree
     ) =>

    int result = -1

    int storageSize = array.size(
         storage
    )

    if storageSize > 0

        for i = 0 to storageSize - 1

            StructureContextObject context = array.get(
                 storage,
                 i
            )

            bool sameTf = context.meta.originTf == originTf

            bool sameDegree = context.degree == degree

            if sameTf and sameDegree

                result := i

                break

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_newStructureContext(
     SwingSequenceNode node
     ) =>

    string subtype = "CTX_" + f_swingDegreeToken(node.degree)

    ObjectMeta contextMeta = f_newMeta(
         "STRUCTURE",
         subtype,
         node.originTf,
         node.pivotTime,
         node.price
    )

    contextMeta.logicalId := f_makeStructureContextLogicalId(
         node.originTf,
         node.degree
    )

    contextMeta.confirmationTime := node.pivotTime
    contextMeta.lastUpdateTime := node.pivotTime

    contextMeta.authority := 0.0
    contextMeta.quality := 0.0
    contextMeta.confluence := 0.0

    contextMeta.confirmed := true
    contextMeta.archived := false

    string latestHighId = ""
    float latestHighPrice = na
    int latestHighTime = na

    string latestLowId = ""
    float latestLowPrice = na
    int latestLowTime = na

    if node.side == SwingSide.High

        latestHighId := node.swingId
        latestHighPrice := node.price
        latestHighTime := node.pivotTime

    else

        latestLowId := node.swingId
        latestLowPrice := node.price
        latestLowTime := node.pivotTime

    StructureContextObject result = StructureContextObject.new(
         contextMeta,
         node.degree,
         Direction.Neutral,
         node.swingId,
         latestHighId,
         latestHighPrice,
         latestHighTime,
         latestLowId,
         latestLowPrice,
         latestLowTime,
         "",
         na,
         na,
         "",
         na,
         na,
         "",
         "",
         "",
         "",
         "",
         na,
         true
    )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_updateStructureContextFromNode(
     StructureContextObject context,
     SwingSequenceNode node
     ) =>

    context.latestStructuralSwingId := node.swingId

    if node.side == SwingSide.High

        context.latestHighSwingId := node.swingId

        context.latestHighPrice := node.price

        context.latestHighTime := node.pivotTime

    else

        context.latestLowSwingId := node.swingId

        context.latestLowPrice := node.price

        context.latestLowTime := node.pivotTime

    ObjectMeta contextMeta = context.meta

    contextMeta.lastUpdateTime := node.pivotTime

    context.meta := contextMeta

    context.initialized := true

    context

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_findSequenceNodeIndexBySwingId(
     array<SwingSequenceNode> storage,
     string swingId
     ) =>

    int result = -1

    int storageSize = array.size(
         storage
    )

    if storageSize > 0 and swingId != ""

        for offset = 0 to storageSize - 1

            int i = storageSize - 1 - offset

            SwingSequenceNode node = array.get(
                 storage,
                 i
            )

            if node.swingId == swingId

                result := i

                break

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getStructuralLegOriginSwingId(
     string brokenSwingId
     ) =>

    string result = ""

    int nodeIndex = f_findSequenceNodeIndexBySwingId(
         swingSequenceNodes,
         brokenSwingId
    )

    if nodeIndex >= 0

        SwingSequenceNode brokenNode = array.get(
             swingSequenceNodes,
             nodeIndex
        )

        result := brokenNode.previousSequenceSwingId

    result

f_getSwingPriceById(
     string swingId
     ) =>

    float result = na

    if swingId != "" and map.contains(
         swingObjectById,
         swingId
    )

        SwingObject swingObject = map.get(
             swingObjectById,
             swingId
        )

        result := swingObject.price

    result

f_getSwingTimeById(
     string swingId
     ) =>

    int result = na

    if swingId != "" and map.contains(
         swingObjectById,
         swingId
    )

        SwingObject swingObject = map.get(
             swingObjectById,
             swingId
        )

        result := swingObject.pivotTime

    result
