// ============================================================================

while array.size(structureSwingQueue) > 0

    SwingSequenceNode structureNode = array.shift(
         structureSwingQueue
    )

    bool degreeEnabled = f_structureDegreeEnabled(
         structureNode.degree
    )

    if enableMarketStructureEngine and degreeEnabled

        int contextIndex = f_findStructureContextIndex(
             structureContexts,
             structureNode.originTf,
             structureNode.degree
        )

        // ====================================================================
        // CREATE NEW CONTEXT
        // ====================================================================

        if contextIndex < 0

            StructureContextObject newContext = f_newStructureContext(
                 structureNode
            )

            array.push(
                 structureContexts,
                 newContext
            )

        // ====================================================================
        // UPDATE EXISTING CONTEXT
        // ====================================================================

        else

            StructureContextObject existingContext = array.get(
                 structureContexts,
                 contextIndex
            )

            existingContext := f_updateStructureContextFromNode(
                 existingContext,
                 structureNode
            )

            array.set(
                 structureContexts,
                 contextIndex,
                 existingContext
            )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

const int CORE_MAX_STRUCTURE_CONTEXTS = 32

while array.size(structureContexts) > CORE_MAX_STRUCTURE_CONTEXTS

    array.shift(
         structureContexts
    )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int structureContextCount = array.size(
     structureContexts
)

int externalStructureContextCount = 0

int internalStructureContextCount = 0

int microStructureContextCount = 0

if barstate.islast and coreDebug and structureContextCount > 0

    for i = 0 to structureContextCount - 1

        StructureContextObject debugContext = array.get(
             structureContexts,
             i
        )

        if debugContext.degree == SwingDegree.External

            externalStructureContextCount += 1

        else if debugContext.degree == SwingDegree.Internal

            internalStructureContextCount += 1

        else if debugContext.degree == SwingDegree.Micro

            microStructureContextCount += 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(structureContextCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG STRUCTURE CONTEXT COUNT",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(externalStructureContextCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG EXTERNAL STRUCTURE CONTEXT",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(internalStructureContextCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG INTERNAL STRUCTURE CONTEXT",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(microStructureContextCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG MICRO STRUCTURE CONTEXT",
     display = display.data_window

string grpStructureQuality = "03 MARKET STRUCTURE / QUALITY"

int structureQualityATRLength = input.int(
     14,
     "Structure Quality ATR Length",
     minval = 1,
     group = grpStructureQuality
)

float structureFullBreakATR = input.float(
     0.15,
     "Full Break Score at ATR Fraction",
     minval = 0.01,
     maxval = 2.0,
     step = 0.01,
     group = grpStructureQuality
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

float structureQualityATR = ta.atr(
     structureQualityATRLength
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_structureEventTypeToken(
     StructureEventType eventType
     ) =>

    string result = "STRUCT"

    if eventType == StructureEventType.BOS
        result := "BOS"

    else if eventType == StructureEventType.CHOCH
        result := "CHOCH"

    else if eventType == StructureEventType.MSS
        result := "MSS"

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_breakBasisToken(
     BreakBasis basis
     ) =>

    string result = "BREAK"

    if basis == BreakBasis.Wick
        result := "WICK"

    else if basis == BreakBasis.Close
        result := "CLOSE"

    else if basis == BreakBasis.Combined
        result := "COMB"

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_classifyStructureEventType(
     Direction currentBias,
     Direction breakDirection
     ) =>

    StructureEventType result = StructureEventType.BOS

    if currentBias == Direction.Neutral

        result := StructureEventType.BOS

    else if currentBias == breakDirection

        result := StructureEventType.BOS

    else

        result := StructureEventType.CHOCH

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getBullishStructureBreak(
     float level
     ) =>

    bool broken = false

    BreakBasis basis = BreakBasis.Wick

    float breakDistance = 0.0

    if not na(level)

        bool wickBreak = high > level

        bool closeBreak = close > level

        if structureBreakMode == "Wick"

            if wickBreak

                broken := true

                basis := BreakBasis.Wick

                breakDistance := math.max(
                     high - level,
                     0.0
                )

        else if structureBreakMode == "Close"

            if closeBreak

                broken := true

                basis := BreakBasis.Close

                breakDistance := math.max(
                     close - level,
                     0.0
                )

        else

            if closeBreak

                broken := true

                basis := BreakBasis.Combined

                breakDistance := math.max(
                     close - level,
                     0.0
                )

            else if wickBreak

                broken := true

                basis := BreakBasis.Wick

                breakDistance := math.max(
                     high - level,
                     0.0
                )

    [broken, basis, breakDistance]

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getBearishStructureBreak(
     float level
     ) =>

    bool broken = false

    BreakBasis basis = BreakBasis.Wick

    float breakDistance = 0.0

    if not na(level)

        bool wickBreak = low < level

        bool closeBreak = close < level

        if structureBreakMode == "Wick"

            if wickBreak

                broken := true

                basis := BreakBasis.Wick

                breakDistance := math.max(
                     level - low,
                     0.0
                )

        else if structureBreakMode == "Close"

            if closeBreak

                broken := true

                basis := BreakBasis.Close

                breakDistance := math.max(
                     level - close,
                     0.0
                )

        else

            if closeBreak

                broken := true

                basis := BreakBasis.Combined

                breakDistance := math.max(
                     level - close,
                     0.0
                )

            else if wickBreak

                broken := true

                basis := BreakBasis.Wick

                breakDistance := math.max(
                     level - low,
                     0.0
                )

    [broken, basis, breakDistance]

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getStructureBreakBasisScore(
     BreakBasis basis
     ) =>

    float result = 65.0

    if basis == BreakBasis.Wick
        result := 65.0

    else if basis == BreakBasis.Close
        result := 90.0

    else if basis == BreakBasis.Combined
        result := 100.0

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getStructureBreakDistanceScore(
     float breakDistance,
     float atrValue
     ) =>

    float result = 0.0

    if breakDistance > 0.0

        if not na(atrValue) and atrValue > 0.0

            float targetDistance = atrValue * structureFullBreakATR

            if targetDistance > 0.0

                result := math.min(
                     breakDistance / targetDistance * 100.0,
                     100.0
                )

        else

            float fallbackDistance = syminfo.mintick * 10.0

            if fallbackDistance > 0.0

                result := math.min(
                     breakDistance / fallbackDistance * 100.0,
                     100.0
                )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_findBaseLiquidityIndexBySourceSwingId(
     string sourceSwingId,
     LiquidityKind expectedKind
     ) =>

    int result = -1

    int storageSize = array.size(
         liquidityObjects
    )

    if storageSize > 0 and sourceSwingId != ""

        for offset = 0 to storageSize - 1

            int i = storageSize - 1 - offset

            LiquidityObject liquidityObject = array.get(
                 liquidityObjects,
                 i
            )

            bool correctSource = liquidityObject.sourceSwingId == sourceSwingId

            bool correctOrigin = liquidityObject.origin == LiquidityOrigin.Swing

            bool correctKind = liquidityObject.kind == expectedKind

            if correctSource and correctOrigin and correctKind

                result := i

                break

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getBaseLiquidityIdForBrokenSwing(
     string swingId,
     Direction breakDirection
     ) =>

    string result = ""

    LiquidityKind expectedKind = LiquidityKind.BSL

    if breakDirection == Direction.Bullish

        expectedKind := LiquidityKind.BSL

    else if breakDirection == Direction.Bearish

        expectedKind := LiquidityKind.SSL

    int liquidityIndex = f_findBaseLiquidityIndexBySourceSwingId(
         swingId,
         expectedKind
    )

    if liquidityIndex >= 0

        LiquidityObject liquidityObject = array.get(
             liquidityObjects,
             liquidityIndex
        )

        result := liquidityObject.meta.logicalId

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getValidProtectedOriginSwingId(
     string brokenSwingId,
     SwingSide expectedSide,
     string originTf,
     SwingDegree degree
     ) =>

    string result = ""

    string candidateId = f_getStructuralLegOriginSwingId(
         brokenSwingId
    )

    if candidateId != "" and map.contains(
         swingObjectById,
         candidateId
    )

        SwingObject candidateSwing = map.get(
             swingObjectById,
             candidateId
        )

        bool correctSide = candidateSwing.side == expectedSide

        bool correctTf = candidateSwing.meta.originTf == originTf

        bool correctDegree = candidateSwing.degree == degree

        if correctSide and correctTf and correctDegree

            result := candidateId

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_findRelevantValidatedSweepEventId(
     Direction structureDirection,
     string originTf,
     SwingDegree degree,
     int minEventTime
     ) =>

    string result = ""

    int eventCount = array.size(
         sweepEvents
    )

    if eventCount > 0

        for offset = 0 to eventCount - 1

            int i = eventCount - 1 - offset

            SweepEventObject event = array.get(
                 sweepEvents,
                 i
            )

            bool validated = event.state == SweepLifecycle.Validated

            bool sameDirection = event.direction == structureDirection

            bool sameTf = event.meta.originTf == originTf

            bool sameDegree = event.sourceDegree == degree

            bool afterLegOrigin = na(minEventTime) or event.lastEventTime >= minEventTime

            bool notFuture = event.lastEventTime <= time_close

            bool validEvent = validated and sameDirection and sameTf and sameDegree and afterLegOrigin and notFuture

            if validEvent

                result := event.meta.logicalId

                break

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getSweepEventQualityById(
     string eventId
     ) =>

    float result = 0.0

    if eventId != ""

        int eventCount = array.size(
             sweepEvents
        )

        if eventCount > 0

            for offset = 0 to eventCount - 1

                int i = eventCount - 1 - offset

                SweepEventObject event = array.get(
                     sweepEvents,
                     i
                )

                if event.meta.logicalId == eventId

                    result := event.quality

                    break

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getLatestContextStructureEventId(
     StructureContextObject context
     ) =>

    string result = ""

    int latestTime = na

    if context.lastBosEventId != "" and map.contains(
         structureEventById,
         context.lastBosEventId
    )

        StructureEventObject bosEvent = map.get(
             structureEventById,
             context.lastBosEventId
        )

        latestTime := bosEvent.meta.confirmationTime

        result := bosEvent.meta.logicalId

    if context.lastChochEventId != "" and map.contains(
         structureEventById,
         context.lastChochEventId
    )

        StructureEventObject chochEvent = map.get(
             structureEventById,
             context.lastChochEventId
        )

        int chochTime = chochEvent.meta.confirmationTime

        if na(latestTime) or chochTime > latestTime

            latestTime := chochTime

            result := chochEvent.meta.logicalId

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getStructureEventQuality(
     SwingObject brokenSwing,
     BreakBasis breakBasis,
     float breakDistance,
     float atrValue,
     float sweepQuality
     ) =>

    float authorityScore = math.min(
         math.max(brokenSwing.meta.authority, 0.0),
         100.0
    )

    float swingQualityScore = math.min(
         math.max(brokenSwing.meta.quality, 0.0),
         100.0
    )

    float distanceScore = f_getStructureBreakDistanceScore(
         breakDistance,
         atrValue
    )

    float basisScore = f_getStructureBreakBasisScore(
         breakBasis
    )

    float normalizedSweepQuality = math.min(
         math.max(sweepQuality, 0.0),
         100.0
    )

    float result = authorityScore * 0.30

    result += swingQualityScore * 0.20

    result += distanceScore * 0.25

    result += basisScore * 0.15

    result += normalizedSweepQuality * 0.10

    result := math.min(
         math.max(result, 0.0),
         100.0
    )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_makeStructureEventLogicalId(
     string originTf,
     SwingDegree degree,
     StructureEventType eventType,
     string brokenSwingId,
     int eventTime
     ) =>

    string result = "STRUCT_EVT|" + syminfo.tickerid + "|" + originTf + "|" + f_swingDegreeToken(degree) + "|" + f_structureEventTypeToken(eventType) + "|" + brokenSwingId + "|" + str.tostring(eventTime)

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_newStructureEvent(
     StructureContextObject context,
     SwingObject brokenSwing,
     StructureEventType eventType,
     Direction direction,
     BreakBasis breakBasis,
     float breakDistance,
     string protectedSwingId,
     float protectedPrice,
     int protectedTime,
     string sourceLiquidityId,
     string linkedSweepEventId,
     string previousStructureEventId
     ) =>

    string subtype = f_structureEventTypeToken(eventType) + "_" + f_swingDegreeToken(context.degree) + "_" + f_breakBasisToken(breakBasis)

    ObjectMeta eventMeta = f_newMeta(
         "STRUCTURE_EVENT",
         subtype,
         context.meta.originTf,
         time,
         brokenSwing.price
    )

    eventMeta.logicalId := f_makeStructureEventLogicalId(
         context.meta.originTf,
         context.degree,
         eventType,
         brokenSwing.meta.logicalId,
         time_close
    )

    float linkedSweepQuality = f_getSweepEventQualityById(
         linkedSweepEventId
    )

    float eventQuality = f_getStructureEventQuality(
         brokenSwing,
         breakBasis,
         breakDistance,
         structureQualityATR,
         linkedSweepQuality
    )

    float eventAuthority = brokenSwing.meta.authority

    eventMeta.confirmationTime := time_close

    eventMeta.lastUpdateTime := time_close

    eventMeta.authority := eventAuthority

    eventMeta.quality := eventQuality

    eventMeta.confluence := linkedSweepQuality

    eventMeta.confirmed := true

    eventMeta.archived := false

    bool sweepLinked = linkedSweepEventId != ""

    StructureEventObject result = StructureEventObject.new(
         eventMeta,
         eventType,
         EventLifecycle.Confirmed,
         direction,
         context.degree,
         breakBasis,
         brokenSwing.meta.logicalId,
         brokenSwing.price,
         brokenSwing.pivotTime,
         protectedSwingId,
         protectedPrice,
         protectedTime,
         sourceLiquidityId,
         linkedSweepEventId,
         "",
         previousStructureEventId,
         breakDistance,
         eventAuthority,
         eventQuality,
         sweepLinked,
         false,
         false
    )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_trimStructureEventHistory() =>

    bool trimmed = false

    while array.size(structureEvents) > CORE_MAX_SWINGS

        StructureEventObject removedEvent = array.shift(
             structureEvents
        )

        if map.contains(
             structureEventById,
             removedEvent.meta.logicalId
        )

            map.remove(
                 structureEventById,
                 removedEvent.meta.logicalId
            )

        trimmed := true

    trimmed

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if enableMarketStructureEngine and barstate.isconfirmed

    int structureContextStorageSize = array.size(
         structureContexts
    )

    if structureContextStorageSize > 0

        // ====================================================================
        // ====================================================================

        int maxContextsThisBar = math.min(
             structureContextStorageSize,
             32
        )

        int contextBudgetUsed = 0

        for contextIndex = 0 to maxContextsThisBar - 1

            StructureContextObject context = array.get(
                 structureContexts,
                 contextIndex
            )

            bool degreeEnabled = f_structureDegreeEnabled(
                 context.degree
            )

            if degreeEnabled and context.initialized

                contextBudgetUsed += 1

                // =============================================================
                // =============================================================

                bool validHighSwing = context.latestHighSwingId != "" and map.contains(
                    swingObjectById,
                    context.latestHighSwingId
                )

                bool validLowSwing = context.latestLowSwingId != "" and map.contains(
                    swingObjectById,
                    context.latestLowSwingId
                )

                SwingObject highSwing = na

                SwingObject lowSwing = na

                if validHighSwing

                    highSwing := map.get(
                        swingObjectById,
                        context.latestHighSwingId
                    )

                if validLowSwing

                    lowSwing := map.get(
                        swingObjectById,
                        context.latestLowSwingId
                    )

                // =============================================================
                // =============================================================

                bool highCanBreak = false

                bool lowCanBreak = false

                if validHighSwing

                    int highConfirmationTime = highSwing.meta.confirmationTime

                    highCanBreak := na(highConfirmationTime) ? time > highSwing.pivotTime : time_close > highConfirmationTime

                if validLowSwing

                    int lowConfirmationTime = lowSwing.meta.confirmationTime

                    lowCanBreak := na(lowConfirmationTime) ? time > lowSwing.pivotTime : time_close > lowConfirmationTime

                // =============================================================
                // DUPLICATE BREAK SUPPRESSION
                // =============================================================

                bool highNotAlreadyBroken = context.latestHighSwingId != "" and context.lastBrokenHighSwingId != context.latestHighSwingId

                bool lowNotAlreadyBroken = context.latestLowSwingId != "" and context.lastBrokenLowSwingId != context.latestLowSwingId

                // =============================================================
                // BREAK SIGNALS
                // =============================================================

                bool bullishBreak = false

                BreakBasis bullishBreakBasis = BreakBasis.Wick

                float bullishBreakDistance = 0.0

                bool bearishBreak = false

                BreakBasis bearishBreakBasis = BreakBasis.Wick

                float bearishBreakDistance = 0.0

                if validHighSwing and highCanBreak and highNotAlreadyBroken

                    [detectedBullishBreak, detectedBullishBasis, detectedBullishDistance] = f_getBullishStructureBreak(context.latestHighPrice)

                    bullishBreak := detectedBullishBreak

                    bullishBreakBasis := detectedBullishBasis

                    bullishBreakDistance := detectedBullishDistance

                if validLowSwing and lowCanBreak and lowNotAlreadyBroken

                    [detectedBearishBreak, detectedBearishBasis, detectedBearishDistance] = f_getBearishStructureBreak(context.latestLowPrice)

                    bearishBreak := detectedBearishBreak

                    bearishBreakBasis := detectedBearishBasis

                    bearishBreakDistance := detectedBearishDistance

                // =============================================================
                // =============================================================

                if bullishBreak and bearishBreak

                    bool bullishCloseBased = bullishBreakBasis == BreakBasis.Close or bullishBreakBasis == BreakBasis.Combined

                    bool bearishCloseBased = bearishBreakBasis == BreakBasis.Close or bearishBreakBasis == BreakBasis.Combined

                    if bullishCloseBased and not bearishCloseBased

                        bearishBreak := false

                    else if bearishCloseBased and not bullishCloseBased

                        bullishBreak := false

                    else if bullishBreakDistance > bearishBreakDistance

                        bearishBreak := false

                    else if bearishBreakDistance > bullishBreakDistance

                        bullishBreak := false

                    else

                        if close >= open

                            bearishBreak := false

                        else

                            bullishBreak := false

                // =============================================================
                // BULLISH STRUCTURE BREAK
                // =============================================================

                if bullishBreak

                    Direction breakDirection = Direction.Bullish

                    StructureEventType eventType = f_classifyStructureEventType(
                         context.bias,
                         breakDirection
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------

                    string protectedSwingId = f_getValidProtectedOriginSwingId(
                         highSwing.meta.logicalId,
                         SwingSide.Low,
                         context.meta.originTf,
                         context.degree
                    )

                    float protectedPrice = f_getSwingPriceById(
                         protectedSwingId
                    )

                    int protectedTime = f_getSwingTimeById(
                         protectedSwingId
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------
                    // Base BSL belonging to broken High.
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------

                    string sourceLiquidityId = f_getBaseLiquidityIdForBrokenSwing(
                         highSwing.meta.logicalId,
                         breakDirection
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------

                    string linkedSweepEventId = f_findRelevantValidatedSweepEventId(
                         breakDirection,
                         context.meta.originTf,
                         context.degree,
                         protectedTime
                    )

                    string previousStructureEventId = f_getLatestContextStructureEventId(
                         context
                    )

                    StructureEventObject structureEvent = f_newStructureEvent(
                         context,
                         highSwing,
                         eventType,
                         breakDirection,
                         bullishBreakBasis,
                         bullishBreakDistance,
                         protectedSwingId,
                         protectedPrice,
                         protectedTime,
                         sourceLiquidityId,
                         linkedSweepEventId,
                         previousStructureEventId
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------
                    // Event database
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------

                    array.push(
                         structureEvents,
                         structureEvent
                    )

                    map.put(
                         structureEventById,
                         structureEvent.meta.logicalId,
                         structureEvent
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Phase 4C
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

                    array.push(
                        structureLifecycleQueue,
                        structureEvent
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Phase 4D
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

                    array.push(
                        structureGovernanceQueue,
                        structureEvent
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------
                    // Context update
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------

                    context.lastBrokenHighSwingId := highSwing.meta.logicalId

                    context.bias := Direction.Bullish

                    context.protectedLowSwingId := protectedSwingId

                    context.protectedLowPrice := protectedPrice

                    context.protectedLowTime := protectedTime

                    // Current bullish structure has a Protected Low.
                    //
                    // Old opposite-side protection is no longer CURRENT
                    // structural protection. Historical Event retains it.

                    context.protectedHighSwingId := ""

                    context.protectedHighPrice := na

                    context.protectedHighTime := na

                    context.lastStructureEventTime := time_close

                    if eventType == StructureEventType.BOS

                        context.lastBosEventId := structureEvent.meta.logicalId

                    else if eventType == StructureEventType.CHOCH

                        context.lastChochEventId := structureEvent.meta.logicalId

                        context.pendingChochEventId := structureEvent.meta.logicalId

                    ObjectMeta contextMeta = context.meta

                    contextMeta.lastUpdateTime := time_close

                    context.meta := contextMeta

                    array.set(
                         structureContexts,
                         contextIndex,
                         context
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Phase 4C
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

                    array.push(
                        structureLifecycleQueue,
                        structureEvent
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Phase 4D
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

                    array.push(
                        structureGovernanceQueue,
                        structureEvent
                    )

                // =============================================================
                // BEARISH STRUCTURE BREAK
                // =============================================================

                else if bearishBreak

                    Direction breakDirection = Direction.Bearish

                    StructureEventType eventType = f_classifyStructureEventType(
                         context.bias,
                         breakDirection
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------

                    string protectedSwingId = f_getValidProtectedOriginSwingId(
                         lowSwing.meta.logicalId,
                         SwingSide.High,
                         context.meta.originTf,
                         context.degree
                    )

                    float protectedPrice = f_getSwingPriceById(
                         protectedSwingId
                    )

                    int protectedTime = f_getSwingTimeById(
                         protectedSwingId
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------
                    // Base SSL belonging to broken Low.
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------

                    string sourceLiquidityId = f_getBaseLiquidityIdForBrokenSwing(
                         lowSwing.meta.logicalId,
                         breakDirection
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------

                    string linkedSweepEventId = f_findRelevantValidatedSweepEventId(
                         breakDirection,
                         context.meta.originTf,
                         context.degree,
                         protectedTime
                    )

                    string previousStructureEventId = f_getLatestContextStructureEventId(
                         context
                    )

                    StructureEventObject structureEvent = f_newStructureEvent(
                         context,
                         lowSwing,
                         eventType,
                         breakDirection,
                         bearishBreakBasis,
                         bearishBreakDistance,
                         protectedSwingId,
                         protectedPrice,
                         protectedTime,
                         sourceLiquidityId,
                         linkedSweepEventId,
                         previousStructureEventId
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------
                    // Event database
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------

                    array.push(
                        structureEvents,
                        structureEvent
                    )

                    map.put(
                        structureEventById,
                        structureEvent.meta.logicalId,
                        structureEvent
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Phase 4C
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

                    array.push(
                        structureLifecycleQueue,
                        structureEvent
                    )

                    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
                    // Phase 4D
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

                    array.push(
                        structureGovernanceQueue,
                        structureEvent
                    )
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------
                    // Context update
                    // ---------------------------------------------------------

                    context.lastBrokenLowSwingId := lowSwing.meta.logicalId

                    context.bias := Direction.Bearish

                    context.protectedHighSwingId := protectedSwingId

                    context.protectedHighPrice := protectedPrice

                    context.protectedHighTime := protectedTime

                    context.protectedLowSwingId := ""

                    context.protectedLowPrice := na

                    context.protectedLowTime := na

                    context.lastStructureEventTime := time_close

                    if eventType == StructureEventType.BOS

                        context.lastBosEventId := structureEvent.meta.logicalId

                    else if eventType == StructureEventType.CHOCH

                        context.lastChochEventId := structureEvent.meta.logicalId

                        context.pendingChochEventId := structureEvent.meta.logicalId

                    ObjectMeta contextMeta = context.meta

                    contextMeta.lastUpdateTime := time_close

                    context.meta := contextMeta

                    array.set(
                         structureContexts,
                         contextIndex,
                         context
                    )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_trimStructureEventHistory()

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int structureEventCount = array.size(
     structureEvents
)

int bosCount = 0

int chochCount = 0

int bullishBosCount = 0

int bearishBosCount = 0

int bullishChochCount = 0

int bearishChochCount = 0

int sweepLinkedStructureCount = 0

if barstate.islast and coreDebug and structureEventCount > 0

    for i = 0 to structureEventCount - 1

        StructureEventObject debugEvent = array.get(
             structureEvents,
             i
        )

        if debugEvent.eventType == StructureEventType.BOS

            bosCount += 1

            if debugEvent.direction == Direction.Bullish

                bullishBosCount += 1

            else if debugEvent.direction == Direction.Bearish

                bearishBosCount += 1

        else if debugEvent.eventType == StructureEventType.CHOCH

            chochCount += 1

            if debugEvent.direction == Direction.Bullish

                bullishChochCount += 1

            else if debugEvent.direction == Direction.Bearish

                bearishChochCount += 1

        if debugEvent.sweepLinked

            sweepLinkedStructureCount += 1

// ============================================================================

plot(

)

plot(

     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(bullishBosCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG BULLISH BOS",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(bearishBosCount) : na,

)

plot(

plot(

plot(
