f_newStructuralSwingState(
     SwingObject swingObject
     ) =>

    StructuralSwingStateObject result = StructuralSwingStateObject.new(
         swingObject.meta.logicalId,
         swingObject.meta.originTf,
         swingObject.degree,
         swingObject.side,
         swingObject.price,
         swingObject.pivotTime,
         StructuralSwingLifecycle.Active,
         "",
         "",
         "",
         na,
         na,
         na,
         time_close
    )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_ensureStructuralSwingState(
     string swingId
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    if swingId != ""

        if map.contains(
             structuralSwingStateById,
             swingId
        )

            result := true

        else if map.contains(
             swingObjectById,
             swingId
        )

            SwingObject swingObject = map.get(
                 swingObjectById,
                 swingId
            )

            StructuralSwingStateObject newState = f_newStructuralSwingState(
                 swingObject
            )

            array.push(
                 structuralSwingStates,
                 newState
            )

            map.put(
                 structuralSwingStateById,
                 swingId,
                 newState
            )

            result := true

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_markStructuralSwingProtected(
     string swingId,
     string structureEventId
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    if f_ensureStructuralSwingState(
         swingId
    )

        StructuralSwingStateObject swingState = map.get(
             structuralSwingStateById,
             swingId
        )

        // Broken Swing must never become Protected again through old history.

        if swingState.lifecycle != StructuralSwingLifecycle.Broken

            swingState.lifecycle := StructuralSwingLifecycle.Protected

            swingState.protectedByEventId := structureEventId

            swingState.protectedTime := time_close

            swingState.lastUpdateTime := time_close

            result := true

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_markStructuralSwingBroken(
     string swingId,
     string structureEventId
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    if f_ensureStructuralSwingState(
         swingId
    )

        StructuralSwingStateObject swingState = map.get(
             structuralSwingStateById,
             swingId
        )

        swingState.lifecycle := StructuralSwingLifecycle.Broken

        swingState.brokenByEventId := structureEventId

        swingState.brokenTime := time_close

        swingState.lastUpdateTime := time_close

        result := true

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_markStructuralSwingConsumed(
     string swingId,
     string structureEventId
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    if f_ensureStructuralSwingState(
         swingId
    )

        StructuralSwingStateObject swingState = map.get(
             structuralSwingStateById,
             swingId
        )

        // Never overwrite actual structural break history.

        if swingState.lifecycle != StructuralSwingLifecycle.Broken

            swingState.lifecycle := StructuralSwingLifecycle.Consumed

            swingState.consumedByEventId := structureEventId

            swingState.consumedTime := time_close

            swingState.lastUpdateTime := time_close

            result := true

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getStructureContextIdFromEvent(
     StructureEventObject structureEvent
     ) =>

    string result = f_makeStructureContextLogicalId(
         structureEvent.meta.originTf,
         structureEvent.degree
    )

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_linkValidatedSweepToStructureByEventId(
     string sweepEventId,
     string structureEventId,
     float structureScore
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    if sweepEventId != ""

        int eventCount = array.size(
             sweepEvents
        )

        if eventCount > 0

            for offset = 0 to eventCount - 1

                int i = eventCount - 1 - offset

                SweepEventObject sweepEvent = array.get(
                     sweepEvents,
                     i
                )

                if sweepEvent.meta.logicalId == sweepEventId

                    sweepEvent.linkedStructureEventId := structureEventId

                    sweepEvent.structureResponseScore := math.min(
                         math.max(structureScore, 0.0),
                         100.0
                    )

                    sweepEvent := f_recalculateSweepEventQuality(
                         sweepEvent
                    )

                    result := true

                    break

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(structureLifecycleQueue) > 0

    StructureEventObject lifecycleEvent = array.shift(
         structureLifecycleQueue
    )

    string contextId = f_getStructureContextIdFromEvent(
         lifecycleEvent
    )

    string brokenSwingId = lifecycleEvent.brokenSwingId

    string newProtectedSwingId = lifecycleEvent.protectedSwingId

    // ========================================================================
    // CURRENT PREVIOUS PROTECTION
    // ========================================================================

    string previousProtectedSwingId = ""

    if map.contains(
         currentProtectedSwingByContextId,
         contextId
    )

        previousProtectedSwingId := map.get(
             currentProtectedSwingByContextId,
             contextId
        )

    // ========================================================================
    // BROKEN STRUCTURAL SWING
    // ========================================================================

    if brokenSwingId != ""

        f_markStructuralSwingBroken(
             brokenSwingId,
             lifecycleEvent.meta.logicalId
        )

    // ========================================================================
    // ========================================================================

    if previousProtectedSwingId != "" and previousProtectedSwingId != newProtectedSwingId

        if previousProtectedSwingId != brokenSwingId

            f_markStructuralSwingConsumed(
                 previousProtectedSwingId,
                 lifecycleEvent.meta.logicalId
            )

    // ========================================================================
    // NEW PROTECTED SWING
    // ========================================================================

    if newProtectedSwingId != ""

        f_markStructuralSwingProtected(
             newProtectedSwingId,
             lifecycleEvent.meta.logicalId
        )

        map.put(
             currentProtectedSwingByContextId,
             contextId,
             newProtectedSwingId
        )

    else

        // No valid new structural protection exists.

        if map.contains(
             currentProtectedSwingByContextId,
             contextId
        )

            map.remove(
                 currentProtectedSwingByContextId,
                 contextId
            )

    // ========================================================================
    // COMPLETE BIDIRECTIONAL SWEEP LINKAGE
    // ========================================================================

    if lifecycleEvent.linkedSweepEventId != ""

        f_linkValidatedSweepToStructureByEventId(
             lifecycleEvent.linkedSweepEventId,
             lifecycleEvent.meta.logicalId,
             lifecycleEvent.eventQuality
        )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(structuralSwingStates) > CORE_MAX_SWINGS

    StructuralSwingStateObject removedState = array.shift(
         structuralSwingStates
    )

    if map.contains(
         structuralSwingStateById,
         removedState.swingId
    )

        map.remove(
             structuralSwingStateById,
             removedState.swingId
        )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int structureProtectionIntegrityViolations = 0

int missingStructureProtectionCount = 0

if barstate.islast and coreDebug

    int contextCount = array.size(
         structureContexts
    )

    if contextCount > 0

        for i = 0 to contextCount - 1

            StructureContextObject context = array.get(
                 structureContexts,
                 i
            )

            // =================================================================
            // BULLISH
            // =================================================================

            if context.bias == Direction.Bullish

                if context.protectedLowSwingId == ""

                    missingStructureProtectionCount += 1

                else

                    int protectedIndex = f_findSwingIndexById(
                         swingObjects,
                         context.protectedLowSwingId
                    )

                    if protectedIndex >= 0

                        SwingObject protectedSwing = array.get(
                             swingObjects,
                             protectedIndex
                        )

                        bool correctSide = protectedSwing.side == SwingSide.Low

                        bool correctTf = protectedSwing.meta.originTf == context.meta.originTf

                        bool correctDegree = protectedSwing.degree == context.degree

                        if not correctSide or not correctTf or not correctDegree

                            structureProtectionIntegrityViolations += 1

            // =================================================================
            // BEARISH
            // =================================================================

            else if context.bias == Direction.Bearish

                if context.protectedHighSwingId == ""

                    missingStructureProtectionCount += 1

                else

                    int protectedIndex = f_findSwingIndexById(
                         swingObjects,
                         context.protectedHighSwingId
                    )

                    if protectedIndex >= 0

                        SwingObject protectedSwing = array.get(
                             swingObjects,
                             protectedIndex
                        )

                        bool correctSide = protectedSwing.side == SwingSide.High

                        bool correctTf = protectedSwing.meta.originTf == context.meta.originTf

                        bool correctDegree = protectedSwing.degree == context.degree

                        if not correctSide or not correctTf or not correctDegree

                            structureProtectionIntegrityViolations += 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int structureLineageOrderViolations = 0

int structureLineageMissingReferences = 0

if barstate.islast and coreDebug

    int eventCount = array.size(
         structureEvents
    )

    if eventCount > 0

        for i = 0 to eventCount - 1

            StructureEventObject currentEvent = array.get(
                 structureEvents,
                 i
            )

            if currentEvent.previousStructureEventId != ""

                if map.contains(
                     structureEventById,
                     currentEvent.previousStructureEventId
                )

                    StructureEventObject previousEvent = map.get(
                         structureEventById,
                         currentEvent.previousStructureEventId
                    )

                    if previousEvent.meta.confirmationTime > currentEvent.meta.confirmationTime

                        structureLineageOrderViolations += 1

                else

                    // Can legitimately occur after old history was trimmed.
                    // Therefore track separately instead of treating it as
                    // a logical failure.

                    structureLineageMissingReferences += 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int pendingChochCount = 0

int invalidPendingChochCount = 0

if barstate.islast and coreDebug

    int contextCount = array.size(
         structureContexts
    )

    if contextCount > 0

        for i = 0 to contextCount - 1

            StructureContextObject context = array.get(
                 structureContexts,
                 i
            )

            if context.pendingChochEventId != ""

                pendingChochCount += 1

                if map.contains(
                     structureEventById,
                     context.pendingChochEventId
                )

                    StructureEventObject pendingEvent = map.get(
                         structureEventById,
                         context.pendingChochEventId
                    )

                    bool correctType = pendingEvent.eventType == StructureEventType.CHOCH

                    bool correctTf = pendingEvent.meta.originTf == context.meta.originTf

                    bool correctDegree = pendingEvent.degree == context.degree

                    bool correctDirection = pendingEvent.direction == context.bias

                    if not correctType or not correctTf or not correctDegree or not correctDirection

                        invalidPendingChochCount += 1

                else

                    invalidPendingChochCount += 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

var array<line> structureDevEventLines = array.new<line>()

var array<label> structureDevEventLabels = array.new<label>()

var array<line> structureDevProtectedLines = array.new<line>()

var array<label> structureDevProtectedLabels = array.new<label>()

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_clearStructureDevDrawings() =>

    while array.size(structureDevEventLines) > 0

        line oldLine = array.pop(
             structureDevEventLines
        )

        if not na(oldLine)
            line.delete(oldLine)

    while array.size(structureDevEventLabels) > 0

        label oldLabel = array.pop(
             structureDevEventLabels
        )

        if not na(oldLabel)
            label.delete(oldLabel)

    while array.size(structureDevProtectedLines) > 0

        line oldLine = array.pop(
             structureDevProtectedLines
        )

        if not na(oldLine)
            line.delete(oldLine)

    while array.size(structureDevProtectedLabels) > 0

        label oldLabel = array.pop(
             structureDevProtectedLabels
        )

        if not na(oldLabel)
            label.delete(oldLabel)

    true

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if barstate.islast

    f_clearStructureDevDrawings()

    // ========================================================================
    // BOS / CHOCH
    // ========================================================================

    if structureDevShowEvents

        int totalEvents = array.size(
             structureEvents
        )

        int eventIndex = totalEvents - 1

        int renderedEvents = 0

        while eventIndex >= 0 and renderedEvents < structureDevEventLimit

            StructureEventObject structureEvent = array.get(
                 structureEvents,
                 eventIndex
            )

            int eventTime = structureEvent.meta.confirmationTime

            int swingTime = structureEvent.brokenSwingTime

            bool validCoordinates = not na(eventTime) and not na(swingTime) and not na(structureEvent.breakLevel)

            if validCoordinates

                color eventColor = color.gray

                if structureEvent.direction == Direction.Bullish
                    eventColor := color.lime

                else if structureEvent.direction == Direction.Bearish
                    eventColor := color.red

                line eventLine = line.new(
                     x1 = swingTime,
                     y1 = structureEvent.breakLevel,
                     x2 = eventTime,
                     y2 = structureEvent.breakLevel,
                     xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                     extend = extend.none,
                     color = eventColor,
                     width = 1,
                     style = line.style_dashed
                )

                array.push(
                     structureDevEventLines,
                     eventLine
                )

                int midpointTime = swingTime + int(
                     (eventTime - swingTime) / 2
                )

                string eventText = f_structureEventTypeToken(
                     structureEvent.eventType
                )

                eventText += " | "

                eventText += f_swingDegreeToken(
                     structureEvent.degree
                )

                label eventLabel = label.new(
                     x = midpointTime,
                     y = structureEvent.breakLevel,
                     text = eventText,
                     xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                     yloc = yloc.price,
                     style = label.style_none,
                     textcolor = eventColor,
                     size = size.tiny
                )

                array.push(
                     structureDevEventLabels,
                     eventLabel
                )

                renderedEvents += 1

            eventIndex -= 1

    // ========================================================================
    // CURRENT PROTECTED HIGH / LOW
    // ========================================================================

    if structureDevShowProtectedLevels

        int contextCount = array.size(
             structureContexts
        )

        if contextCount > 0

            for i = 0 to contextCount - 1

                StructureContextObject context = array.get(
                     structureContexts,
                     i
                )

                // =============================================================
                // BULLISH -> PROTECTED LOW
                // =============================================================

                if context.bias == Direction.Bullish and context.protectedLowSwingId != "" and not na(context.protectedLowPrice)

                    int startTime = context.protectedLowTime

                    if na(startTime)
                        startTime := time

                    line protectedLine = line.new(
                         x1 = startTime,
                         y1 = context.protectedLowPrice,
                         x2 = time,
                         y2 = context.protectedLowPrice,
                         xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                         extend = extend.right,
                         color = color.lime,
                         width = 2,
                         style = line.style_solid
                    )

                    array.push(
                         structureDevProtectedLines,
                         protectedLine
                    )

                    string protectedText = "PL | " + f_swingDegreeToken(
                         context.degree
                    )

                    label protectedLabel = label.new(
                         x = time,
                         y = context.protectedLowPrice,
                         text = protectedText,
                         xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                         yloc = yloc.price,
                         style = label.style_label_left,
                         color = color.new(color.lime, 65),
                         textcolor = color.white,
                         size = size.tiny
                    )

                    array.push(
                         structureDevProtectedLabels,
                         protectedLabel
                    )

                // =============================================================
                // BEARISH -> PROTECTED HIGH
                // =============================================================

                else if context.bias == Direction.Bearish and context.protectedHighSwingId != "" and not na(context.protectedHighPrice)

                    int startTime = context.protectedHighTime

                    if na(startTime)
                        startTime := time

                    line protectedLine = line.new(
                         x1 = startTime,
                         y1 = context.protectedHighPrice,
                         x2 = time,
                         y2 = context.protectedHighPrice,
                         xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                         extend = extend.right,
                         color = color.red,
                         width = 2,
                         style = line.style_solid
                    )

                    array.push(
                         structureDevProtectedLines,
                         protectedLine
                    )

                    string protectedText = "PH | " + f_swingDegreeToken(
                         context.degree
                    )

                    label protectedLabel = label.new(
                         x = time,
                         y = context.protectedHighPrice,
                         text = protectedText,
                         xloc = xloc.bar_time,
                         yloc = yloc.price,
                         style = label.style_label_left,
                         color = color.new(color.red, 65),
                         textcolor = color.white,
                         size = size.tiny
                    )

                    array.push(
                         structureDevProtectedLabels,
                         protectedLabel
                    )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int protectedStructuralSwingCount = 0

int brokenStructuralSwingCount = 0

int consumedStructuralSwingCount = 0

if barstate.islast and coreDebug

    int stateCount = array.size(
         structuralSwingStates
    )

    if stateCount > 0

        for i = 0 to stateCount - 1

            StructuralSwingStateObject swingState = array.get(
                 structuralSwingStates,
                 i
            )

            if swingState.lifecycle == StructuralSwingLifecycle.Protected

                protectedStructuralSwingCount += 1

            else if swingState.lifecycle == StructuralSwingLifecycle.Broken

                brokenStructuralSwingCount += 1

            else if swingState.lifecycle == StructuralSwingLifecycle.Consumed

                consumedStructuralSwingCount += 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(protectedStructuralSwingCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG PROTECTED STRUCTURAL SWINGS",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(brokenStructuralSwingCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG BROKEN STRUCTURAL SWINGS",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(consumedStructuralSwingCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG CONSUMED STRUCTURAL SWINGS",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(structureProtectionIntegrityViolations) : na,
     title = "DEBUG PROTECTION INTEGRITY VIOLATIONS",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(missingStructureProtectionCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG MISSING STRUCTURE PROTECTION",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(structureLineageOrderViolations) : na,
     title = "DEBUG STRUCTURE LINEAGE ORDER VIOLATIONS",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(pendingChochCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG PENDING CHOCH COUNT",
     display = display.data_window
)

plot(
     coreDebug ? float(invalidPendingChochCount) : na,
     title = "DEBUG INVALID PENDING CHOCH",
     display = display.data_window
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================
f_makeMssCandidateLogicalId(
     string sourceChochEventId
     ) =>

    string result = "MSS_CAND|" + sourceChochEventId

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getPreviousStructureDirection(
     StructureEventObject chochEvent
     ) =>

    Direction result = Direction.Neutral

    if chochEvent.previousStructureEventId != ""

        if map.contains(
             structureEventById,
             chochEvent.previousStructureEventId
        )

            StructureEventObject previousEvent = map.get(
                 structureEventById,
                 chochEvent.previousStructureEventId
            )

            result := previousEvent.direction

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_validateChochMssContract(
     StructureEventObject chochEvent
     ) =>

    // ========================================================================
    // BASIC EVENT VALIDITY
    // ========================================================================

    bool correctType = chochEvent.eventType == StructureEventType.CHOCH

    bool confirmedEvent = chochEvent.lifecycle == EventLifecycle.Confirmed and chochEvent.meta.confirmed

    bool validDirection = chochEvent.direction != Direction.Neutral

    bool validBreakLevel = not na(chochEvent.breakLevel)

    bool hasBrokenSwing = chochEvent.brokenSwingId != ""

    bool hasPreviousStructure = chochEvent.previousStructureEventId != ""

    // ========================================================================
    // ========================================================================

    bool brokenSwingValid = false

    if hasBrokenSwing and map.contains(
     swingObjectById,
     chochEvent.brokenSwingId
    )

        SwingObject brokenSwing = map.get(
            swingObjectById,
            chochEvent.brokenSwingId
        )

        bool sameTf = brokenSwing.meta.originTf == chochEvent.meta.originTf

        bool sameDegree = brokenSwing.degree == chochEvent.degree

        bool correctBrokenSide = false

        if chochEvent.direction == Direction.Bullish

            correctBrokenSide := brokenSwing.side == SwingSide.High

        else if chochEvent.direction == Direction.Bearish

            correctBrokenSide := brokenSwing.side == SwingSide.Low

        brokenSwingValid := sameTf and sameDegree and correctBrokenSide

    // ========================================================================
    // PREVIOUS STRUCTURE VALIDATION
    // ========================================================================

    bool previousStructureValid = false

    if hasPreviousStructure

        if map.contains(
             structureEventById,
             chochEvent.previousStructureEventId
        )

            StructureEventObject previousEvent = map.get(
                 structureEventById,
                 chochEvent.previousStructureEventId
            )

            bool previousConfirmed = previousEvent.lifecycle == EventLifecycle.Confirmed and previousEvent.meta.confirmed

            bool previousDirectionExists = previousEvent.direction != Direction.Neutral

            bool directionChanged = previousEvent.direction != chochEvent.direction

            bool sameTf = previousEvent.meta.originTf == chochEvent.meta.originTf

            bool sameDegree = previousEvent.degree == chochEvent.degree

            bool chronological = previousEvent.meta.confirmationTime <= chochEvent.meta.confirmationTime

            previousStructureValid := previousConfirmed and previousDirectionExists and directionChanged and sameTf and sameDegree and chronological

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

    bool result = correctType and confirmedEvent and validDirection and validBreakLevel and brokenSwingValid and previousStructureValid

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_newMssCandidateFromChoch(
     StructureEventObject chochEvent,
     string contextId,
     string previousCandidateId,
     bool contractValid
     ) =>

    MSSCandidateObject candidate = MSSCandidateObject.new()

    string subtype = "MSS_CAND_" + f_swingDegreeToken(
         chochEvent.degree
    )

    ObjectMeta candidateMeta = f_newMeta(
         "MSS_CANDIDATE",
         subtype,
         chochEvent.meta.originTf,
         chochEvent.meta.confirmationTime,
         chochEvent.breakLevel
    )

    candidateMeta.logicalId := f_makeMssCandidateLogicalId(
         chochEvent.meta.logicalId
    )

    candidateMeta.confirmationTime := na

    candidateMeta.lastUpdateTime := chochEvent.meta.confirmationTime

    candidateMeta.authority := chochEvent.eventAuthority

    candidateMeta.quality := chochEvent.eventQuality

    candidateMeta.confluence := chochEvent.sweepLinked ? chochEvent.eventQuality : 0.0

    candidateMeta.confirmed := false

    candidateMeta.archived := not contractValid

    candidate.meta := candidateMeta

    if contractValid
        candidate.state := MSSCandidateLifecycle.Pending
    else
        candidate.state := MSSCandidateLifecycle.Invalid

    candidate.contextId := contextId

    candidate.sourceChochEventId := chochEvent.meta.logicalId

    candidate.sourcePreviousStructureEventId := chochEvent.previousStructureEventId

    candidate.previousCandidateId := previousCandidateId

    candidate.supersededByChochEventId := ""

    candidate.previousDirection := f_getPreviousStructureDirection(
         chochEvent
    )

    candidate.direction := chochEvent.direction

    candidate.degree := chochEvent.degree

    candidate.breakLevel := chochEvent.breakLevel

    candidate.brokenSwingId := chochEvent.brokenSwingId

    candidate.protectedSwingId := chochEvent.protectedSwingId

    candidate.protectedPrice := chochEvent.protectedPrice

    candidate.protectedTime := chochEvent.protectedTime

    candidate.linkedSweepEventId := chochEvent.linkedSweepEventId

    candidate.linkedDisplacementId := ""

    candidate.lastSupportingBosEventId := ""

    candidate.supportingBosCount := 0

    candidate.candidateTime := chochEvent.meta.confirmationTime

    candidate.confirmationTime := na

    candidate.lastUpdateTime := chochEvent.meta.confirmationTime

    candidate.authority := chochEvent.eventAuthority

    candidate.quality := chochEvent.eventQuality

    candidate.structureContractValid := contractValid

    candidate

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_supersedeMssCandidate(
     MSSCandidateObject candidate,
     string supersedingChochEventId,
     int updateTime
     ) =>

    if candidate.state == MSSCandidateLifecycle.Pending

        candidate.state := MSSCandidateLifecycle.Superseded

        candidate.supersededByChochEventId := supersedingChochEventId

        candidate.lastUpdateTime := updateTime

        candidate.meta.lastUpdateTime := updateTime

        candidate.meta.archived := true

    candidate

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_addSupportingBosToMssCandidate(
     MSSCandidateObject candidate,
     StructureEventObject bosEvent
     ) =>

    if candidate.state == MSSCandidateLifecycle.Pending

        bool sameDirection = candidate.direction == bosEvent.direction

        bool sameTf = candidate.meta.originTf == bosEvent.meta.originTf

        bool sameDegree = candidate.degree == bosEvent.degree

        bool afterCandidate = bosEvent.meta.confirmationTime >= candidate.candidateTime

        bool validSupport = sameDirection and sameTf and sameDegree and afterCandidate

        if validSupport

            candidate.lastSupportingBosEventId := bosEvent.meta.logicalId

            candidate.supportingBosCount += 1

            candidate.lastUpdateTime := bosEvent.meta.confirmationTime

            candidate.meta.lastUpdateTime := bosEvent.meta.confirmationTime

    candidate

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_mssCandidateAcceptsDisplacement(
     MSSCandidateObject candidate,
     Direction displacementDirection,
     string displacementTf,
     SwingDegree displacementDegree,
     int displacementTime
     ) =>

    bool pending = candidate.state == MSSCandidateLifecycle.Pending

    bool validContract = candidate.structureContractValid

    bool sameDirection = candidate.direction == displacementDirection

    bool sameTf = candidate.meta.originTf == displacementTf

    bool sameDegree = candidate.degree == displacementDegree

    bool validTime = not na(displacementTime) and not na(candidate.candidateTime)

    bool chronological = false

    if validTime
        chronological := displacementTime >= candidate.candidateTime

    bool result = pending and validContract and sameDirection and sameTf and sameDegree and chronological

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================
while array.size(structureGovernanceQueue) > 0

    StructureEventObject governanceEvent = array.shift(
         structureGovernanceQueue
    )

    string contextId = f_makeStructureContextLogicalId(
         governanceEvent.meta.originTf,
         governanceEvent.degree
    )

    int contextIndex = f_findStructureContextIndex(
         structureContexts,
         governanceEvent.meta.originTf,
         governanceEvent.degree
    )

    // ========================================================================
    // NEW CHOCH
    // ========================================================================

    if governanceEvent.eventType == StructureEventType.CHOCH

        string newCandidateId = f_makeMssCandidateLogicalId(
            governanceEvent.meta.logicalId
        )

        // ====================================================================
        // DUPLICATE CANDIDATE GUARD
        // ====================================================================

        if not map.contains(
            mssCandidateById,
            newCandidateId
        )

            // =================================================================
            // VALIDATE NEW CHOCH FIRST
            // =================================================================

            bool contractValid = f_validateChochMssContract(
                governanceEvent
            )

            string previousCandidateId = ""

            // =================================================================
            // VALID CHOCH
            // =================================================================

            if contractValid

                // =============================================================
                // SUPERSEDE PREVIOUS PENDING CANDIDATE
                // =============================================================

                if map.contains(
                    pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                    contextId
                )

                    MSSCandidateObject previousCandidate = map.get(
                        pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                        contextId
                    )

                    previousCandidateId := previousCandidate.meta.logicalId

                    bool differentChoch = previousCandidate.sourceChochEventId != governanceEvent.meta.logicalId

                    if differentChoch and previousCandidate.state == MSSCandidateLifecycle.Pending

                        previousCandidate := f_supersedeMssCandidate(
                            previousCandidate,
                            governanceEvent.meta.logicalId,
                            governanceEvent.meta.confirmationTime
                        )

                        map.put(
                            mssCandidateById,
                            previousCandidate.meta.logicalId,
                            previousCandidate
                        )

                    map.remove(
                        pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                        contextId
                    )

                // =============================================================
                // CREATE NEW VALID PENDING CANDIDATE
                // =============================================================

                MSSCandidateObject newCandidate = f_newMssCandidateFromChoch(
                    governanceEvent,
                    contextId,
                    previousCandidateId,
                    true
                )

                array.push(
                    mssCandidateObjects,
                    newCandidate
                )

                map.put(
                    mssCandidateById,
                    newCandidate.meta.logicalId,
                    newCandidate
                )

                map.put(
                    pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                    contextId,
                    newCandidate
                )

                // =============================================================
                // SYNCHRONIZE STRUCTURE CONTEXT
                // =============================================================

                if contextIndex >= 0

                    StructureContextObject context = array.get(
                        structureContexts,
                        contextIndex
                    )

                    context.pendingChochEventId := governanceEvent.meta.logicalId

                    context.meta.lastUpdateTime := governanceEvent.meta.confirmationTime

                    array.set(
                        structureContexts,
                        contextIndex,
                        context
                    )

            // =================================================================
            // =================================================================

            else

                MSSCandidateObject invalidCandidate = f_newMssCandidateFromChoch(
                    governanceEvent,
                    contextId,
                    "",
                    false
                )

                array.push(
                    mssCandidateObjects,
                    invalidCandidate
                )

                map.put(
                    mssCandidateById,
                    invalidCandidate.meta.logicalId,
                    invalidCandidate
                )

        // ====================================================================
        // EXISTING DUPLICATE CANDIDATE
        // ====================================================================

        else

            MSSCandidateObject existingCandidate = map.get(
                mssCandidateById,
                newCandidateId
            )

            if existingCandidate.state == MSSCandidateLifecycle.Pending

                map.put(
                    pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                    contextId,
                    existingCandidate
                )

    // ========================================================================
    // SAME-DIRECTION BOS AFTER CHOCH
    // ========================================================================

    else if governanceEvent.eventType == StructureEventType.BOS

        if map.contains(
             pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
             contextId
        )

            MSSCandidateObject pendingCandidate = map.get(
                 pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                 contextId
            )

            pendingCandidate := f_addSupportingBosToMssCandidate(
                 pendingCandidate,
                 governanceEvent
            )

            map.put(
                 mssCandidateById,
                 pendingCandidate.meta.logicalId,
                 pendingCandidate
            )

            map.put(
                 pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                 contextId,
                 pendingCandidate
            )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if enableMarketStructureEngine and barstate.isconfirmed

    int governanceContextCount = array.size(
         structureContexts
    )

    if governanceContextCount > 0

        for i = 0 to governanceContextCount - 1

            StructureContextObject context = array.get(
                 structureContexts,
                 i
            )

            string contextId = context.meta.logicalId

            bool contextChanged = false

            // =================================================================
            // PENDING CANDIDATE EXISTS
            // =================================================================

            if map.contains(
                 pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                 contextId
            )

                MSSCandidateObject candidate = map.get(
                     pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                     contextId
                )

                bool candidatePending = candidate.state == MSSCandidateLifecycle.Pending

                bool validContract = candidate.structureContractValid

                bool directionConsistent = candidate.direction == context.bias

                // =============================================================
                // VALID CURRENT CANDIDATE
                // =============================================================

                if candidatePending and validContract and directionConsistent

                    if context.pendingChochEventId != candidate.sourceChochEventId

                        context.pendingChochEventId := candidate.sourceChochEventId

                        contextChanged := true

                // =============================================================
                // =============================================================

                else if candidatePending and validContract and not directionConsistent

                    candidate.state := MSSCandidateLifecycle.Superseded

                    candidate.lastUpdateTime := time_close

                    candidate.meta.lastUpdateTime := time_close

                    candidate.meta.archived := true

                    // If the current Context has a newer CHOCH,
                    // preserve that lineage relationship.

                    if context.pendingChochEventId != "" and context.pendingChochEventId != candidate.sourceChochEventId

                        candidate.supersededByChochEventId := context.pendingChochEventId

                    map.put(
                         mssCandidateById,
                         candidate.meta.logicalId,
                         candidate
                    )

                    map.remove(
                         pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                         contextId
                    )

                    if context.pendingChochEventId == candidate.sourceChochEventId

                        context.pendingChochEventId := ""

                        contextChanged := true

                // =============================================================
                // ACTUALLY INVALID CANDIDATE
                // =============================================================

                else if candidatePending and not validContract

                    candidate.state := MSSCandidateLifecycle.Invalid

                    candidate.lastUpdateTime := time_close

                    candidate.meta.lastUpdateTime := time_close

                    candidate.meta.archived := true

                    map.put(
                         mssCandidateById,
                         candidate.meta.logicalId,
                         candidate
                    )

                    map.remove(
                         pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                         contextId
                    )

                    if context.pendingChochEventId == candidate.sourceChochEventId

                        context.pendingChochEventId := ""

                        contextChanged := true

                // =============================================================
                // NON-PENDING OBJECT MUST NOT REMAIN IN PENDING MAP
                // =============================================================

                else if not candidatePending

                    map.remove(
                         pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                         contextId
                    )

                    if context.pendingChochEventId == candidate.sourceChochEventId

                        context.pendingChochEventId := ""

                        contextChanged := true

            // =================================================================
            // NO PENDING CANDIDATE
            // =================================================================

            else

                // Do NOT blindly clear pendingChochEventId if it points to a
                // freshly-created CHOCH that Governance has not yet attached.
                //
                // Verify whether it has a valid pending Candidate.

                if context.pendingChochEventId != ""

                    string expectedCandidateId = f_makeMssCandidateLogicalId(
                         context.pendingChochEventId
                    )

                    bool validPendingReference = false

                    if map.contains(
                         mssCandidateById,
                         expectedCandidateId
                    )

                        MSSCandidateObject referencedCandidate = map.get(
                             mssCandidateById,
                             expectedCandidateId
                        )

                        validPendingReference := referencedCandidate.state == MSSCandidateLifecycle.Pending and referencedCandidate.structureContractValid

                    if not validPendingReference

                        context.pendingChochEventId := ""

                        contextChanged := true

            // =================================================================
            // EXPLICIT CONTEXT WRITE
            // =================================================================

            if contextChanged

                context.meta.lastUpdateTime := time_close

                array.set(
                     structureContexts,
                     i,
                     context
                )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(mssCandidateObjects) > CORE_MAX_SWINGS

    MSSCandidateObject removedCandidate = array.shift(
         mssCandidateObjects
    )

    if map.contains(
         mssCandidateById,
         removedCandidate.meta.logicalId
    )

        map.remove(
             mssCandidateById,
             removedCandidate.meta.logicalId
        )

    if map.contains(
         pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
         removedCandidate.contextId
    )

        MSSCandidateObject mappedPendingCandidate = map.get(
             pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
             removedCandidate.contextId
        )

        if mappedPendingCandidate.meta.logicalId == removedCandidate.meta.logicalId

            map.remove(
                 pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                 removedCandidate.contextId
            )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int totalMssCandidateCount = array.size(
     mssCandidateObjects
)

int pendingMssCandidateCount = 0

int confirmedMssCandidateCount = 0

int supersededMssCandidateCount = 0

int invalidMssCandidateCount = 0

int sweepLinkedMssCandidateCount = 0

int bosSupportedMssCandidateCount = 0

int mssCandidateDirectionViolations = 0

int mssCandidateSourceViolations = 0

if barstate.islast and coreDebug and totalMssCandidateCount > 0

    for i = 0 to totalMssCandidateCount - 1

        MSSCandidateObject candidate = array.get(
             mssCandidateObjects,
             i
        )

        // ====================================================================
        // STATE COUNTS
        // ====================================================================

        if candidate.state == MSSCandidateLifecycle.Pending

            pendingMssCandidateCount += 1

        else if candidate.state == MSSCandidateLifecycle.Confirmed

            confirmedMssCandidateCount += 1

        else if candidate.state == MSSCandidateLifecycle.Superseded

            supersededMssCandidateCount += 1

        else if candidate.state == MSSCandidateLifecycle.Invalid

            invalidMssCandidateCount += 1

        // ====================================================================
        // CONTEXT COUNTS
        // ====================================================================

        if candidate.linkedSweepEventId != ""

            sweepLinkedMssCandidateCount += 1

        if candidate.supportingBosCount > 0

            bosSupportedMssCandidateCount += 1

        // ====================================================================
        // PREVIOUS STRUCTURE DIRECTION VALIDATION
        // ====================================================================

        if candidate.previousDirection != Direction.Neutral

            if candidate.previousDirection == candidate.direction

                mssCandidateDirectionViolations += 1

        // ====================================================================
        // SOURCE CHOCH VALIDATION
        // ====================================================================

        if candidate.sourceChochEventId != ""

            if map.contains(
                 structureEventById,
                 candidate.sourceChochEventId
            )

                StructureEventObject sourceEvent = map.get(
                     structureEventById,
                     candidate.sourceChochEventId
                )

                bool correctType = sourceEvent.eventType == StructureEventType.CHOCH

                bool sameDirection = sourceEvent.direction == candidate.direction

                bool sameDegree = sourceEvent.degree == candidate.degree

                bool sameTf = sourceEvent.meta.originTf == candidate.meta.originTf

                if not correctType or not sameDirection or not sameDegree or not sameTf

                    mssCandidateSourceViolations += 1

            else

                // Missing historical source after history trimming is not
                // treated as a structural logic violation here.

                na

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

int pendingContextCandidateMismatchCount = 0

if barstate.islast and coreDebug

    int contextCount = array.size(
         structureContexts
    )

    if contextCount > 0

        for i = 0 to contextCount - 1

            StructureContextObject context = array.get(
                 structureContexts,
                 i
            )

            string contextId = context.meta.logicalId

            if map.contains(
                 pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                 contextId
            )

                MSSCandidateObject candidate = map.get(
                     pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                     contextId
                )

                bool validState = candidate.state == MSSCandidateLifecycle.Pending

                bool validDirection = candidate.direction == context.bias

                bool validChochId = candidate.sourceChochEventId == context.pendingChochEventId

                if not validState or not validDirection or not validChochId

                    pendingContextCandidateMismatchCount += 1

// ============================================================================

string grpDisplacement = "06 DISPLACEMENT ENGINE"

     65.0,
     "Minimum Body %",
     minval = 30.0,
     maxval = 95.0,
     step = 5.0,
     group = grpDisplacement,
     tooltip = "Minimum body as percentage of total candle range."
)

     "Minimum ATR Ratio",
     minval = 0.5,
     maxval = 5.0,
     step = 0.1,
     group = grpDisplacement,
     tooltip = "Minimum candle range / ATR ratio for displacement."

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

float displacementATR = ta.atr(displacementATRLength)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_isDisplacementCandle(
     float candleOpen,
     float candleHigh,
     float candleLow,
     float candleClose,
     float atr,
     float minBodyPct,
     float minATRRatio
     ) =>

    bool result = false
    Direction direction = Direction.Neutral

    float candleRange = candleHigh - candleLow
    float candleBody = math.abs(candleClose - candleOpen)
    float bodyPercent = candleRange > 0 ? (candleBody / candleRange) * 100.0 : 0.0
    float atrRatio = atr > 0 ? candleRange / atr : 0.0

    bool bodyDominant = bodyPercent >= minBodyPct
    bool rangeExpanded = atrRatio >= minATRRatio
    bool hasRange = candleRange > 0

    if bodyDominant and rangeExpanded and hasRange

        result := true
        direction := candleClose > candleOpen ? Direction.Bullish : Direction.Bearish

    [result, direction, candleRange, candleBody, bodyPercent, atrRatio]

f_getDisplacementMomentumScore(
     float bodyPercent,
     float atrRatio,
     float closeLocation
     ) =>

    float bodyScore = math.min(bodyPercent, 100.0) * 0.4
    float atrScore = math.min(atrRatio * 25.0, 40.0)
    float closeScore = closeLocation * 20.0

    float result = math.min(bodyScore + atrScore + closeScore, 100.0)
    result

f_getDisplacementQuality(
     Direction direction,
     float bodyPercent,
     float atrRatio,
     float momentumScore
     ) =>

    float directionScore = direction != Direction.Neutral ? 30.0 : 0.0
    float bodyScore = math.min(bodyPercent, 100.0) * 0.35
    float atrScore = math.min(atrRatio * 20.0, 35.0)
    float momentumComponent = momentumScore * 0.15

    float result = math.min(directionScore + bodyScore + atrScore + momentumComponent, 100.0)
    result
f_newDisplacementObject(
     string originTf,
     Direction direction,
     int candleTime,
     int candleBar,
     float candleOpen,
     float candleHigh,
     float candleLow,
     float candleClose,
     float candleRange,
     float candleBody,
     float bodyPercent,
     float atr,
     float atrRatio,
     float momentumScore,
     float qualityScore
     ) =>

    ObjectMeta meta = f_newMeta(
         "DISP",
         direction == Direction.Bullish ? "BULL" : "BEAR",
         originTf,
         candleTime,
         candleClose
    )

    meta.authority := math.min(50.0 + atrRatio * 10.0, 100.0)
    meta.quality := qualityScore
    meta.confirmed := true
    meta.archived := false

    DisplacementObject obj = DisplacementObject.new(
         meta,
         direction,
         candleTime,
         candleBar,
         candleOpen,
         candleHigh,
         candleLow,
         candleClose,
         candleRange,
         candleBody,
         bodyPercent,
         atr,
         atrRatio,
         na,
         momentumScore,
         "",
         DisplacementState.Confirmed
    )

    obj

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

bool displacementDetected = false
Direction displacementDirection = Direction.Neutral

if displacementATR > 0 and barstate.isconfirmed

    [candleIsDisplacement, candleDirection, candleRange, candleBody, candleBodyPercent, candleATRRatio] = f_isDisplacementCandle(
         open,
         high,
         low,
         close,
         displacementATR,
         displacementMinBodyPercent,
         displacementMinATRRatio
    )

    if candleIsDisplacement

        displacementDetected := true
        displacementDirection := candleDirection

        float closeLocation = candleRange > 0 ? (close - low) / candleRange : 0.5

        float momentumScore = f_getDisplacementMomentumScore(
             candleBodyPercent,
             candleATRRatio,
             closeLocation
        )

        float qualityScore = f_getDisplacementQuality(
             candleDirection,
             candleBodyPercent,
             candleATRRatio,
             momentumScore
        )

        DisplacementObject newDisplacement = f_newDisplacementObject(
             timeframe.period,
             candleDirection,
             time_close,
             bar_index,
             open,
             high,
             low,
             close,
             candleRange,
             candleBody,
             candleBodyPercent,
             displacementATR,
             candleATRRatio,
             momentumScore,
             qualityScore
        )

        array.push(
             displacementObjects,
             newDisplacement
        )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(displacementObjects) > CORE_MAX_DISPLACEMENTS
    array.shift(displacementObjects)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if displacementATR > 0 and barstate.isconfirmed and displacementDetected

    int mssCandidateCount = array.size(mssCandidateObjects)

    bool displacementStored = array.size(displacementObjects) > 0

    string latestDisplacementId = displacementStored ?
         array.get(displacementObjects, array.size(displacementObjects) - 1).meta.logicalId :
         ""

    if mssCandidateCount > 0

        // Bounded, newest-first scan.
        // candidates are always recent, so only the tail is worth scanning.

        int mssScanStop = math.max(0, mssCandidateCount - MSS_ACTIVE_SCAN)

        for candidateIndex = mssCandidateCount - 1 to mssScanStop

            MSSCandidateObject candidate = array.get(
                 mssCandidateObjects,
                 candidateIndex
            )

            // Only Pending candidates can be confirmed. Skipping the rest
            // before the expensive acceptance check cuts most of the work.
            if candidate.state != MSSCandidateLifecycle.Pending
                continue

            // ====================================================================
            // ACCEPTANCE CONTRACT + BREAK LEVEL
            // ====================================================================

            bool accepts = f_mssCandidateAcceptsDisplacement(
                 candidate,
                 displacementDirection,
                 timeframe.period,
                 candidate.degree,
                 time_close
            )

            bool breakLevelConfirmed = false

            if not na(candidate.breakLevel)

                if displacementDirection == Direction.Bullish

                    breakLevelConfirmed := close > candidate.breakLevel

                else if displacementDirection == Direction.Bearish

                    breakLevelConfirmed := close < candidate.breakLevel

            // ====================================================================
            // CONFIRM MSS CANDIDATE
            // ====================================================================

            if accepts and breakLevelConfirmed

                MSSCandidateObject confirmedCandidate = candidate

                confirmedCandidate.state := MSSCandidateLifecycle.Confirmed

                confirmedCandidate.confirmationTime := time_close

                confirmedCandidate.linkedDisplacementId := latestDisplacementId

                array.set(
                     mssCandidateObjects,
                     candidateIndex,
                     confirmedCandidate
                )

                map.put(
                     mssCandidateById,
                     confirmedCandidate.meta.logicalId,
                     confirmedCandidate
                )

                // Remove this candidate from pending map if present.
                // pendingMssCandidateByContextId is keyed by contextId.
                // We find the contextId that stored this candidate and remove it.
                int contextsSize = array.size(structureContexts)
                for ctxScan = 0 to contextsSize - 1
                    StructureContextObject ctxObj = array.get(structureContexts, ctxScan)
                    string ctxLogicalId = ctxObj.meta.logicalId
                    if map.contains(pendingMssCandidateByContextId, ctxLogicalId)
                        MSSCandidateObject mapCandidate = map.get(pendingMssCandidateByContextId, ctxLogicalId)
                        if mapCandidate.meta.logicalId == confirmedCandidate.meta.logicalId
                            map.remove(pendingMssCandidateByContextId, ctxLogicalId)
                            break

                break

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(mssCandidateObjects) > CORE_MAX_SWINGS

    MSSCandidateObject evictedMss = array.shift(mssCandidateObjects)

    // SAFETY: the first trim block already evicts map entries when it runs,
    // but placement depends on which trim fires first in a given bar. This
    // second block MUST also clean the map to guarantee no leaked entry can
    // pin a dead object after both blocks have run.

    if not na(evictedMss)

        if map.contains(mssCandidateById, evictedMss.meta.logicalId)

            map.remove(mssCandidateById, evictedMss.meta.logicalId)

        if map.contains(pendingMssCandidateByContextId, evictedMss.contextId)

            MSSCandidateObject pendingMss = map.get(
                 pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                 evictedMss.contextId
            )

            if not na(pendingMss) and pendingMss.meta.logicalId == evictedMss.meta.logicalId

                map.remove(
                     pendingMssCandidateByContextId,
                     evictedMss.contextId
                )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

string grpFVG = "07 FVG ENGINE"

bool enableFVGEngine = input.bool(
     false,
     "Enable FVG Engine",
     group = grpFVG
)

bool showBullishFVG = input.bool(
     true,
     "Show Bullish FVGs",
     group = grpFVG
)

bool showBearishFVG = input.bool(
     true,
     "Show Bearish FVGs",
     group = grpFVG
)

color bullishFVGColor = input.color(
     color.new(color.green, 78),
     "Bullish FVG Color",
     group = grpFVG
)

color bearishFVGColor = input.color(
     color.new(color.red, 78),
     "Bearish FVG Color",
     group = grpFVG
)

float fvgMitigationPercent = input.float(
     50.0,
     "Mitigation Fill %",
     minval = 25.0,
     maxval = 100.0,
     step = 5.0,
     group = grpFVG,
     tooltip = "Gap fill percentage at which an FVG is considered mitigated."
)

int fvgMaxAge = input.int(
     150,
     "Max Age (bars)",
     minval = 20,
     maxval = 1000,
     group = grpFVG,
     tooltip = "Live FVGs older than this are retired to Invalid so they stop " +
          "consuming per-bar scan slots. Data is kept, not deleted."
)

float fvgMinSizeATR = input.float(
     0.0,
     "Minimum Gap Size (x ATR)",
     minval = 0.0,
     maxval = 3.0,
     step = 0.05,
     group = grpFVG,
     tooltip = "Gaps smaller than this multiple of ATR are ignored. 0 = keep all."
)

string fvgExtendMode = input.string(
     "To Current Price",
     "FVG Box Extension",
     options = ["To Current Price", "Until Mitigated", "Fixed Bars After Current", "Fixed Bars After Mitigation"],
     group = grpFVG,
     tooltip = "How far the FVG box extends to the right."
)

int fvgFixedExtendBars = input.int(
     50,
     "FVG Fixed Extend Bars",
     minval = 5,
     maxval = 500,
     group = grpFVG
)

const int FVG_ACTIVE_SCAN = 80

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_detectFVG() =>
    // Bullish FVG: candle1 high is below candle3 low (price displaced up)
    bool bullishGap = high[2] < low

    // Bearish FVG: candle1 low is above candle3 high (price displaced down)
    bool bearishGap = low[2] > high

    [bullishGap, bearishGap]

f_makeFVGLogicalId(
     string originTf,
     int originTime,
     float top,
     float bottom
     ) =>

    string normalizedTf = f_normalizeTf(originTf)
    string timeToken = na(originTime) ? "NA" : str.tostring(originTime)
    string topToken = na(top) ? "NA" : str.tostring(f_priceToTicks(top))
    string bottomToken = na(bottom) ? "NA" : str.tostring(f_priceToTicks(bottom))

    string result = "FVG|" + normalizedTf + "|" + timeToken + "|" + topToken + "|" + bottomToken
    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_updateFVGState(
     FVGObject fvg,
     float currentLow,
     float currentHigh
     ) =>

    FVGState newState = fvg.state

    float gapTop = math.max(fvg.top, fvg.bottom)
    float gapBottom = math.min(fvg.top, fvg.bottom)

    float gapSize = gapTop - gapBottom

    if gapSize > 0

        bool touched = currentHigh >= gapBottom and currentLow <= gapTop

        if touched

            float fillDistance = 0.0

            if fvg.direction == Direction.Bullish

                // Retracement comes down from above.
                fillDistance := gapTop - currentLow

            else

                // Retracement comes up from below.
                fillDistance := currentHigh - gapBottom

            float rawFill = math.min(
                 math.max(fillDistance / gapSize, 0.0) * 100.0,
                 100.0
            )

            // Never let a later bar reduce a fill already achieved.
            float fillPercent = math.max(rawFill, nz(fvg.fillPercent, 0.0))

            fvg.fillPercent := fillPercent

            if fillPercent >= fvgMitigationPercent

                newState := FVGState.Mitigated

            else if fillPercent > 0.0

                newState := FVGState.Partial

    newState

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(fvgObjects) > CORE_MAX_FVGS

    FVGObject evictedFVG = array.shift(fvgObjects)

    if not na(evictedFVG)

        if map.contains(fvgObjectById, evictedFVG.meta.logicalId)

            map.remove(fvgObjectById, evictedFVG.meta.logicalId)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if enableFVGEngine and barstate.isconfirmed

    [bullishGapRaw, bearishGapRaw] = f_detectFVG()

    // Minimum-size filter. displacementATR is already computed in Phase 5.
    float fvgMinSize = fvgMinSizeATR > 0.0 and not na(displacementATR)
         ? displacementATR * fvgMinSizeATR
         : 0.0

    float bullGapSize = high[2] < low ? low - high[2] : 0.0
    float bearGapSize = low[2] > high ? low[2] - high : 0.0

    bool bullishGap = bullishGapRaw and bullGapSize >= fvgMinSize
    bool bearishGap = bearishGapRaw and bearGapSize >= fvgMinSize

    // ========================================================================
    // CREATE NEW FVG
    // ========================================================================

    if bullishGap

        // Bullish gap spans from candle1 high up to candle3 low.
        float gapBottom = high[2]
        float gapTop = low

        string fvgId = f_makeFVGLogicalId(
             timeframe.period,
             time_close,
             gapTop,
             gapBottom
        )

        if not map.contains(
             fvgObjectById,
             fvgId
        )

            ObjectMeta meta = f_newMeta(
                 "FVG",
                 "BULL",
                 timeframe.period,
                 time_close,
                 (gapTop + gapBottom) / 2.0
            )

            // Keep the map key and the object identity in sync.
            meta.logicalId := fvgId

            meta.authority := 55.0
            meta.quality := 70.0
            meta.confirmed := true
            meta.archived := false

            float ce = (gapTop + gapBottom) / 2.0

            FVGObject newFVG = FVGObject.new(
                 meta,
                 Direction.Bullish,
                 gapTop,
                 gapBottom,
                 ce,
                 bar_index,
                 FVGState.Fresh,
                 0.0,
                 na,
                 na,
                 "",
                 ""
            )

            array.push(
                 fvgObjects,
                 newFVG
            )

            map.put(
                 fvgObjectById,
                 fvgId,
                 newFVG
            )

    else if bearishGap

        // Bearish gap spans from candle3 high up to candle1 low.
        float gapBottom = high
        float gapTop = low[2]

        string fvgId = f_makeFVGLogicalId(
             timeframe.period,
             time_close,
             gapTop,
             gapBottom
        )

        if not map.contains(
             fvgObjectById,
             fvgId
        )

            ObjectMeta meta = f_newMeta(
                 "FVG",
                 "BEAR",
                 timeframe.period,
                 time_close,
                 (gapTop + gapBottom) / 2.0
            )

            // Keep the map key and the object identity in sync.
            meta.logicalId := fvgId

            meta.authority := 55.0
            meta.quality := 70.0
            meta.confirmed := true
            meta.archived := false

            float ce = (gapTop + gapBottom) / 2.0

            FVGObject newFVG = FVGObject.new(
                 meta,
                 Direction.Bearish,
                 gapTop,
                 gapBottom,
                 ce,
                 bar_index,
                 FVGState.Fresh,
                 0.0,
                 na,
                 na,
                 "",
                 ""
            )

            array.push(
                 fvgObjects,
                 newFVG
            )

            map.put(
                 fvgObjectById,
                 fvgId,
                 newFVG
            )

    // ========================================================================
    // ========================================================================

    int fvgStorageSize = array.size(fvgObjects)

    if fvgStorageSize > 0

        int fvgScanFrom = math.max(0, fvgStorageSize - FVG_ACTIVE_SCAN)

        for fvgIndex = fvgScanFrom to fvgStorageSize - 1

            FVGObject fvg = array.get(
                 fvgObjects,
                 fvgIndex
            )

            bool fvgLive = fvg.state == FVGState.Fresh
                 or fvg.state == FVGState.Partial

            if fvgLive

                // Age retirement
                if bar_index - fvg.originBar > fvgMaxAge

                    fvg.state := FVGState.Invalid

                    array.set(
                         fvgObjects,
                         fvgIndex,
                         fvg
                    )

                else

                    FVGState updatedState = f_updateFVGState(
                         fvg,
                         low,
                         high
                    )

                    if na(fvg.firstTouchTime) and fvg.fillPercent > 0.0

                        fvg.firstTouchTime := time_close

                    if updatedState != fvg.state

                        fvg.state := updatedState

                        if updatedState == FVGState.Mitigated

                            fvg.mitigationTime := time_close

                    array.set(
                         fvgObjects,
                         fvgIndex,
                         fvg
                    )
