const int OB_ACTIVE_SCAN = 80

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_detectLastOpposingCandleBeforeDisplacement(
     Direction dispDirection,
     int lookback
     ) =>

    int foundBar = na
    float foundHigh = na
    float foundLow = na
    int foundTime = na

    for lookbackIndex = 1 to lookback

        float candleOpen  = open[lookbackIndex]
        float candleClose = close[lookbackIndex]
        float candleHigh  = high[lookbackIndex]
        float candleLow   = low[lookbackIndex]

        bool isBearish = candleClose < candleOpen
        bool isBullish = candleClose > candleOpen

        if dispDirection == Direction.Bullish and isBearish

            foundBar    := bar_index - lookbackIndex
            foundHigh   := candleHigh
            foundLow    := candleLow
            foundTime   := time_close[lookbackIndex]
            break

        else if dispDirection == Direction.Bearish and isBullish

            foundBar    := bar_index - lookbackIndex
            foundHigh   := candleHigh
            foundLow    := candleLow
            foundTime   := time_close[lookbackIndex]
            break

    [foundBar, foundHigh, foundLow, foundTime]

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_makeOBLogicalId(
     string originTf,
     int originTime,
     float highVal,
     float lowVal
     ) =>

    string normalizedTf = f_normalizeTf(originTf)
    string timeToken = na(originTime) ? "NA" : str.tostring(originTime)
    string highToken = na(highVal) ? "NA" : str.tostring(f_priceToTicks(highVal))
    string lowToken  = na(lowVal) ? "NA" : str.tostring(f_priceToTicks(lowVal))

    string result = "OB|" + normalizedTf + "|" + timeToken + "|" + highToken + "|" + lowToken
    result

f_newOBObject(
     ObjectMeta meta,
     Direction direction,
     float highPrice,
     float lowPrice,
     int originTime,
     int originBar
     ) =>

    OBObject.new(
         meta,
         direction,
         highPrice,
         lowPrice,
         originTime,
         originBar,
         OBState.Candidate,
         "",
         "",
         "",
         ""
    )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_updateOBState(
     OBObject ob,
     float currentHigh,
     float currentLow,
     float currentClose
     ) =>

    OBState newState = ob.state

    float obHigh = math.max(ob.high, ob.low)
    float obLow  = math.min(ob.high, ob.low)

    if ob.state == OBState.Candidate

        // Immediately promote to Fresh on creation.
        newState := OBState.Fresh

    else if ob.state == OBState.Fresh

        bool entered = currentLow <= obHigh and currentHigh >= obLow

        if entered

            bool filledThrough = false

            if ob.direction == Direction.Bullish

                // Bullish OB mitigated when price drops below OB low.
                filledThrough := currentClose < obLow

            else

                // Bearish OB mitigated when price rises above OB high.
                filledThrough := currentClose > obHigh

            if filledThrough

                newState := OBState.Mitigated

            else

                newState := OBState.Tested

    else if ob.state == OBState.Tested

        bool filledThrough = false

        if ob.direction == Direction.Bullish

            filledThrough := currentClose < obLow

        else

            filledThrough := currentClose > obHigh

        if filledThrough

            newState := OBState.Mitigated

    newState

f_isBreakerOB(
     OBObject ob,
     float currentHigh,
     float currentLow
     ) =>

    // A Breaker is a VIOLATED Order Block that price returns to. The zone
    // could ever become a Breaker.

    bool result = false

    if ob.state == OBState.Mitigated

        float obHigh = math.max(ob.high, ob.low)
        float obLow  = math.min(ob.high, ob.low)

        bool reEntered = currentHigh >= obLow and currentLow <= obHigh

        result := reEntered

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(obObjects) > CORE_MAX_OBS

    OBObject evictedOB = array.shift(obObjects)

    if not na(evictedOB)

        if map.contains(obObjectById, evictedOB.meta.logicalId)

            map.remove(obObjectById, evictedOB.meta.logicalId)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if enableOBEngine and barstate.isconfirmed and displacementDetected

    [obBar, obHigh, obLow, obTime] = f_detectLastOpposingCandleBeforeDisplacement(
         displacementDirection,
         obLookbackRange
    )

    if not na(obBar) and not na(obHigh) and not na(obLow) and not na(obTime)

        string obId = f_makeOBLogicalId(
             timeframe.period,
             obTime,
             obHigh,
             obLow
        )

        if not map.contains(
             obObjectById,
             obId
        )

            ObjectMeta meta = f_newMeta(
                 "OB",
                 displacementDirection == Direction.Bullish ? "BULL" : "BEAR",
                 timeframe.period,
                 obTime,
                 (obHigh + obLow) / 2.0
            )

            // Keep the map key and the object identity in sync so the trim
            // in section 213 can evict the matching map entry.
            meta.logicalId := obId

            meta.authority := 65.0
            meta.quality   := 70.0
            meta.confirmed := true
            meta.archived  := false

            OBObject newOB = f_newOBObject(
                 meta,
                 displacementDirection,
                 obHigh,
                 obLow,
                 obTime,
                 obBar
            )

            newOB.linkedDisplacementId := array.size(displacementObjects) > 0
                 ? array.get(displacementObjects, array.size(displacementObjects) - 1).meta.logicalId
                 : ""

            array.push(
                 obObjects,
                 newOB
            )

            map.put(
                 obObjectById,
                 obId,
                 newOB
            )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if enableOBEngine and barstate.isconfirmed

    int obStorageSize = array.size(obObjects)

    if obStorageSize > 0

        int obScanFrom = math.max(0, obStorageSize - OB_ACTIVE_SCAN)

        for obIndex = obScanFrom to obStorageSize - 1

            OBObject ob = array.get(
                 obObjects,
                 obIndex
            )

            if ob.state != OBState.Invalid

                // Age retirement. Breakers are exempt — they stay meaningful.
                int barsSinceOB = bar_index - ob.originBar
                bool agedOut = barsSinceOB > obMaxAge

                if agedOut and ob.state != OBState.Breaker

                    ob.state := OBState.Invalid

                    array.set(
                         obObjects,
                         obIndex,
                         ob
                    )

                else if ob.state == OBState.Mitigated

                    // Breaker promotion
                    if f_isBreakerOB(ob, high, low)

                        ob.state := OBState.Breaker

                        array.set(
                             obObjects,
                             obIndex,
                             ob
                        )

                else if ob.state != OBState.Breaker

                    OBState updatedState = f_updateOBState(
                         ob,
                         high,
                         low,
                         close
                    )

                    if updatedState != ob.state

                        ob.state := updatedState

                        array.set(
                             obObjects,
                             obIndex,
                             ob
                        )

f_classifyPDLocation(
     float price,
     float eq,
     float drHigh,
     float drLow
     ) =>

    PDLocation result = PDLocation.Unknown

    float rangeSize = drHigh - drLow

    if rangeSize > 0

        if price > eq

            result := PDLocation.Premium

        else if price < eq

            result := PDLocation.Discount

        else

            result := PDLocation.Equilibrium

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_calcOTE(
     float drHigh,
     float drLow
     ) =>

    float rangeSize = drHigh - drLow

    float ote62  = drHigh - rangeSize * 0.618
    float ote705 = high - rangeSize * 0.705
    float ote79  = high - rangeSize * 0.786

    [ote62, ote705, ote79]

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_makeDRLogicalId(
     int highBar,
     int lowBar
     ) =>

    "DR|" + str.tostring(highBar) + "|" + str.tostring(lowBar)

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

float drRollingHigh    = ta.highest(high, drLookbackBars)
float drRollingLow     = ta.lowest(low, drLookbackBars)
int   drRollingHighOff = ta.highestbars(high, drLookbackBars)
int   drRollingLowOff  = ta.lowestbars(low, drLookbackBars)

int drRollingHighBar = na(drRollingHighOff) ? na : bar_index + drRollingHighOff
int drRollingLowBar  = na(drRollingLowOff) ? na : bar_index + drRollingLowOff

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(dealingRangeObjects) > CORE_MAX_RANGES
    array.shift(dealingRangeObjects)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if enableDealingRangeEngine and barstate.isconfirmed

    float swH   = drRollingHigh
    int   swHBar = drRollingHighBar
    float swL   = drRollingLow
    int   swLBar = drRollingLowBar

    if not na(swH) and not na(swL) and swH > swL

        string drId = f_makeDRLogicalId(swHBar, swLBar)

        int drCount = array.size(dealingRangeObjects)

        bool drTailMatches = false

        // Only the TAIL is compared. Historical ranges are immutable.
        if drCount > 0

            DealingRangeObject drTail = array.get(
                 dealingRangeObjects,
                 drCount - 1
            )

            if drTail.meta.logicalId == drId

                drTailMatches := true

                // Bounds unchanged — refresh PD location in place.
                drTail.currentLocation := f_classifyPDLocation(
                     close,
                     drTail.equilibrium,
                     drTail.high,
                     drTail.low
                )

                array.set(
                     dealingRangeObjects,
                     drCount - 1,
                     drTail
                )

            else

                // Bounds changed — retire the previous range.
                if drTail.active

                    drTail.active := false

                    array.set(
                         dealingRangeObjects,
                         drCount - 1,
                         drTail
                    )

        if not drTailMatches

            float eq = (swH + swL) / 2.0

            [ote62, ote705, ote79] = f_calcOTE(swH, swL)

            PDLocation currentPD = f_classifyPDLocation(
                 close,
                 eq,
                 swH,
                 swL
            )

            ObjectMeta meta = f_newMeta(
                 "DR",
                 "RANGE",
                 timeframe.period,
                 time_close,
                 eq
            )

            meta.logicalId := drId

            meta.authority := 70.0
            meta.quality   := 80.0
            meta.confirmed := true
            meta.archived  := false

            DealingRangeObject newDR = DealingRangeObject.new(
                 meta,
                 Direction.Bullish,
                 swH,
                 swL,
                 eq,
                 ote62,
                 ote705,
                 ote79,
                 "",
                 "",
                 currentPD,
                 true
            )

            array.push(
                 dealingRangeObjects,
                 newDR
            )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_parseSessionWindow(string windowDef) =>

    array<string> parts = str.split(windowDef, "-")

    string startToken = array.size(parts) > 0 ? array.get(parts, 0) : "0000"
    string endToken   = array.size(parts) > 1 ? array.get(parts, 1) : "0000"

    int startMinutes = na
    int endMinutes   = na

    if str.length(startToken) == 4

        float startHour = str.tonumber(str.substring(startToken, 0, 2))
        float startMin  = str.tonumber(str.substring(startToken, 2, 4))

        if not na(startHour) and not na(startMin)
            startMinutes := int(startHour) * 60 + int(startMin)

    if str.length(endToken) == 4

        float endHour = str.tonumber(str.substring(endToken, 0, 2))
        float endMin  = str.tonumber(str.substring(endToken, 2, 4))

        if not na(endHour) and not na(endMin)
            endMinutes := int(endHour) * 60 + int(endMin)

    [startMinutes, endMinutes]

f_isInSession(
     int barLocalMinutes,
     int sessionStartMinutes,
     int sessionEndMinutes
     ) =>

    bool result = false

    if na(barLocalMinutes) or na(sessionStartMinutes) or na(sessionEndMinutes)

        result := false

    else if sessionStartMinutes < sessionEndMinutes

        // Same-day window (e.g. 0700-1000)
        result := barLocalMinutes >= sessionStartMinutes and barLocalMinutes < sessionEndMinutes

    else if sessionStartMinutes > sessionEndMinutes

        // Crosses midnight (e.g. 2300-0200)
        result := barLocalMinutes >= sessionStartMinutes or barLocalMinutes < sessionEndMinutes

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getBarLocalMinutes(string tz) =>
    int barLocalHour   = hour(time, tz)
    int barLocalMinute = minute(time, tz)
    barLocalHour * 60 + barLocalMinute

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_getSessionDayKey(string tz) =>
    str.tostring(year(time, tz)) + "-" +
     str.tostring(month(time, tz)) + "-" +
     str.tostring(dayofmonth(time, tz))

f_makeSessionLogicalId(
     string sessionName,
     string dayKey,
     int startTime,
     int endTime
     ) =>

    "SESS|" + sessionName + "|" + dayKey + "|" +
     str.tostring(startTime) + "|" + str.tostring(endTime)

f_makeSessionObject(
     SessionKind kind,
     string sessionName,
     string dayKey,
     int startTime,
     int endTime
     ) =>

    ObjectMeta meta = f_newMeta(
         "SESS",
         sessionName,
         timeframe.period,
         time_close,
         close
    )

    meta.logicalId := f_makeSessionLogicalId(
         sessionName,
         dayKey,
         startTime,
         endTime
    )

    meta.authority := 60.0
    meta.quality   := 50.0
    meta.confirmed := true
    meta.archived  := false

    SessionObject.new(
         meta,
         kind,
         SessionState.Active,
         time,
         na,
         open,
         high,
         low,
         close,
         "",
         ""
    )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_upsertSession(
     SessionKind kind,
     string sessionName,
     string windowDef,
     int barLocalMinutes,
     string dayKey
     ) =>

    [winStart, winEnd] = f_parseSessionWindow(windowDef)

    bool inWindow = f_isInSession(
         barLocalMinutes,
         winStart,
         winEnd
    )

    if inWindow

        string sessId = f_makeSessionLogicalId(
             sessionName,
             dayKey,
             winStart,
             winEnd
        )

        bool found = false

        int sessCount = array.size(sessionObjects)

        if sessCount > 0

            int scanFrom = math.max(0, sessCount - 8)

            for si = scanFrom to sessCount - 1

                SessionObject sCheck = array.get(sessionObjects, si)

                if sCheck.meta.logicalId == sessId

                    found := true

                    sCheck.highPrice  := math.max(sCheck.highPrice, high)
                    sCheck.lowPrice   := math.min(sCheck.lowPrice, low)
                    sCheck.closePrice := close
                    sCheck.endTime    := time
                    sCheck.state      := SessionState.Active

                    array.set(sessionObjects, si, sCheck)

                    break

        if not found

            SessionObject newSess = f_makeSessionObject(
                 kind,
                 sessionName,
                 dayKey,
                 winStart,
                 winEnd
            )

            array.push(sessionObjects, newSess)

    inWindow

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(sessionObjects) > CORE_MAX_SESSIONS
    array.shift(sessionObjects)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if showKillzones and barstate.isconfirmed

    int barLocalMinutes = f_getBarLocalMinutes(killzoneTimezone)
    string sessionDayKey = f_getSessionDayKey(killzoneTimezone)

    if showAsiaKillzone

        f_upsertSession(
             SessionKind.Asia,
             "ASIA",
             asiaSessionWindow,
             barLocalMinutes,
             sessionDayKey
        )

    if showLondonKillzone

        f_upsertSession(
             SessionKind.London,
             "LONDON",
             londonSessionWindow,
             barLocalMinutes,
             sessionDayKey
        )

    if showNewYorkKillzone

        f_upsertSession(
             SessionKind.NewYork,
             "NY",
             newYorkSessionWindow,
             barLocalMinutes,
             sessionDayKey
        )

    if showLondonCloseKillzone

        f_upsertSession(
             SessionKind.Custom,
             "LONDONCLOSE",
             londonCloseSessionWindow,
             barLocalMinutes,
             sessionDayKey
        )

    if showMYKillzone

        f_upsertSession(
             SessionKind.MY,
             "MY",
             myKillzoneWindow,
             barLocalMinutes,
             sessionDayKey
        )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if showKillzones and barstate.isconfirmed

    int closeScanLocalMinutes = f_getBarLocalMinutes(killzoneTimezone)

    int sessTotal = array.size(sessionObjects)

    if sessTotal > 0

        int closeScanFrom = math.max(0, sessTotal - 8)

        for ci = closeScanFrom to sessTotal - 1

            SessionObject sClose = array.get(sessionObjects, ci)

            if sClose.state == SessionState.Active

                // startTime / endTime hold epoch ms, so the window bounds are
                // re-derived from the SessionKind's configured definition.
                string windowDef = sClose.sessionKind == SessionKind.Asia
                     ? asiaSessionWindow
                     : sClose.sessionKind == SessionKind.London
                     ? londonSessionWindow
                     : sClose.sessionKind == SessionKind.NewYork
                     ? newYorkSessionWindow
                     : londonCloseSessionWindow

                [wStart, wEnd] = f_parseSessionWindow(windowDef)

                bool inWindowNow = f_isInSession(
                     closeScanLocalMinutes,
                     wStart,
                     wEnd
                )

                if not inWindowNow

                    sClose.state := SessionState.Closed

                    array.set(sessionObjects, ci, sClose)

f_vmClearBoxPool(array<box> pool) =>
    while array.size(pool) > 0
        box b = array.pop(pool)
        if not na(b)
            box.delete(b)
    true

f_vmClearLinePool(array<line> pool) =>
    while array.size(pool) > 0
        line l = array.pop(pool)
        if not na(l)
            line.delete(l)
    true

f_vmClearLabelPool(array<label> pool) =>
    while array.size(pool) > 0
        label lb = array.pop(pool)
        if not na(lb)
            label.delete(lb)
    true

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_vmApplyBoxTransparency(color baseColor, bool isMitigated) =>
    color.new(baseColor, f_getBoxTransparency(isMitigated))

f_vmApplyLineTransparency(color baseColor, bool isMitigated) =>
    color.new(baseColor, f_getLineTransparency(isMitigated))

f_vmApplyLabelTransparency(color baseColor, bool isMitigated) =>
    color.new(baseColor, f_getLabelTransparency(isMitigated))

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_vmRenderFVGs() =>

    f_vmClearBoxPool(vmFVGBoxes)

    if enableFVGEngine

        int fvgTotal = array.size(fvgObjects)

        if fvgTotal > 0

            int fvgDrawn = 0

            // Newest-first walk, hard-bounded so a history full of Invalid
            // FVGs cannot turn this into a 1000-iteration scan.
            int fvgScanStop = math.max(0, fvgTotal - FVG_ACTIVE_SCAN)

            for fi = fvgTotal - 1 to fvgScanStop

                if fvgDrawn >= VM_BUDGET_FVG
                    break

                FVGObject fvg = array.get(fvgObjects, fi)

                bool dirVisible = fvg.direction == Direction.Bullish
                     ? showBullishFVG
                     : showBearishFVG

                bool stateVisible = fvg.state != FVGState.Invalid

                if dirVisible and stateVisible

                    bool isMit = fvg.state == FVGState.Mitigated

                    color baseColor = fvg.direction == Direction.Bullish
                         ? bullishFVGColor
                         : bearishFVGColor

                    color fillColor = f_vmApplyBoxTransparency(baseColor, isMit)

                    // Compute right edge based on extend mode
                    int boxRight = bar_index
                    if fvgExtendMode == "Fixed Bars After Current"
                        boxRight := fvg.originBar + fvgFixedExtendBars
                    else if fvgExtendMode == "Until Mitigated" and not isMit
                        boxRight := bar_index
                    else if fvgExtendMode == "Until Mitigated" and isMit
                        boxRight := fvg.mitigationTime
                    else if fvgExtendMode == "Fixed Bars After Mitigation" and isMit
                        boxRight := fvg.mitigationTime + fvgFixedExtendBars

                    // originBar is candle3. The gap physically opens at
                    // candle1, two bars earlier.
                    box newBox = box.new(
                         left         = fvg.originBar - 2,
                         top          = math.max(fvg.top, fvg.bottom),
                         right        = boxRight,
                         bottom       = math.min(fvg.top, fvg.bottom),
                         bgcolor      = fillColor,
                         border_color = fillColor,
                         border_width = 1,
                         xloc         = xloc.bar_index
                    )

                    array.push(vmFVGBoxes, newBox)

                    fvgDrawn += 1

    true

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_vmRenderOBs() =>

    f_vmClearBoxPool(vmOBBoxes)

    if enableOBEngine

        int obTotal = array.size(obObjects)

        if obTotal > 0

            int obDrawn = 0

            // Newest-first walk, hard-bounded for the same reason as FVGs.
            int obScanStop = math.max(0, obTotal - OB_ACTIVE_SCAN)

            for oi = obTotal - 1 to obScanStop

                if obDrawn >= VM_BUDGET_OB
                    break

                OBObject ob = array.get(obObjects, oi)

                bool dirVisible = ob.direction == Direction.Bullish
                     ? showBullishOBs
                     : showBearishOBs

                bool stateVisible = ob.state != OBState.Invalid
                     and ob.state != OBState.Candidate

                if dirVisible and stateVisible

                    bool isMit = ob.state == OBState.Mitigated
                         or ob.state == OBState.Breaker

                    color baseColor = ob.direction == Direction.Bullish
                         ? bullishOBColor
                         : bearishOBColor

                    color fillColor = f_vmApplyBoxTransparency(baseColor, isMit)
                    color lineColor = f_vmApplyLineTransparency(baseColor, isMit)

                    bool isMitOB = ob.state == OBState.Mitigated
                         or ob.state == OBState.Breaker

                    int obRight = ob.originBar + 1
                    if obExtendMode == "Extend to Current Price"
                        obRight := bar_index
                    else if obExtendMode == "Until Mitigated" and not isMitOB
                        obRight := bar_index
                    else if obExtendMode == "Until Mitigated" and isMitOB
                        obRight := bar_index
                    else if obExtendMode == "Fixed Bars After Current"
                        obRight := ob.originBar + obFixedExtendBars
                    else if obExtendMode == "Fixed Bars After Mitigation" and isMitOB
                        obRight := bar_index + obFixedExtendBars

                    box newBox = box.new(
                         left         = ob.originBar,
                         top          = math.max(ob.high, ob.low),
                         right        = obRight,
                         bottom       = math.min(ob.high, ob.low),
                         bgcolor      = fillColor,
                         border_color = lineColor,
                         border_width = 1,
                         xloc         = xloc.bar_index
                    )

                    array.push(vmOBBoxes, newBox)

                    obDrawn += 1

    true

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_vmRenderDealingRange() =>

    f_vmClearLinePool(vmDRLines)
    f_vmClearLabelPool(vmDRLabels)
    f_vmClearBoxPool(vmDRZoneBoxes)

    if enableDealingRangeEngine

        int drTotal = array.size(dealingRangeObjects)

        if drTotal > 0

            DealingRangeObject dr = array.get(
                 dealingRangeObjects,
                 drTotal - 1
            )

            if dr.active

                int leftBar  = bar_index - drLookbackBars
                int rightBar = bar_index + 5

                // ============================================================
                // PREMIUM / DISCOUNT ZONES
                // ============================================================

                if showPDZone

                    box premiumBox = box.new(
                         left         = leftBar,
                         top          = dr.high,
                         right        = rightBar,
                         bottom       = dr.equilibrium,
                         bgcolor      = f_vmApplyBoxTransparency(premiumZoneColor, false),
                         border_color = color.new(color.white, 100),
                         xloc         = xloc.bar_index
                    )

                    array.push(vmDRZoneBoxes, premiumBox)

                    box discountBox = box.new(
                         left         = leftBar,
                         top          = dr.equilibrium,
                         right        = rightBar,
                         bottom       = dr.low,
                         bgcolor      = f_vmApplyBoxTransparency(discountZoneColor, false),
                         border_color = color.new(color.white, 100),
                         xloc         = xloc.bar_index
                    )

                    array.push(vmDRZoneBoxes, discountBox)

                // ============================================================
                // RANGE BOUNDS + EQUILIBRIUM
                // ============================================================

                if showDealingRange

                    line highLine = line.new(
                         x1    = leftBar,
                         y1    = dr.high,
                         x2    = rightBar,
                         y2    = dr.high,
                         color = color.new(color.red, drTransparency),
                         width = 1,
                         style = line.style_solid,
                         xloc  = xloc.bar_index
                    )

                    array.push(vmDRLines, highLine)

                    line lowLine = line.new(
                         x1    = leftBar,
                         y1    = dr.low,
                         x2    = rightBar,
                         y2    = dr.low,
                         color = color.new(color.green, drTransparency),
                         width = 1,
                         style = line.style_solid,
                         xloc  = xloc.bar_index
                    )

                    array.push(vmDRLines, lowLine)

                    line eqLine = line.new(
                         x1    = leftBar,
                         y1    = dr.equilibrium,
                         x2    = rightBar,
                         y2    = dr.equilibrium,
                         color = color.new(equilibriumColor, drTransparency),
                         width = 1,
                         style = line.style_dashed,
                         xloc  = xloc.bar_index
                    )

                    array.push(vmDRLines, eqLine)

                    label eqLabel = label.new(
                         x         = rightBar,
                         y         = dr.equilibrium,
                         text      = "EQ  " + str.tostring(dr.currentLocation),
                         color     = color.new(color.black, 100),
                         textcolor = color.new(equilibriumColor, drTransparency),
                         style     = label.style_label_left,
                         size      = size.tiny,
                         xloc      = xloc.bar_index
                    )

                    array.push(vmDRLabels, eqLabel)

                // ============================================================
                // OTE LEVELS
                // ============================================================

                if showOTE

                    line l62 = line.new(
                         x1    = leftBar,
                         y1    = dr.ote62,
                         x2    = rightBar,
                         y2    = dr.ote62,
                         color = color.new(ote62Color, drTransparency),
                         width = 1,
                         style = line.style_dotted,
                         xloc  = xloc.bar_index
                    )

                    array.push(vmDRLines, l62)

                    line l705 = line.new(
                         x1    = leftBar,
                         y1    = dr.ote705,
                         x2    = rightBar,
                         y2    = dr.ote705,
                         color = color.new(ote705Color, drTransparency),
                         width = 1,
                         style = line.style_dotted,
                         xloc  = xloc.bar_index
                    )

                    array.push(vmDRLines, l705)

                    line l79 = line.new(
                         x1    = leftBar,
                         y1    = dr.ote79,
                         x2    = rightBar,
                         y2    = dr.ote79,
                         color = color.new(ote79Color, drTransparency),
                         width = 1,
                         style = line.style_dotted,
                         xloc  = xloc.bar_index
                    )

                    array.push(vmDRLines, l79)

                    label oteLabel = label.new(
                         x         = rightBar,
                         y         = dr.ote705,
                         text      = "OTE",
                         color     = color.new(color.black, 100),
                         textcolor = color.new(ote705Color, drTransparency),
                         style     = label.style_label_left,
                         size      = size.tiny,
                         xloc      = xloc.bar_index
                    )

                    array.push(vmDRLabels, oteLabel)

    true

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

var box vmLiveAsiaBox      = box(na)
var box vmLiveLondonBox    = box(na)
var box vmLiveNewYorkBox   = box(na)
var box vmLiveLondonCloseBox = box(na)
var box vmLiveMYBox        = box(na)

f_vmTrimKillzonePool() =>
    while array.size(vmKillzoneBoxes) > VM_BUDGET_KILLZONE
        box oldBox = array.shift(vmKillzoneBoxes)
        if not na(oldBox)
            box.delete(oldBox)
    true

f_vmOpenKillzoneBox(color kzColor) =>
    box.new(
         left         = bar_index,
         top          = high,
         right        = bar_index,
         bottom       = low,
         bgcolor      = f_vmApplyBoxTransparency(kzColor, false),
         border_color = color.new(color.white, 100),
         xloc         = xloc.bar_index
    )

f_vmExtendKillzoneBox(box target) =>
    if not na(target)
        box.set_right(target, bar_index)
        box.set_top(target, math.max(box.get_top(target), high))
        box.set_bottom(target, math.min(box.get_bottom(target), low))
    true

f_vmCloseKillzoneBox(
     box target,
     string sessionName,
     color kzColor
     ) =>
    if not na(target)
        array.push(vmKillzoneBoxes, target)

        if killzoneLabelPosition != "HIDE"
            float boxTop    = box.get_top(target)
            float boxBot    = box.get_bottom(target)
            int   boxRight  = box.get_right(target)

            float labelY = killzoneLabelPosition == "Bottom"
                 ? boxBot
                 : killzoneLabelPosition == "Inside"
                      ? (boxTop + boxBot) / 2.0
                      : boxTop

            label kzLabel = label.new(
                 x         = boxRight,
                 y         = labelY,
                 text      = sessionName,
                 color     = color.new(color.black, 100),
                 textcolor = f_vmApplyLabelTransparency(kzColor, false),
                 style     = killzoneLabelPosition == "Bottom"
                      ? label.style_label_up
                      : killzoneLabelPosition == "Inside"
                           ? label.style_label_center
                           : label.style_label_down,
                 size      = size.tiny,
                 xloc      = xloc.bar_index
            )
            array.push(vmKillzoneLabels, kzLabel)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if showKillzones

    int vmBarLocalMinutes = f_getBarLocalMinutes(killzoneTimezone)

    // ========================================================================
    // ASIA
    // ========================================================================

    if showAsiaKillzone

        [vmAsiaStart, vmAsiaEnd] = f_parseSessionWindow(asiaSessionWindow)

        bool vmInAsia = not na(vmAsiaStart) and not na(vmAsiaEnd)
             and f_isInSession(vmBarLocalMinutes, vmAsiaStart, vmAsiaEnd)

        if vmInAsia

            if na(vmLiveAsiaBox)
                vmLiveAsiaBox := f_vmOpenKillzoneBox(asiaKillzoneColor)
            else
                f_vmExtendKillzoneBox(vmLiveAsiaBox)

        else

            if not na(vmLiveAsiaBox)
                f_vmCloseKillzoneBox(vmLiveAsiaBox, "ASIA", asiaKillzoneColor)
                vmLiveAsiaBox := box(na)

    else

        if not na(vmLiveAsiaBox)
            box.delete(vmLiveAsiaBox)
            vmLiveAsiaBox := box(na)

    // ========================================================================
    // LONDON
    // ========================================================================

    if showLondonKillzone

        [vmLondonStart, vmLondonEnd] = f_parseSessionWindow(londonSessionWindow)

        bool vmInLondon = not na(vmLondonStart) and not na(vmLondonEnd)
             and f_isInSession(vmBarLocalMinutes, vmLondonStart, vmLondonEnd)

        if vmInLondon

            if na(vmLiveLondonBox)
                vmLiveLondonBox := f_vmOpenKillzoneBox(londonKillzoneColor)
            else
                f_vmExtendKillzoneBox(vmLiveLondonBox)

        else

            if not na(vmLiveLondonBox)
                f_vmCloseKillzoneBox(vmLiveLondonBox, "LONDON", londonKillzoneColor)
                vmLiveLondonBox := box(na)

    else

        if not na(vmLiveLondonBox)
            box.delete(vmLiveLondonBox)
            vmLiveLondonBox := box(na)

    // ========================================================================
    // NEW YORK
    // ========================================================================

    if showNewYorkKillzone

        [vmNyStart, vmNyEnd] = f_parseSessionWindow(newYorkSessionWindow)

        bool vmInNy = not na(vmNyStart) and not na(vmNyEnd)
             and f_isInSession(vmBarLocalMinutes, vmNyStart, vmNyEnd)

        if vmInNy

            if na(vmLiveNewYorkBox)
                vmLiveNewYorkBox := f_vmOpenKillzoneBox(newYorkKillzoneColor)
            else
                f_vmExtendKillzoneBox(vmLiveNewYorkBox)

        else

            if not na(vmLiveNewYorkBox)
                f_vmCloseKillzoneBox(vmLiveNewYorkBox, "NY", newYorkKillzoneColor)
                vmLiveNewYorkBox := box(na)

    else

        if not na(vmLiveNewYorkBox)
            box.delete(vmLiveNewYorkBox)
            vmLiveNewYorkBox := box(na)

    // ========================================================================
    // LONDON CLOSE
    // ========================================================================

    if showLondonCloseKillzone

        [vmLcStart, vmLcEnd] = f_parseSessionWindow(londonCloseSessionWindow)

        bool vmInLc = not na(vmLcStart) and not na(vmLcEnd)
             and f_isInSession(vmBarLocalMinutes, vmLcStart, vmLcEnd)

        if vmInLc

            if na(vmLiveLondonCloseBox)
                vmLiveLondonCloseBox := f_vmOpenKillzoneBox(londonCloseKillzoneColor)
            else
                f_vmExtendKillzoneBox(vmLiveLondonCloseBox)

        else

            if not na(vmLiveLondonCloseBox)
                f_vmCloseKillzoneBox(vmLiveLondonCloseBox, "LONDONCLOSE", londonCloseKillzoneColor)
                vmLiveLondonCloseBox := box(na)

    else

        if not na(vmLiveLondonCloseBox)
            box.delete(vmLiveLondonCloseBox)
            vmLiveLondonCloseBox := box(na)

    // ========================================================================
    // MY
    // ========================================================================

    if showMYKillzone

        [vmMyStart, vmMyEnd] = f_parseSessionWindow(myKillzoneWindow)

        bool vmInMy = not na(vmMyStart) and not na(vmMyEnd)
             and f_isInSession(vmBarLocalMinutes, vmMyStart, vmMyEnd)

        if vmInMy

            if na(vmLiveMYBox)
                vmLiveMYBox := f_vmOpenKillzoneBox(myKillzoneColor)
            else
                f_vmExtendKillzoneBox(vmLiveMYBox)

        else

            if not na(vmLiveMYBox)
                f_vmCloseKillzoneBox(vmLiveMYBox, "MY", myKillzoneColor)
                vmLiveMYBox := box(na)

    else

        if not na(vmLiveMYBox)
            box.delete(vmLiveMYBox)
            vmLiveMYBox := box(na)

    f_vmTrimKillzonePool()

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if not showKillzones

    if not na(vmLiveAsiaBox)
        box.delete(vmLiveAsiaBox)
        vmLiveAsiaBox := box(na)

    if not na(vmLiveLondonBox)
        box.delete(vmLiveLondonBox)
        vmLiveLondonBox := box(na)

    if not na(vmLiveNewYorkBox)
        box.delete(vmLiveNewYorkBox)
        vmLiveNewYorkBox := box(na)

    if not na(vmLiveLondonCloseBox)
        box.delete(vmLiveLondonCloseBox)
        vmLiveLondonCloseBox := box(na)

    if not na(vmLiveMYBox)
        box.delete(vmLiveMYBox)
        vmLiveMYBox := box(na)

    f_vmClearBoxPool(vmKillzoneBoxes)
    f_vmClearLabelPool(vmKillzoneLabels)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if barstate.islast

    f_vmRenderFVGs()
    f_vmRenderOBs()
    f_vmRenderDealingRange()

string grpMTF = "10 MTF CONTEXT ENGINE"

bool enableMTFEngine = input.bool(
     false,
     "Enable MTF Context Engine",
     group = grpMTF
)

string mtfProfile = input.string(
     "Intraday",
     "MTF Profile",
     options = ["Macro", "Intraday", "Scalping", "Custom"],
     group = grpMTF,
     tooltip = "Macro: W/D/4H/30M | Intraday: D/4H/1H/15M | Scalping: 4H/1H/15M/5M"
)

string customTf1 = input.string(
     "D",
     "Custom TF 1 (Highest)",
     group = grpMTF,
     tooltip = "Only used when Profile = Custom"
)

string customTf2 = input.string(
     "240",
     "Custom TF 2",
     group = grpMTF
)

string customTf3 = input.string(
     "60",
     "Custom TF 3",
     group = grpMTF
)

string customTf4 = input.string(
     "15",
     "Custom TF 4 (Lowest)",
     group = grpMTF
)

bool showMTFBias = input.bool(
     true,
     "Show MTF Bias on Dashboard",
     group = grpMTF
)

bool showMTFAlignment = input.bool(
     true,
     "Show MTF Alignment Indicator",
     group = grpMTF
)

bool showMTFOverlay = input.bool(
     false,
     "Show HTF Overlay on Current Chart",
     group = grpMTF,
     tooltip = "Draw higher timeframe BSL/SSL/FVG/OB levels on the current chart. HTF priority on overlap."
)

color mtfOverlayColor = input.color(
     color.new(color.yellow, 40),
     "HTF Overlay Line Color",
     group = grpMTF
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_resolveMTFProfile() =>
    string tf1 = "D"
    string tf2 = "240"
    string tf3 = "60"
    string tf4 = "15"

    if mtfProfile == "Macro"

        tf1 := "W"
        tf2 := "D"
        tf3 := "240"
        tf4 := "30"

    else if mtfProfile == "Intraday"

        tf1 := "D"
        tf2 := "240"
        tf3 := "60"
        tf4 := "15"

    else if mtfProfile == "Scalping"

        tf1 := "240"
        tf2 := "60"
        tf3 := "15"
        tf4 := "5"

    else if mtfProfile == "Custom"

        tf1 := customTf1
        tf2 := customTf2
        tf3 := customTf3
        tf4 := customTf4

    [tf1, tf2, tf3, tf4]

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_classifyTfRole(string fetchTf, string chartTf) =>
    RelativeTfRole result = RelativeTfRole.Unknown

    int fetchMs = timeframe.in_seconds(fetchTf) * 1000
    int chartMs = timeframe.in_seconds(chartTf) * 1000

    if fetchMs > chartMs

        result := RelativeTfRole.Higher

    else if fetchMs == chartMs

        result := RelativeTfRole.Current

    else

        result := RelativeTfRole.Lower

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

float _mtfBias1 = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, mtfProfile == "Macro" ? "W" : mtfProfile == "Scalping" ? "240" : "D", close > open ? 1.0 : close < open ? -1.0 : 0.0, barmerge.gaps_off, barmerge.lookahead_off)
float _mtfBias2 = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, "240", close > open ? 1.0 : close < open ? -1.0 : 0.0, barmerge.gaps_off, barmerge.lookahead_off)
float _mtfBias3 = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, "60", close > open ? 1.0 : close < open ? -1.0 : 0.0, barmerge.gaps_off, barmerge.lookahead_off)
float _mtfBias4 = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, "15", close > open ? 1.0 : close < open ? -1.0 : 0.0, barmerge.gaps_off, barmerge.lookahead_off)

float _mtfStruct1 = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, mtfProfile == "Macro" ? "W" : mtfProfile == "Scalping" ? "240" : "D", close, barmerge.gaps_off, barmerge.lookahead_off)
float _mtfStruct2 = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, "240", close, barmerge.gaps_off, barmerge.lookahead_off)
float _mtfStruct3 = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, "60", close, barmerge.gaps_off, barmerge.lookahead_off)
float _mtfStruct4 = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, "15", close, barmerge.gaps_off, barmerge.lookahead_off)

float _htfHigh1 = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, mtfProfile == "Macro" ? "W" : mtfProfile == "Scalping" ? "240" : "D", high, barmerge.gaps_off, barmerge.lookahead_off)
float _htfLow1  = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, mtfProfile == "Macro" ? "W" : mtfProfile == "Scalping" ? "240" : "D", low, barmerge.gaps_off, barmerge.lookahead_off)
float _htfHigh2 = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, "240", high, barmerge.gaps_off, barmerge.lookahead_off)
float _htfLow2  = request.security(syminfo.tickerid, "240", low, barmerge.gaps_off, barmerge.lookahead_off)

f_buildMTFContexts() =>

    array.clear(mtfContexts)

    string chartTf = timeframe.period

    [tf1, tf2, tf3, tf4] = f_resolveMTFProfile()

    string[] tfArray = array.new<string>()
    array.push(tfArray, tf1)
    array.push(tfArray, tf2)
    array.push(tfArray, tf3)
    array.push(tfArray, tf4)

    // Bias values from the global fetches above
    array<float> biasArr = array.from(_mtfBias1, _mtfBias2, _mtfBias3, _mtfBias4)
    array<float> structArr = array.from(_mtfStruct1, _mtfStruct2, _mtfStruct3, _mtfStruct4)

    for tfIdx = 0 to 3

        string fetchTf = array.get(tfArray, tfIdx)

        RelativeTfRole role = f_classifyTfRole(fetchTf, chartTf)

        float biasVal = array.get(biasArr, tfIdx)
        Direction bias = biasVal > 0 ? Direction.Bullish : biasVal < 0 ? Direction.Bearish : Direction.Neutral

        float structClose = array.get(structArr, tfIdx)
        float structPrev = tfIdx == 0 ? _mtfStruct1 : tfIdx == 1 ? _mtfStruct2 : tfIdx == 2 ? _mtfStruct3 : _mtfStruct4

        PDLocation pdLoc = PDLocation.Unknown

        ObjectMeta meta = f_newMeta(
             "MTF",
             fetchTf,
             fetchTf,
             time_close,
             close
        )

        meta.authority := role == RelativeTfRole.Higher ? 80.0 : role == RelativeTfRole.Current ? 60.0 : 40.0
        meta.quality   := 60.0
        meta.confirmed := true
        meta.archived  := false

        MTFContextObject ctx = MTFContextObject.new(
             fetchTf,
             ConfirmationState.Confirmed,
             bias,
             bias,
             bias,
             "",
             "",
             "",
             "",
             "",
             "",
             "",
             "",
             "",
             "",
             pdLoc,
             "",
             60.0,
             time_close
        )

        array.push(mtfContexts, ctx)

    true

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_calcMTFAlignment() =>
    float score = 0.0
    int count = array.size(mtfContexts)

    if count > 0

        float totalBias = 0.0

        for i = 0 to count - 1

            MTFContextObject ctx = array.get(mtfContexts, i)

            if ctx.externalBias == Direction.Bullish

                totalBias += 1.0

            else if ctx.externalBias == Direction.Bearish

                totalBias -= 1.0

        score := totalBias / count

    score

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if enableMTFEngine and barstate.isconfirmed

    f_buildMTFContexts()

string grpDOL = "10B DOL ENGINE"

bool enableDOLEngine = input.bool(
     false,
     "Enable DOL Engine",
     group = grpDOL
)

bool showDOLTargets = input.bool(
     true,
     "Show DOL Targets",
     group = grpDOL
)

int dolLookbackBars = input.int(
     100,
     "DOL Lookback (bars)",
     minval = 20,
     maxval = 500,
     group = grpDOL
)

float dolMinScore = input.float(
     30.0,
     "Minimum DOL Score",
     minval = 0.0,
     maxval = 100.0,
     step = 5.0,
     group = grpDOL
)

color dolPrimaryColor = input.color(
     color.new(color.blue, 75),
     "Primary DOL Color",
     group = grpDOL
)

color dolSecondaryColor = input.color(
     color.new(color.orange, 75),
     "Secondary DOL Color",
     group = grpDOL
)

color dolTertiaryColor = input.color(
     color.new(color.gray, 75),
     "Tertiary DOL Color",
     group = grpDOL
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

string grpZigzag = "10A ZIGZAG"

bool showZigZag = input.bool(
     false,
     "Show ZigZag",
     group = grpZigzag,
     tooltip = "Connects confirmed swings with lines. Purely visual — no analysis impact."
)

color zigzagColorBull = input.color(
     color.new(color.green, 60),
     "Bullish ZigZag Color",
     group = grpZigzag
)

color zigzagColorBear = input.color(
     color.new(color.red, 60),
     "Bearish ZigZag Color",
     group = grpZigzag
)

int zigzagWidth = input.int(
     1,
     "ZigZag Width",
     minval = 1,
     maxval = 4,
     group = grpZigzag
)

string zigzagStyleStr = input.string(
     "Solid",
     "ZigZag Line Style",
     options = ["Solid", "Dotted", "Dashed"],
     group = grpZigzag
)

int zigzagMaxSegments = input.int(
     20,
     "Max Visible ZigZag Segments",
     minval = 5,
     maxval = 40,
     group = grpZigzag
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_dolScoreDirectionAlignment(
     LiquidityObject liq,
     Direction marketBias
     ) =>

    float score = 0.0

    // BSL is a bullish target (price goes up to it)
    // SSL is a bearish target (price goes down to it)
    bool liqIsBullishTarget = liq.kind == LiquidityKind.BSL
    bool liqIsBearishTarget = liq.kind == LiquidityKind.SSL

    if marketBias == Direction.Bullish and liqIsBullishTarget

        score := 30.0

    else if marketBias == Direction.Bearish and liqIsBearishTarget

        score := 30.0

    else if marketBias == Direction.Bullish and liqIsBearishTarget

        score := -15.0

    else if marketBias == Direction.Bearish and liqIsBullishTarget

        score := -15.0

    score

f_dolScoreAuthority(
     LiquidityObject liq
     ) =>

    float score = 0.0

    // Authority is already stored in meta (0-100 scale)
    // Map to ±20 range
    score := (liq.meta.authority / 100.0) * 20.0

    score

f_dolScoreFreshness(
     LiquidityObject liq
     ) =>

    float score = 0.0

    int barsSinceCreation = bar_index - liq.meta.createdBar

    if liq.state == LiquidityLifecycle.Fresh

        score := 20.0

    else if liq.state == LiquidityLifecycle.Touched

        score := 10.0

    else if liq.state == LiquidityLifecycle.SweepCandidate

        score := 5.0

    else if liq.state == LiquidityLifecycle.ValidatedSweep

        score := 0.0

    else

        score := -20.0

    // Decay with age
    if barsSinceCreation > 200

        score := score * 0.5

    else if barsSinceCreation > 100

        score := score * 0.75

    score

float dolScoreAtr = ta.atr(14)

f_dolScoreDistance(
     float targetPrice,
     float currentPrice,
     float atrVal
     ) =>

    float score = 0.0

    float distance = math.abs(targetPrice - currentPrice)

    if atrVal > 0

        float distanceInATR = distance / atrVal

        // Closer is better (up to 5 ATR)
        if distanceInATR <= 2.0

            score := 15.0

        else if distanceInATR <= 5.0

            score := 10.0

        else if distanceInATR <= 10.0

            score := 5.0

        else

            score := 0.0

    score

f_dolScorePDLocation(
     PDLocation liqLocation,
     PDLocation currentLocation
     ) =>

    float score = 0.0

    if liqLocation == PDLocation.Premium and currentLocation == PDLocation.Discount

        // Price in discount, target in premium = bullish move needed
        score := 15.0

    else if liqLocation == PDLocation.Discount and currentLocation == PDLocation.Premium

        // Price in premium, target in discount = bearish move needed
        score := 15.0

    else if liqLocation == PDLocation.Equilibrium

        score := 7.5

    score

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================
f_classifyDOLPriority(float score) =>
    DOLPriority result = DOLPriority.Tertiary

    if score >= 70.0
        result := DOLPriority.Primary
    else if score >= 40.0
        result := DOLPriority.Secondary

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_makeDOLLogicalId(string liqId, int barTime) =>
    "DOL|" + liqId + "|" + str.tostring(barTime)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

while array.size(dolObjects) > CORE_MAX_DOLS
    array.shift(dolObjects)

if map.size(dolObjectById) > CORE_MAX_DOLS
    map.clear(dolObjectById)
    for _doli = 0 to array.size(dolObjects) - 1
        DOLObject _dol = array.get(dolObjects, _doli)
        map.put(dolObjectById, _dol.meta.logicalId, _dol)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if enableDOLEngine and barstate.isconfirmed

    int liqTotal = array.size(liquidityObjects)

    if liqTotal > 0

        // Get current market bias from the latest structure context
        Direction marketBias = Direction.Neutral

        int ctxTotal = array.size(structureContexts)

        if ctxTotal > 0

            // Use the most recent context's bias
            int ctxIdx = math.max(0, ctxTotal - 1)
            StructureContextObject latestCtx = array.get(structureContexts, ctxIdx)
            marketBias := latestCtx.bias

        // Get current PD location from the latest dealing range
        PDLocation currentPD = PDLocation.Unknown

        int drTotal = array.size(dealingRangeObjects)

        if drTotal > 0

            DealingRangeObject latestDR = array.get(dealingRangeObjects, drTotal - 1)
            currentPD := latestDR.currentLocation

        // Scan liquidity objects (capped)
        int scanCap = math.min(liqTotal, 80)
        int dolCreated = 0
        int maxDOLPerBar = 2

        for liqIdx = 0 to scanCap - 1

            if dolCreated >= maxDOLPerBar
                break

            LiquidityObject liq = array.get(liquidityObjects, liqIdx)

            // Skip consumed/invalid liquidity
            if liq.state == LiquidityLifecycle.Consumed or liq.state == LiquidityLifecycle.Invalid
                continue

            // Score the candidate (inline to avoid CW10003)
            float dirScore    = f_dolScoreDirectionAlignment(liq, marketBias)
            float authScore   = f_dolScoreAuthority(liq)
            float freshScore  = f_dolScoreFreshness(liq)
            float distScore   = f_dolScoreDistance(liq.price, close, dolScoreAtr)

            // Compute PD location of the liquidity target relative to latest dealing range
            PDLocation liqPD = PDLocation.Unknown
            if drTotal > 0
                DealingRangeObject drRef = array.get(dealingRangeObjects, drTotal - 1)
                float drHigh = drRef.high
                float drLow  = drRef.low
                float drMid  = (drHigh + drLow) / 2.0
                float dr66   = drLow + (drHigh - drLow) * 0.66
                float dr33   = drLow + (drHigh - drLow) * 0.33
                if liq.price >= dr66
                    liqPD := PDLocation.Premium
                else if liq.price <= dr33
                    liqPD := PDLocation.Discount
                else
                    liqPD := PDLocation.Equilibrium

            float pdScore     = f_dolScorePDLocation(liqPD, currentPD)

            float score = dirScore + authScore + freshScore + distScore + pdScore
            score := math.max(score, 0.0)
            score := math.min(score, 100.0)

            if score >= dolMinScore

                DOLPriority priority = f_classifyDOLPriority(score)

                string dolId = f_makeDOLLogicalId(liq.meta.logicalId, time_close)

                if not map.contains(
                     dolObjectById,
                     dolId
                )

                    ObjectMeta meta = f_newMeta(
                         "DOL",
                         str.tostring(priority),
                         timeframe.period,
                         time_close,
                         liq.price
                    )

                    meta.authority := liq.meta.authority
                    meta.quality   := score
                    meta.confirmed := true
                    meta.archived  := false

                    DOLObject newDOL = DOLObject.new(
                         meta,
                         liq.kind == LiquidityKind.BSL or liq.kind == LiquidityKind.EQH or liq.kind == LiquidityKind.PDH or liq.kind == LiquidityKind.PWH or liq.kind == LiquidityKind.SessionHigh
                              ? Direction.Bullish
                              : Direction.Bearish,
                         liq.meta.logicalId,
                         liq.price,
                         score,
                         priority,
                         DOLState.Active
                    )

                    array.push(
                         dolObjects,
                         newDOL
                    )

                    map.put(
                         dolObjectById,
                         dolId,
                         newDOL
                    )

                    dolCreated += 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

// ============================================================================

var array<line>  vmStructureLines    = array.new<line>()
var array<label> vmStructureLabels   = array.new<label>()

var array<label> vmDisplacementLabels = array.new<label>()
var array<box>   vmDisplacementBoxes  = array.new<box>()

var array<line>  vmZigZagLines       = array.new<line>()
var array<label> vmSwingLabels       = array.new<label>()

var array<line>  vmDOLLines          = array.new<line>()
var array<label> vmDOLLabels         = array.new<label>()

var array<label> vmMTFLabels         = array.new<label>()

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

const int VM_BUDGET_STRUCTURE   = 20
const int VM_BUDGET_DISPLACEMENT = 8
const int VM_BUDGET_ZIGZAG     = 25
const int VM_BUDGET_SWING      = 25
const int VM_BUDGET_DOL        = 12
const int VM_BUDGET_MTF        = 8

// ============================================================================

f_vmRenderStructureEvents() =>

    f_vmClearLinePool(vmStructureLines)
    f_vmClearLabelPool(vmStructureLabels)

    if structureDevShowEvents

        int structTotal = array.size(structureEvents)

        if structTotal > 0

            int drawn = 0
            int scanCap = math.min(structTotal, 100)

            // Iterate newest first
            for si = structTotal - 1 to math.max(0, structTotal - scanCap)

                if drawn >= VM_BUDGET_STRUCTURE
                    break

                StructureEventObject se = array.get(
                     structureEvents,
                     si
                )

                if se.lifecycle != EventLifecycle.Confirmed and se.lifecycle != EventLifecycle.Archived
                    continue

                bool isMSS = se.eventType == StructureEventType.MSS
                bool isCHoCH = se.eventType == StructureEventType.CHOCH
                bool isBOS = se.eventType == StructureEventType.BOS

                color lineColor = isMSS
                     ? color.new(color.purple, 50)
                     : isCHoCH
                          ? color.new(color.red, 50)
                          : color.new(color.blue, 50)

                color labelColor = isMSS
                     ? color.new(color.purple, 70)
                     : isCHoCH
                          ? color.new(color.red, 70)
                          : color.new(color.blue, 70)

                line breakLine = line.new(
                     x1    = se.meta.createdBar,
                     y1    = se.breakLevel,
                     x2    = extendStructureToPrice ? bar_index + 5 : se.meta.createdBar + 1,
                     y2    = se.breakLevel,
                     color = f_vmApplyLineTransparency(lineColor, false),
                     width = isMSS ? 2 : 1,
                     style = isMSS ? line.style_solid : line.style_dashed,
                     xloc  = xloc.bar_index
                )

                array.push(vmStructureLines, breakLine)

                string labelText = str.tostring(se.eventType) + " | " + str.tostring(se.degree)

                label evLabel = label.new(
                     x         = se.meta.createdBar,
                     y         = se.breakLevel,
                     text      = labelText,
                     color     = color.new(color.black, 100),
                     textcolor = f_vmApplyLabelTransparency(labelColor, false),
                     style     = label.style_label_down,
                     size      = size.tiny,
                     xloc      = xloc.bar_index
                )

                array.push(vmStructureLabels, evLabel)

                drawn += 1

    true

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_vmRenderDisplacementMarkers() =>

    f_vmClearLabelPool(vmDisplacementLabels)
    f_vmClearBoxPool(vmDisplacementBoxes)

    if array.size(displacementObjects) > 0

        int dispTotal = array.size(displacementObjects)
        int drawn = 0
        int scanCap = math.min(dispTotal, 50)

        string dispMode = displacementDisplayMode
        bool showMarker = dispMode != "Box Only"
        bool showBox    = dispMode != "Marker Only"

        string markerSizeVal = displacementMarkerSizeStr
        string dispLabelSize = markerSizeVal == "Tiny" ? size.tiny
             : markerSizeVal == "Normal" ? size.normal
             : size.small

        for di = dispTotal - 1 to math.max(0, dispTotal - scanCap)

            if drawn >= VM_BUDGET_DISPLACEMENT
                break

            DisplacementObject disp = array.get(
                 displacementObjects,
                 di
            )

            if disp.state != DisplacementState.Confirmed
                continue

            bool isBullish = disp.direction == Direction.Bullish
            color markerColor = isBullish
                 ? color.new(color.green, 50)
                 : color.new(color.red, 50)

            if showMarker
                float markerY = isBullish
                     ? disp.highPrice * 1.001
                     : disp.lowPrice * 0.999

                label marker = label.new(
                     x         = disp.candleBar,
                     y         = markerY,
                     text      = "DISP",
                     color     = color.new(color.black, 100),
                     textcolor = f_vmApplyLabelTransparency(markerColor, false),
                     style     = isBullish ? label.style_label_up : label.style_label_down,
                     size      = dispLabelSize,
                     xloc      = xloc.bar_index
                )
                array.push(vmDisplacementLabels, marker)

            if showBox
                box dispBox = box.new(
                     left    = disp.candleBar,
                     top     = disp.highPrice,
                     right   = disp.candleBar + 1,
                     bottom  = disp.lowPrice,
                     bgcolor = f_vmApplyLineTransparency(displacementBoxColor, false),
                     border_color = f_vmApplyLineTransparency(markerColor, false),
                     border_width = 1,
                     xloc    = xloc.bar_index
                )
                array.push(vmDisplacementBoxes, dispBox)

            drawn += 1

    true

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_vmRenderZigZag() =>

    f_vmClearLinePool(vmZigZagLines)

    int seqTotal = array.size(swingSequenceNodes)

    if seqTotal >= 2

        int drawn = 0
        int cap = math.min(seqTotal - 1, zigzagMaxSegments)

        for zi = 1 to cap

            if drawn >= zigzagMaxSegments
                break

            SwingSequenceNode prevNode = array.get(
                 swingSequenceNodes,
                 zi - 1
            )

            SwingSequenceNode currNode = array.get(
                 swingSequenceNodes,
                 zi
            )

            // Only connect alternating High→Low or Low→High
            if prevNode.side == currNode.side
                continue

            bool isBullish = currNode.relation == SwingRelation.HH or currNode.relation == SwingRelation.HL

            color zigColor = isBullish ? zigzagColorBull : zigzagColorBear

            if not na(prevNode.pivotTime) and not na(currNode.pivotTime)

                line zigLine = line.new(
                     x1    = prevNode.pivotTime,
                     y1    = prevNode.price,
                     x2    = currNode.pivotTime,
                     y2    = currNode.price,
                     color = f_vmApplyLineTransparency(zigColor, false),
                     width = zigzagWidth,
                     style = zigzagStyleStr == "Dotted" ? line.style_dotted : zigzagStyleStr == "Dashed" ? line.style_dashed : line.style_solid,
                     xloc  = xloc.bar_time
                )

                array.push(vmZigZagLines, zigLine)

                drawn += 1

    true

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_vmRenderSwingMarkers() =>

    f_vmClearLabelPool(vmSwingLabels)

    string swingLabelSize = swingLabelSizeStr == "Tiny"
         ? size.tiny
         : swingLabelSizeStr == "Normal"
              ? size.normal
              : size.small

    int swingTotal = array.size(swingObjects)

    if swingTotal > 0 and showSwingMarkers

        int drawn = 0
        int drawnExternal = 0
        int drawnInternal = 0
        int drawnMicro    = 0
        int scanCap = math.min(swingTotal, VM_BUDGET_SWING)

        // Track drawn pivot+side combos for dedup (highest priority wins)
        array<int> drawnPivotKeys = array.new<int>()

        for si = swingTotal - 1 to math.max(0, swingTotal - scanCap)

            if drawn >= VM_BUDGET_SWING
                break

            SwingObject swing = array.get(swingObjects, si)

            // Degree visibility filter + per-degree max
            bool degreeVisible = false
            if swing.degree == SwingDegree.External
                degreeVisible := showExternalSwing and drawnExternal < maxVisibleExternalSwing
            else if swing.degree == SwingDegree.Internal
                degreeVisible := showInternalSwing and drawnInternal < maxVisibleInternalSwing
            else
                degreeVisible := showMicroSwing and drawnMicro < maxVisibleMicroSwing

            if not degreeVisible
                continue

            // Priority dedup: same pivotBar+side = skip lower priority
            int pivotKey = swing.pivotBar * 10 + (swing.side == SwingSide.High ? 1 : 2)
            int degreeRank = swing.degree == SwingDegree.External ? 1
                 : swing.degree == SwingDegree.Internal ? 2 : 3

            bool alreadyDrawn = false
            int existingIdx = array.indexof(drawnPivotKeys, pivotKey)
            if existingIdx >= 0
                alreadyDrawn := true

            if alreadyDrawn
                continue

            array.push(drawnPivotKeys, pivotKey)

            bool isHigh = swing.side == SwingSide.High
            color markerColor = isHigh
                 ? color.new(color.blue, 60)
                 : color.new(color.orange, 60)

            string labelText = str.tostring(swing.degree)

            // Y-offset per degree to prevent overlap
            float yOffset = swing.degree == SwingDegree.Internal
                 ? (isHigh ? swing.price * 1.002 : swing.price * 0.998)
                 : swing.degree == SwingDegree.Micro
                      ? (isHigh ? swing.price * 1.004 : swing.price * 0.996)
                      : swing.price

            label swingLabel = label.new(
                 x         = swing.pivotBar,
                 y         = yOffset,
                 text      = labelText,
                 color     = color.new(color.black, 100),
                 textcolor = f_vmApplyLabelTransparency(markerColor, false),
                 style     = isHigh ? label.style_label_down : label.style_label_up,
                 size      = swingLabelSize,
                 xloc      = xloc.bar_index
            )

            array.push(vmSwingLabels, swingLabel)

            drawn += 1
            if swing.degree == SwingDegree.External
                drawnExternal += 1
            else if swing.degree == SwingDegree.Internal
                drawnInternal += 1
            else
                drawnMicro += 1

    true

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_vmRenderDOLTargets() =>

    f_vmClearLinePool(vmDOLLines)
    f_vmClearLabelPool(vmDOLLabels)

    if showDOLTargets

        int dolTotal = array.size(dolObjects)

        if dolTotal > 0

            int drawn = 0
            int scanCap = math.min(dolTotal, VM_BUDGET_DOL)

            for di = dolTotal - 1 to math.max(0, dolTotal - scanCap)

                if drawn >= VM_BUDGET_DOL
                    break

                DOLObject dol = array.get(dolObjects, di)

                if dol.state == DOLState.Invalid
                    continue

                color lineColor = dol.priority == DOLPriority.Primary
                     ? dolPrimaryColor
                     : dol.priority == DOLPriority.Secondary
                          ? dolSecondaryColor
                          : dolTertiaryColor

                line dolLine = line.new(
                     x1    = dol.meta.createdBar,
                     y1    = dol.targetPrice,
                     x2    = bar_index + 10,
                     y2    = dol.targetPrice,
                     color = f_vmApplyLineTransparency(lineColor, false),
                     width = dol.priority == DOLPriority.Primary ? 2 : 1,
                     style = line.style_dotted,
                     xloc  = xloc.bar_index
                )

                array.push(vmDOLLines, dolLine)

                label dolLabel = label.new(
                     x         = bar_index + 1,
                     y         = dol.targetPrice,
                     text      = "DOL " + str.tostring(dol.priority) + " | " + str.tostring(dol.direction) + " | " + str.tostring(dol.targetPrice, format.mintick),
                     color     = color.new(color.black, 100),
                     textcolor = f_vmApplyLabelTransparency(lineColor, false),
                     style     = label.style_label_left,
                     size      = size.tiny,
                     xloc      = xloc.bar_index
                )

                array.push(vmDOLLabels, dolLabel)

                drawn += 1

    true

// ============================================================================
