f_vmRenderMTFBias() =>

    f_vmClearLabelPool(vmMTFLabels)

    if showMTFBias and enableMTFEngine

        int ctxTotal = array.size(mtfContexts)

        if ctxTotal > 0

            int drawn = 0

            for ci = 0 to ctxTotal - 1

                if drawn >= VM_BUDGET_MTF
                    break

                MTFContextObject ctx = array.get(mtfContexts, ci)

                string biasText = str.tostring(ctx.originTf) + ": "

                if ctx.externalBias == Direction.Bullish
                    biasText += "BULL"
                else if ctx.externalBias == Direction.Bearish
                    biasText += "BEAR"
                else
                    biasText += "NEUT"

                color biasColor = ctx.externalBias == Direction.Bullish
                     ? color.new(color.green, 70)
                     : ctx.externalBias == Direction.Bearish
                          ? color.new(color.red, 70)
                          : color.new(color.gray, 70)

                label mtfLabel = label.new(
                     x         = bar_index - ctxTotal + ci + 1,
                     y         = high * 1.005,
                     text      = biasText,
                     color     = color.new(color.black, 100),
                     textcolor = biasColor,
                     style     = label.style_label_up,
                     size      = size.tiny,
                     xloc      = xloc.bar_index
                )

                array.push(vmMTFLabels, mtfLabel)

                drawn += 1

    true

var array<line>  vmMTFOverlayLines = array.new<line>()
var array<label> vmMTFOverlayLabels = array.new<label>()

f_vmRenderMTFOverlay() =>
    f_vmClearLinePool(vmMTFOverlayLines)
    f_vmClearLabelPool(vmMTFOverlayLabels)
    if showMTFOverlay and enableMTFEngine
        string tf1N = mtfProfile == "Macro" ? "W" : mtfProfile == "Scalping" ? "4H" : "D"
        color lc = color.new(mtfOverlayColor, 20)
        if not na(_htfHigh1)
            array.push(vmMTFOverlayLines, line.new(bar_index - 20, _htfHigh1, bar_index, _htfHigh1, color = lc, width = 1, style = line.style_dotted, xloc = xloc.bar_index))
            array.push(vmMTFOverlayLabels, label.new(bar_index, _htfHigh1, tf1N + " H", color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = lc, style = label.style_label_left, size = size.tiny, xloc = xloc.bar_index))
        if not na(_htfLow1)
            array.push(vmMTFOverlayLines, line.new(bar_index - 20, _htfLow1, bar_index, _htfLow1, color = lc, width = 1, style = line.style_dotted, xloc = xloc.bar_index))
            array.push(vmMTFOverlayLabels, label.new(bar_index, _htfLow1, tf1N + " L", color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = lc, style = label.style_label_left, size = size.tiny, xloc = xloc.bar_index))
        if not na(_htfHigh2)
            bool dupH1 = not na(_htfHigh1) and math.abs(_htfHigh2 - _htfHigh1) / _htfHigh2 < 0.001
            bool dupH2 = not na(_htfLow1) and math.abs(_htfHigh2 - _htfLow1) / _htfHigh2 < 0.001
            if not dupH1 and not dupH2
                array.push(vmMTFOverlayLines, line.new(bar_index - 20, _htfHigh2, bar_index, _htfHigh2, color = lc, width = 1, style = line.style_dotted, xloc = xloc.bar_index))
                array.push(vmMTFOverlayLabels, label.new(bar_index, _htfHigh2, "4H H", color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = lc, style = label.style_label_left, size = size.tiny, xloc = xloc.bar_index))
        if not na(_htfLow2)
            bool dupL1 = not na(_htfLow1) and math.abs(_htfLow2 - _htfLow1) / _htfLow2 < 0.001
            bool dupL2 = not na(_htfHigh1) and math.abs(_htfLow2 - _htfHigh1) / _htfLow2 < 0.001
            if not dupL1 and not dupL2
                array.push(vmMTFOverlayLines, line.new(bar_index - 20, _htfLow2, bar_index, _htfLow2, color = lc, width = 1, style = line.style_dotted, xloc = xloc.bar_index))
                array.push(vmMTFOverlayLabels, label.new(bar_index, _htfLow2, "4H L", color = color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor = lc, style = label.style_label_left, size = size.tiny, xloc = xloc.bar_index))
    true
// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if barstate.islast

    f_vmRenderFVGs()
    f_vmRenderOBs()
    f_vmRenderDealingRange()
    f_vmRenderStructureEvents()

    if showDisplacementMarkers
        f_vmRenderDisplacementMarkers()

    if showZigZag
        f_vmRenderZigZag()
    f_vmRenderSwingMarkers()
    f_vmRenderDOLTargets()
    f_vmRenderMTFBias()
    f_vmRenderMTFOverlay()

string grpDashboard = "11 DASHBOARD"

bool enableDashboard = input.bool(
     false,
     "Enable Dashboard",
     group = grpDashboard
)

string dashboardPosition = input.string(
     "Top Right",
     "Dashboard Position",
     options = ["Top Right", "Top Left", "Bottom Right", "Bottom Left"],
     group = grpDashboard
)

int dashboardColumns = input.int(
     2,
     "Dashboard Columns",
     minval = 1,
     maxval = 4,
     group = grpDashboard
)

string dashboardSizeStr = input.string(
     "Medium",
     "Dashboard Size",
     options = ["Small", "Medium", "Large"],
     group = grpDashboard,
     tooltip = "Controls text size in the dashboard table."
)

bool showDashBias = input.bool(
     true,
     "Show Bias Section",
     group = grpDashboard

bool showDashStructure = input.bool(
     true,
     "Show Structure Section",
     group = grpDashboard

     true,
     "Show Premium/Discount Section",
     group = grpDashboard

     true,

     group = grpDashboard

     true,

     true,
     "Show DOL Section",
     group = grpDashboard

var table dashTable = na

f_getDashboardPosition() =>
    string result = "top_right"

    if dashboardPosition == "Top Left"
        result := "top_left"
    else if dashboardPosition == "Bottom Right"
        result := "bottom_right"
    else if dashboardPosition == "Bottom Left"
        result := "bottom_left"

    result

f_getDashboardTextSize() =>
    string result = "small"

    if dashboardSizeStr == "Small"
        result := "tiny"
    else if dashboardSizeStr == "Large"
        result := "normal"

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_buildDashboardBias(table t, int row) =>
    string dashTS = f_getDashboardTextSize()
    int r = row
    table.cell(t, 0, r, "BIAS", text_color = color.white, text_size = dashTS, bgcolor = color.new(color.gray, 60))

    Direction extBias = Direction.Neutral

    if array.size(structureContexts) > 0

        // Find the External degree context
        for ci = 0 to array.size(structureContexts) - 1
            StructureContextObject ctx = array.get(structureContexts, ci)
            if ctx.degree == SwingDegree.External
                extBias := ctx.bias
                break

    string biasText = extBias == Direction.Bullish ? "BULLISH" : extBias == Direction.Bearish ? "BEARISH" : "NEUTRAL"
    color biasColor = extBias == Direction.Bullish ? color.green : extBias == Direction.Bearish ? color.red : color.gray

    table.cell(t, 1, r, biasText, text_color = biasColor, text_size = dashTS)
    r + 1

f_buildDashboardStructure(table t, int row) =>
    string dashTS = f_getDashboardTextSize()
    int r = row
    table.cell(t, 0, r, "STRUCTURE", text_color = color.white, text_size = dashTS, bgcolor = color.new(color.gray, 60))

    int structCount = array.size(structureEvents)
    string structText = "Events: " + str.tostring(structCount)

    if structCount > 0
        StructureEventObject last = array.get(structureEvents, structCount - 1)
        structText := str.tostring(last.eventType) + " | " + str.tostring(last.degree)

    table.cell(t, 1, r, structText, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashTS)
    r + 1

f_buildDashboardLiquidity(table t, int row) =>
    string dashTS = f_getDashboardTextSize()
    int r = row
    table.cell(t, 0, r, "LIQUIDITY", text_color = color.white, text_size = dashTS, bgcolor = color.new(color.gray, 60))

    int liqCount = array.size(liquidityObjects)
    int sweepCount = array.size(sweepEvents)

    table.cell(t, 1, r, "Active: " + str.tostring(liqCount) + " | Sweeps: " + str.tostring(sweepCount), text_color = color.white, text_size = dashTS)
    r + 1

f_buildDashboardPD(table t, int row) =>
    string dashTS = f_getDashboardTextSize()
    int r = row
    table.cell(t, 0, r, "PD LOCATION", text_color = color.white, text_size = dashTS, bgcolor = color.new(color.gray, 60))

    string pdText = "Unknown"

    if array.size(dealingRangeObjects) > 0
        DealingRangeObject dr = array.get(dealingRangeObjects, array.size(dealingRangeObjects) - 1)
        pdText := str.tostring(dr.currentLocation)

    color pdColor = pdText == "Premium" ? color.red : pdText == "Discount" ? color.green : color.gray

    table.cell(t, 1, r, pdText, text_color = pdColor, text_size = dashTS)
    r + 1

f_buildDashboardMTF(table t, int row) =>
    string dashTS = f_getDashboardTextSize()
    int r = row
    table.cell(t, 0, r, "MTF", text_color = color.white, text_size = dashTS, bgcolor = color.new(color.gray, 60))

    string mtfText = "OFF"

    if array.size(mtfContexts) > 0

        float alignment = f_calcMTFAlignment()
        mtfText := "Align: " + str.tostring(math.round(alignment * 100) / 100)

    table.cell(t, 1, r, mtfText, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashTS)
    r + 1

f_buildDashboardObjects(table t, int row) =>
    string dashTS = f_getDashboardTextSize()
    int r = row
    table.cell(t, 0, r, "OBJECTS", text_color = color.white, text_size = dashTS, bgcolor = color.new(color.gray, 60))

    string objText = "Sw:" + str.tostring(array.size(swingObjects))
    objText += " Liq:" + str.tostring(array.size(liquidityObjects))
    objText += " Str:" + str.tostring(array.size(structureEvents))
    objText += " FVG:" + str.tostring(array.size(fvgObjects))
    objText += " OB:" + str.tostring(array.size(obObjects))

    table.cell(t, 1, r, objText, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashTS)
    r + 1

f_buildDashboardDOL(table t, int row) =>
    string dashTS = f_getDashboardTextSize()
    int r = row
    table.cell(t, 0, r, "DOL", text_color = color.white, text_size = dashTS, bgcolor = color.new(color.gray, 60))

    string dolText = "OFF"

    if array.size(dolObjects) > 0

        int dolCount = array.size(dolObjects)
        DOLObject primaryDOL = array.get(dolObjects, dolCount - 1)
        dolText := str.tostring(primaryDOL.priority) + " @ " + str.tostring(primaryDOL.targetPrice, format.mintick)

    table.cell(t, 1, r, dolText, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashTS)
    r + 1

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if enableDashboard and barstate.islast

    if not na(dashTable)
        table.delete(dashTable)

    dashTable := table.new(
         f_getDashboardPosition(),
         dashboardColumns,
         10,
         bgcolor = color.new(color.black, 30),
         border_width = 1,
         border_color = color.new(color.gray, 50),
         frame_width = 1,
         frame_color = color.new(color.gray, 50)
    )

    int currentRow = 0

    if showDashBias
        currentRow := f_buildDashboardBias(dashTable, currentRow)

    if showDashStructure
        currentRow := f_buildDashboardStructure(dashTable, currentRow)

    if showDashLiquidity
        currentRow := f_buildDashboardLiquidity(dashTable, currentRow)

    if showDashPD
        currentRow := f_buildDashboardPD(dashTable, currentRow)

    if showDashMTF
        currentRow := f_buildDashboardMTF(dashTable, currentRow)

    if showDashObjects
        currentRow := f_buildDashboardObjects(dashTable, currentRow)

    if showDashDOL
        currentRow := f_buildDashboardDOL(dashTable, currentRow)

// ============================================================================

string grpAlerts = "14 ALERT ENGINE"

bool enableAlerts = input.bool(
     false,
     "Enable Alert Engine",
     group = grpAlerts
)

bool alertSweep = input.bool(
     true,
     "Alert on Sweep",
     group = grpAlerts
)

bool alertStructure = input.bool(
     true,
     "Alert on BOS/CHoCH/MSS",
     group = grpAlerts
)

bool alertDisplacement = input.bool(
     true,
     "Alert on Displacement",
     group = grpAlerts
)

bool alertFVG = input.bool(
     true,
     "Alert on FVG Created",
     group = grpAlerts
)

bool alertOB = input.bool(
     true,
     "Alert on OB Created",
     group = grpAlerts
)

bool alertMitigation = input.bool(
     true,
     "Alert on FVG/OB Mitigation",
     group = grpAlerts
)

bool alertDOL = input.bool(
     true,
     "Alert on DOL Target Reached",
     group = grpAlerts
)

bool alertKillzone = input.bool(
     false,
     "Alert on Killzone Entry/Exit",
     group = grpAlerts
)

bool alertMTFBias = input.bool(
     false,
     "Alert on MTF Bias Change",
     group = grpAlerts
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

var Direction prevExternalBias = Direction.Neutral
var bool prevInKillzone = false

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_isInKillzoneForAlert(SessionKind kind) =>
    bool result = false

    int localMin = f_getBarLocalMinutes(killzoneTimezone)

    if kind == SessionKind.Asia
        [s, e] = f_parseSessionWindow(asiaSessionWindow)
        result := not na(s) and not na(e) and f_isInSession(localMin, s, e)
    else if kind == SessionKind.London
        [s, e] = f_parseSessionWindow(londonSessionWindow)
        result := not na(s) and not na(e) and f_isInSession(localMin, s, e)
    else if kind == SessionKind.NewYork
        [s, e] = f_parseSessionWindow(newYorkSessionWindow)
        result := not na(s) and not na(e) and f_isInSession(localMin, s, e)
    else if kind == SessionKind.Custom
        [s, e] = f_parseSessionWindow(londonCloseSessionWindow)
        result := not na(s) and not na(e) and f_isInSession(localMin, s, e)
    else if kind == SessionKind.MY
        [s, e] = f_parseSessionWindow(myKillzoneWindow)
        result := not na(s) and not na(e) and f_isInSession(localMin, s, e)

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

float alertAtr = ta.atr(14)

if enableAlerts and barstate.isconfirmed

    // ========================================================================
    // SWEEP ALERTS
    // ========================================================================

    if alertSweep and array.size(sweepEvents) > 0

        SweepEventObject latestSweep = array.get(
             sweepEvents,
             array.size(sweepEvents) - 1
        )

        if latestSweep.state == SweepLifecycle.Validated
             and not na(latestSweep.validationTime)
             and latestSweep.validationTime == time_close

            string msg = "SWEEP " + str.tostring(latestSweep.sourceKind)
                 + " @ " + str.tostring(latestSweep.levelPrice)
                 + " | " + str.tostring(timeframe.period)
                 + " | " + syminfo.tickerid

            alert(msg, alert.freq_once_per_bar)

    // ========================================================================
    // STRUCTURE EVENT ALERTS
    // ========================================================================

    if alertStructure and array.size(structureEvents) > 0

        StructureEventObject latestEvent = array.get(
             structureEvents,
             array.size(structureEvents) - 1
        )

        if latestEvent.lifecycle == EventLifecycle.Confirmed
             and latestEvent.meta.createdBar == bar_index

            string msg = str.tostring(latestEvent.eventType)
                 + " " + str.tostring(latestEvent.direction)
                 + " @ " + str.tostring(latestEvent.breakLevel)
                 + " | " + str.tostring(latestEvent.degree)
                 + " | " + timeframe.period
                 + " | " + syminfo.tickerid

            alert(msg, alert.freq_once_per_bar)

    // ========================================================================
    // DISPLACEMENT ALERTS
    // ========================================================================

    if alertDisplacement and array.size(displacementObjects) > 0

        DisplacementObject latestDisp = array.get(
             displacementObjects,
             array.size(displacementObjects) - 1
        )

        if latestDisp.state == DisplacementState.Confirmed
             and latestDisp.candleBar == bar_index

            string msg = "DISPLACEMENT " + str.tostring(latestDisp.direction)
                 + " | Body%: " + str.tostring(math.round(latestDisp.bodyPercent))
                 + " | ATR Ratio: " + str.tostring(math.round(latestDisp.atrRatio * 10) / 10)
                 + " | " + timeframe.period
                 + " | " + syminfo.tickerid

            alert(msg, alert.freq_once_per_bar)

    // ========================================================================
    // FVG ALERTS
    // ========================================================================

    if alertFVG and array.size(fvgObjects) > 0

        FVGObject latestFVG = array.get(
             fvgObjects,
             array.size(fvgObjects) - 1
        )

        if latestFVG.state == FVGState.Fresh
             and latestFVG.originBar == bar_index

            string msg = "FVG " + str.tostring(latestFVG.direction)
                 + " | Top: " + str.tostring(latestFVG.top)
                 + " | Bottom: " + str.tostring(latestFVG.bottom)
                 + " | " + timeframe.period
                 + " | " + syminfo.tickerid

            alert(msg, alert.freq_once_per_bar)

    // ========================================================================
    // OB ALERTS
    // ========================================================================

    if alertOB and array.size(obObjects) > 0

        OBObject latestOB = array.get(
             obObjects,
             array.size(obObjects) - 1
        )

        if (latestOB.state == OBState.Fresh or latestOB.state == OBState.Breaker)
             and latestOB.originBar == bar_index

            string msg = "OB " + str.tostring(latestOB.state)
                 + " " + str.tostring(latestOB.direction)
                 + " | High: " + str.tostring(latestOB.high)
                 + " | Low: " + str.tostring(latestOB.low)
                 + " | " + timeframe.period
                 + " | " + syminfo.tickerid

            alert(msg, alert.freq_once_per_bar)

    // ========================================================================
    // MITIGATION ALERTS
    // ========================================================================

    if alertMitigation

        // Check FVG mitigation
        int fvgCount = array.size(fvgObjects)

        if fvgCount > 0

            for fi = 0 to math.min(fvgCount - 1, 5)

                FVGObject fvg = array.get(fvgObjects, fvgCount - 1 - fi)

                if fvg.state == FVGState.Mitigated
                     and not na(fvg.mitigationTime)
                     and fvg.mitigationTime == time_close

                    string msg = "FVG MITIGATED " + str.tostring(fvg.direction)
                         + " | CE: " + str.tostring(fvg.consequentEncroachment)
                         + " | " + timeframe.period
                         + " | " + syminfo.tickerid

                    alert(msg, alert.freq_once_per_bar)
                    break

        // Check OB mitigation
        int obCount = array.size(obObjects)

        if obCount > 0

            for oi = 0 to math.min(obCount - 1, 5)

                OBObject ob = array.get(obObjects, obCount - 1 - oi)

                if ob.state == OBState.Mitigated

                    string msg = "OB MITIGATED " + str.tostring(ob.direction)
                         + " | High: " + str.tostring(ob.high)
                         + " | Low: " + str.tostring(ob.low)
                         + " | " + timeframe.period
                         + " | " + syminfo.tickerid

                    alert(msg, alert.freq_once_per_bar)
                    break

    // ========================================================================
    // DOL REACHED ALERTS
    // ========================================================================

    if alertDOL and array.size(dolObjects) > 0

        int dolCount = array.size(dolObjects)

        for di = 0 to math.min(dolCount - 1, 5)

            DOLObject dol = array.get(dolObjects, dolCount - 1 - di)

            if dol.state == DOLState.Active

                float tolerance = alertAtr * 0.1

                if math.abs(close - dol.targetPrice) < tolerance

                    string msg = "DOL REACHED " + str.tostring(dol.priority)
                         + " | Target: " + str.tostring(dol.targetPrice)
                         + " | Score: " + str.tostring(math.round(dol.score))
                         + " | " + timeframe.period
                         + " | " + syminfo.tickerid

                    alert(msg, alert.freq_once_per_bar)

                    dol.state := DOLState.Reached
                    array.set(dolObjects, dolCount - 1 - di, dol)
                    break

    // ========================================================================
    // KILLZONE ENTRY/EXIT ALERTS
    // ========================================================================

    if alertKillzone

        bool currentInKillzone = showKillzones and (
             (showAsiaKillzone and f_isInKillzoneForAlert(SessionKind.Asia))
             or (showLondonKillzone and f_isInKillzoneForAlert(SessionKind.London))
             or (showNewYorkKillzone and f_isInKillzoneForAlert(SessionKind.NewYork))
             or (showLondonCloseKillzone and f_isInKillzoneForAlert(SessionKind.Custom))
             or (showMYKillzone and f_isInKillzoneForAlert(SessionKind.MY))
        )

        if currentInKillzone and not prevInKillzone

            string msg = "KILLZONE OPEN | " + timeframe.period + " | " + syminfo.tickerid
            alert(msg, alert.freq_once_per_bar)

        else if not currentInKillzone and prevInKillzone

            string msg = "KILLZONE CLOSE | " + timeframe.period + " | " + syminfo.tickerid
            alert(msg, alert.freq_once_per_bar)

        prevInKillzone := currentInKillzone

    // ========================================================================
    // MTF BIAS CHANGE ALERTS
    // ========================================================================

    if alertMTFBias and enableMTFEngine and array.size(mtfContexts) > 0

        MTFContextObject latestCtx = array.get(mtfContexts, 0)

        if latestCtx.externalBias != prevExternalBias
             and prevExternalBias != Direction.Neutral

            string msg = "MTF BIAS CHANGE " + str.tostring(latestCtx.originTf)
                 + " | " + str.tostring(prevExternalBias)
                 + " → " + str.tostring(latestCtx.externalBias)
                 + " | " + timeframe.period
                 + " | " + syminfo.tickerid

            alert(msg, alert.freq_once_per_bar)

        prevExternalBias := latestCtx.externalBias

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

string grpAssistant = "13 TRADING ASSISTANT"

bool enableAssistant = input.bool(
     false,
     "Enable Trading Assistant",
     group = grpAssistant
)

bool showSetupLabel = input.bool(
     true,
     "Show Setup Label",
     group = grpAssistant
)

float setupMinScore = input.float(
     50.0,
     "Minimum Setup Score",
     minval = 0.0,
     maxval = 100.0,
     step = 5.0,
     group = grpAssistant
)

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_scoreStructureAlignment() =>
    float score = 0.0

    int structCount = array.size(structureEvents)

    if structCount > 0

        StructureEventObject last = array.get(structureEvents, structCount - 1)

        if last.lifecycle == EventLifecycle.Confirmed

            score := 30.0

            if last.eventType == StructureEventType.MSS
                score := 30.0
            else if last.eventType == StructureEventType.CHOCH
                score := 20.0
            else if last.eventType == StructureEventType.BOS
                score := 15.0

    score

f_scoreLiquiditySweep() =>
    float score = 0.0

    int sweepCount = array.size(sweepEvents)

    if sweepCount > 0

        SweepEventObject last = array.get(sweepEvents, sweepCount - 1)

        if last.state == SweepLifecycle.Validated

            score := 20.0

            // Quality bonus
            if last.quality >= 70.0
                score := 20.0
            else if last.quality >= 50.0
                score := 15.0
            else
                score := 10.0

    score

f_scoreDisplacementQuality() =>
    float score = 0.0

    int dispCount = array.size(displacementObjects)

    if dispCount > 0

        DisplacementObject last = array.get(displacementObjects, dispCount - 1)

        if last.state == DisplacementState.Confirmed

            // Body% >= 65% and ATR Ratio >= 1.2 is the base contract
            if last.bodyPercent >= 65.0 and last.atrRatio >= 1.2

                score := 20.0

                // Quality bonus for stronger displacement
                if last.bodyPercent >= 80.0 and last.atrRatio >= 2.0
                    score := 20.0
                else if last.bodyPercent >= 70.0 and last.atrRatio >= 1.5
                    score := 15.0
                else
                    score := 10.0

    score

f_scoreFVGConfluence() =>
    float score = 0.0

    int fvgCount = array.size(fvgObjects)

    if fvgCount > 0

        FVGObject last = array.get(fvgObjects, fvgCount - 1)

        if last.state == FVGState.Fresh or last.state == FVGState.Partial

            score := 7.5

    int obCount = array.size(obObjects)

    if obCount > 0

        OBObject lastOB = array.get(obObjects, obCount - 1)

        if lastOB.state == OBState.Fresh or lastOB.state == OBState.Tested

            score := score + 7.5

    score

f_scoreMTFAlignment() =>
    float score = 0.0

    if enableMTFEngine and array.size(mtfContexts) > 0

        float alignment = f_calcMTFAlignment()

        if alignment >= 0.5
            score := 10.0
        else if alignment >= 0.25
            score := 7.5
        else if alignment <= -0.5
            score := 5.0
        else
            score := 2.5

    score

f_scorePDLocation() =>
    float score = 0.0

    int drCount = array.size(dealingRangeObjects)

    if drCount > 0

        DealingRangeObject dr = array.get(dealingRangeObjects, drCount - 1)

        // Discount + bullish bias = good entry zone
        // Premium + bearish bias = good entry zone
        if dr.currentLocation == PDLocation.Discount
            score := 5.0
        else if dr.currentLocation == PDLocation.Premium
            score := 3.0
        else
            score := 1.0

    score

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_calcConfluenceScore() =>
    float structScore = f_scoreStructureAlignment()
    float sweepScore  = f_scoreLiquiditySweep()
    float dispScore   = f_scoreDisplacementQuality()
    float fvgScore    = f_scoreFVGConfluence()
    float mtfScore    = f_scoreMTFAlignment()
    float pdScore     = f_scorePDLocation()

    float total = structScore + sweepScore + dispScore + fvgScore + mtfScore + pdScore

    total := math.min(total, 100.0)

    total

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_classifySetup(float score) =>
    string result = "NO SETUP"

    if score >= 80.0
        result := "SNIPER"
    else if score >= 65.0
        result := "A+"
    else if score >= setupMinScore
        result := "A"

    result

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

f_renderSetupLabel() =>

    var label setupLabel = na

    if showSetupLabel and barstate.islast

        if not na(setupLabel)
            label.delete(setupLabel)

        float score = f_calcConfluenceScore()
        string setupType = f_classifySetup(score)

        if setupType != "NO SETUP"

            color setupColor = setupType == "SNIPER"
                 ? color.new(color.purple, 40)
                 : setupType == "A+"
                      ? color.new(color.blue, 40)
                      : color.new(color.teal, 40)

            string labelText = setupType + " | Score: " + str.tostring(math.round(score))

            setupLabel := label.new(
                 x         = bar_index,
                 y         = low * 0.995,
                 text      = labelText,
                 color     = setupColor,
                 textcolor = color.white,
                 style     = label.style_label_up,
                 size      = size.small,
                 xloc      = xloc.bar_index
            )

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================

if enableAssistant

    f_renderSetupLabel()

// ============================================================================
// ============================================================================
